1://! Planning, as the PlanningScope says: a plain, quick list of the work
2://! still to do, as Linear keeps issues. Each story is written as it will be
3://! sent, a title, a prompt, and its mode, and the stories are gathered into
4://! the next sprint and sent to the agent in order, all at once.
5://!
6://! The stories are kept with the project's data, in `.suspense/planning/`: a
7://! file per story holding its hidden anchor as a queued prompt's is, with
8://! its mode, model, effort, Slice, attachments, and steps, and `order.json`
9://! listing the Next sprint, Backlog, and Done sections and their stories in
10://! order, with each story's title, its marks, and the task it was sent as,
11://! and the proposals still to be decided.
12://!
13://! The agent never writes the plan: drafting runs read the project and
14://! change nothing, and what they propose waits as a [`Proposal`] until it is
15://! accepted, as the PlanningScope's proposals say.
16://!
17://! [`Planning`] is the view, configured by [`PlanningHooks`]: what sending a
18://! story does, how a sent story's task stands, opening and resuming that
19://! task, and running the harness are the application's, so the view knows
20://! nothing of the queue it sends to.
21:

113:/// Planning's shortcuts: Ctrl+N (Cmd+N on macOS) adds a story and
114:/// Ctrl+Shift+Enter sends the sprint while Planning has focus; on a focused
115:/// row, the arrows move between rows, Alt with them moves the story, Enter
116:/// opens it, Ctrl+Enter sends it, M picks its mode, and Delete deletes it
117:/// once confirmed; on a focused proposal, A accepts it, E edits it, and R
118:/// rejects it.
119:pub fn bind_keys(cx: &mut App) {
120:    let row = Some(ROW_CONTEXT);
121:    let proposal = Some(PROPOSAL_CONTEXT);
122:    cx.bind_keys([
123:        KeyBinding::new("secondary-n", NewStory, Some(CONTEXT)),
124:        KeyBinding::new("secondary-shift-enter", SendSprint, Some(CONTEXT)),
125:        KeyBinding::new("secondary-shift-d", OpenDraftStories, Some(CONTEXT)),
126:        KeyBinding::new("secondary-shift-r", OpenResearch, Some(CONTEXT)),
127:        KeyBinding::new("f6", NextRegion, Some(CONTEXT)),
128:        KeyBinding::new("space", ToggleSwitch, Some("PlanningSwitch")),
129:        KeyBinding::new("enter", OpenCitation, Some("PlanningCitation")),
130:        KeyBinding::new("secondary-enter", ToggleSprint, row),
131:        KeyBinding::new("up", PreviousRow, row),
132:        KeyBinding::new("down", NextRow, row),
133:        KeyBinding::new("enter", ToggleStory, row),
134:        KeyBinding::new("alt-up", MoveStoryUp, row),
135:        KeyBinding::new("alt-down", MoveStoryDown, row),
136:        KeyBinding::new("m", PickMode, row),
137:        KeyBinding::new("delete", DeleteStory, row),
138:        #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
139:        KeyBinding::new("backspace", DeleteStory, row),
140:        KeyBinding::new("up", PreviousRow, proposal),
141:        KeyBinding::new("down", NextRow, proposal),
142:        KeyBinding::new("a", AcceptProposal, proposal),
143:        KeyBinding::new("e", EditProposal, proposal),
144:        KeyBinding::new("r", RejectProposal, proposal),
145:        KeyBinding::new("alt-up", MoveStoryUp, Some(STEP_CONTEXT)),
146:        KeyBinding::new("alt-down", MoveStoryDown, Some(STEP_CONTEXT)),
147:    ]);

174:/// A section of the plan.
175:#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
176:pub enum Section {
177:    /// The sprint sent, while it holds a story.
178:    Current,
179:    Next,
180:    Backlog,
181:    Done,
182:}
183:
184:impl Section {
185:    fn label(self) -> &'static str {
186:        match self {
187:            Section::Current => "Current sprint",
188:            Section::Next => "Next sprint",
189:            Section::Backlog => "Backlog",
190:            Section::Done => "Done",
191:        }
192:    }
193:
194:    /// What its empty state says: what is missing, and what to do.
195:    fn empty(self) -> String {
196:        match self {
197:            Section::Next => format!("Nothing planned. Press {MOD}N to add a story."),
198:            Section::Backlog => "Nothing waiting.".to_string(),
199:            Section::Done => "Nothing done yet.".into(),
200:            Section::Current => "No sprint under way.".into(),
201:        }
202:    }
203:
204:    fn key(self) -> &'static str {
205:        match self {
206:            Section::Current => "current",
207:            Section::Next => "next",
208:            Section::Backlog => "backlog",
209:            Section::Done => "done",
210:        }
211:    }
212:}

248:/// The mode last picked for a story, which a new one takes.
249:static LAST_MODE: Mutex<Option<SendMode>> = Mutex::new(None);
250:
251:fn last_mode() -> SendMode {
252:    LAST_MODE
253:        .lock()
254:        .ok()

259:/// One piece of work, written as it will be sent.
260:pub struct Story {
261:    /// What it is known by: its hidden anchor's name, and its file's.
262:    pub name: String,
263:    pub title: String,
264:    /// Its prompt, as written; empty to send its title.
265:    pub prompt: String,
266:    /// Its mode, model, effort, Slice, attachments, and steps.
267:    pub anchor: HiddenAnchor,
268:    /// Accepted from the agent and not yet opened.
269:    pub from_agent: bool,
270:    /// Given a step by the agent and not opened since.
271:    pub updated: bool,
272:    /// The step the agent added last, shown highlighted the first time the
273:    /// story is opened after.
274:    pub highlight: Option<usize>,
275:    /// The spec elements it touches, as the PlanningScope's spec grounding
276:    /// says.
277:    pub spec: Vec<Citation>,
278:    /// The task it was last sent as, by name.
279:    pub task: Option<String>,
280:}
281:
282:impl Story {
283:    /// A new story of `mode`, titled `title`, with the model and effort
284:    /// chosen now for the project's harness.
285:    pub fn new(title: String, prompt: String, mode: SendMode, project_dir: &Path) -> Self {
286:        let mut anchor = HiddenAnchor::random();
287:        anchor.rename(story_name());
288:        anchor.mode = Some(mode);
289:        let agent = crate::agent::of_project(Some(project_dir));
290:        anchor.model = crate::models::chosen(agent);
291:        anchor.effort = crate::effort::chosen(agent);
292:        Self {
293:            name: anchor.name().to_string(),
294:            title,
295:            prompt,
296:            anchor,
297:            from_agent: false,
298:            updated: false,
299:            highlight: None,
300:            spec: Vec::new(),
301:            task: None,
302:        }
303:    }
304:
305:    pub fn mode(&self) -> SendMode {
306:        self.anchor.mode.unwrap_or(SendMode::Both)
307:    }
308:
309:    /// What it sends: its prompt, or its title where it has none.
310:    pub fn text(&self) -> String {
311:        if self.prompt.trim().is_empty() {
312:            self.title.clone()
313:        } else {
314:            self.prompt.clone()
315:        }
316:    }
317:
318:    /// Its steps, after its own prompt.
319:    pub fn steps(&self) -> &[AddedStep] {
320:        &self.anchor.added_steps
321:    }
322:
323:    /// Whether anything is attached to it.
324:    fn has_attachments(&self) -> bool {
325:        !self.anchor.attached_text.is_empty()
326:            || !self.anchor.attached_images.is_empty()
327:            || !self.anchor.attached_files.is_empty()
328:    }
329:
330:    /// A copy of it under a new name, its steps with it, never sent.
331:    fn duplicate(&self) -> Self {
332:        let source = self.anchor.source(&self.prompt);
333:        let mut anchor = HiddenAnchor::parse(&source)
334:            .map(|(anchor, _)| anchor)
335:            .unwrap_or_else(HiddenAnchor::random);
336:        anchor.rename(story_name());
337:        Self {
338:            name: anchor.name().to_string(),
339:            title: self.title.clone(),
340:            prompt: self.prompt.clone(),
341:            anchor,
342:            from_agent: false,
343:            updated: false,
344:            highlight: None,
345:            spec: self.spec.clone(),
346:            task: None,
347:        }
348:    }
349:}

351:/// A fresh name for a story's anchor and file.
352:fn story_name() -> String {
353:    HiddenAnchor::random_name().replacen("Prompt_", "Story_", 1)
354:}

405:/// What kind of spec element a citation is, as the PlanningScope's spec
406:/// grounding says, shown as a small muted label before it.
407:#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
408:pub enum CitationKind {
409:    Requirement,
410:    Behaviour,
411:    Name,
412:    Section,
413:}
414:
415:impl CitationKind {
416:    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str {
417:        match self {
418:            Self::Requirement => "Requirement",
419:            Self::Behaviour => "Behaviour",
420:            Self::Name => "Name",
421:            Self::Section => "Section",
422:        }
423:    }
424:
425:    /// The kind a word names, as a `spec` entry begins with it.
426:    fn of_word(word: &str) -> Option<Self> {
427:        let word = word.trim().trim_end_matches(':').to_ascii_lowercase();
428:        match word.as_str() {
429:            "requirement" => Some(Self::Requirement),
430:            "behaviour" | "behavior" => Some(Self::Behaviour),
431:            "name" => Some(Self::Name),
432:            "section" => Some(Self::Section),
433:            _ => None,
434:        }
435:    }
436:}
437:
438:/// A spec element a story cites, as the PlanningScope's spec grounding
439:/// says: a link to the element in the spec's Piton source, its file and the
440:/// anchor or property it names, as `spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint`,
441:/// with its kind, what in it the story touches, and the few words of the
442:/// spec it rests on; or, with no link, "No match", with what was looked for.
443:#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
444:pub struct Citation {
445:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
446:    pub link: Option<String>,
447:    #[serde(default)]
448:    pub note: String,
449:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
450:    pub kind: Option<CitationKind>,
451:    /// The words of the spec it rests on, quoted.
452:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "String::is_empty")]
453:    pub quote: String,
454:}
455:
456:impl Citation {
457:    /// A citation of `link`, picked by hand, with nothing more said of it.
458:    pub fn of_link(link: String) -> Self {
459:        Self {
460:            link: Some(link),
461:            note: String::new(),
462:            kind: None,
463:            quote: String::new(),
464:        }
465:    }
466:
467:    /// The citation a `spec` list's entry holds: its kind first, where it
468:    /// names one; then `No match — what was looked for`, a markdown link, or
469:    /// a link and what it touches; and the words it rests on, in quotes, at
470:    /// its end.
471:    pub fn parse(entry: &str) -> Option<Self> {
472:        let entry = entry.trim().trim_start_matches(['-', '*']).trim();
473:        if entry.is_empty() {
474:            return None;
475:        }
476:        let rest = |text: &str| {
477:            text.trim()
478:                .trim_start_matches(['—', '–', '-', ':', ','])
479:                .trim()
480:                .to_string()
481:        };
482:        // Its kind, where it begins with one.
483:        let (kind, entry) = match entry.split_once(char::is_whitespace) {
484:            Some((word, after)) if CitationKind::of_word(word).is_some() => {
485:                (CitationKind::of_word(word), after.trim())
486:            }
487:            _ => (None, entry),
488:        };
489:        let (link, note) = if entry.to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("no match") {
490:            (None, rest(&entry["no match".len()..]))
491:        } else if let Some(open) = entry.strip_prefix('[')
492:            && let Some((name, after)) = open.split_once("](")
493:            && let Some((link, note)) = after.split_once(')')
494:        {
495:            let note = rest(note);
496:            (
497:                Some(link.trim().to_string()),
498:                if note.is_empty() {
499:                    name.to_string()
500:                } else {
501:                    note
502:                },
503:            )
504:        } else {
505:            let (link, note) = entry.split_once(char::is_whitespace).unwrap_or((entry, ""));
506:            (
507:                Some(link.trim_matches(['`', '<', '>']).to_string()),
508:                rest(note),
509:            )
510:        };
511:        let (note, quote) = split_quote(&note);
512:        Some(Self {
513:            link,
514:            note: rest(note.trim_end().trim_end_matches(['—', '–', '-', ','])),
515:            kind,
516:            quote,
517:        })
518:    }
519:
520:    /// The element it names, as `Planning.sprint`, or its file's name where
521:    /// it names none; "No match" for none.
522:    pub fn label(&self) -> String {
523:        let Some(link) = &self.link else {
524:            return "No match".into();
525:        };
526:        match link.split_once('#') {
527:            Some((_, element)) if !element.is_empty() => element.to_string(),
528:            _ => Path::new(link)
529:                .file_name()
530:                .map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
531:                .unwrap_or_else(|| link.clone()),
532:        }
533:    }
534:
535:    /// The file it links, relative to the project.
536:    pub fn file(&self) -> Option<&str> {
537:        let link = self.link.as_deref()?;
538:        Some(link.split('#').next().unwrap_or(link))
539:    }
540:}
541:
542:/// `note` with the words in quotes at its end taken out, and those words.
543:fn split_quote(note: &str) -> (String, String) {
544:    let trimmed = note.trim_end();
545:    for (open, close) in [('"', '"'), ('“', '”')] {
546:        if let Some(body) = trimmed.strip_suffix(close)
547:            && let Some(start) = body.rfind(open)
548:        {
549:            let quote = body[start + open.len_utf8()..].trim().to_string();
550:            if !quote.is_empty() {
551:                return (body[..start].to_string(), quote);
552:            }
553:        }
554:    }
555:    (note.to_string(), String::new())
556:}
557:
558:/// The spec location, relative to the project, as citations are written
559:/// under it: `spec` where `piton.config.pi` names none.
560:pub fn spec_location(project_dir: &Path) -> String {
561:    crate::hidden_anchor::spec_dir(project_dir)
562:        .and_then(|dir| {
563:            dir.strip_prefix(project_dir)
564:                .ok()
565:                .map(|dir| dir.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/"))
566:        })
567:        .filter(|dir| !dir.is_empty())
568:        .unwrap_or_else(|| "spec".into())
569:}
570:
571:/// The elements a Piton file declares, each with the line it is declared
572:/// on, from the top: its anchors, and the properties on them, as
573:/// `Planning` and `Planning.sprint`, as `piton slice` names them.
574:pub fn declared_elements(text: &str) -> Vec<(String, usize)> {
575:    // The element each open block belongs to, its indent, and the indent
576:    // its own properties are at, once seen.
577:    let mut open: Vec<(usize, String, Option<usize>)> = Vec::new();
578:    let mut found = Vec::new();
579:    for (at, line) in text.lines().enumerate() {
580:        let code = line.split("//").next().unwrap_or_default();
581:        if code.trim().is_empty() {
582:            continue;
583:        }
584:        let indent = code.len() - code.trim_start().len();
585:        if indent == 0 {
586:            open.clear();
587:            if let Some((_, name)) = crate::spec_components::declared_names(code)
588:                .into_iter()
589:                .next()
590:            {
591:                found.push((name.clone(), at));
592:                open.push((0, name, None));
593:            }
594:            continue;
595:        }
596:        while open
597:            .last()
598:            .is_some_and(|(at_indent, ..)| *at_indent >= indent)
599:        {
600:            open.pop();
601:        }
602:        let Some((_, parent, child)) = open.last_mut() else {
603:            continue;
604:        };
605:        // Only lines at its properties' own indent are properties of it;
606:        // deeper ones are their text.
607:        let child = *child.get_or_insert(indent);
608:        if child != indent {
609:            continue;
610:        }
611:        let word: String = code
612:            .trim_start()
613:            .chars()
614:            .take_while(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || *c == '_' || *c == '-')
615:            .collect();
616:        let after = code.trim_start()[word.len()..].trim_start();
617:        if word.is_empty() || !after.starts_with(':') {
618:            continue;
619:        }
620:        let name = format!("{parent}.{word}");
621:        found.push((name.clone(), at));
622:        open.push((indent, name, None));
623:    }
624:    found
625:}
626:
627:/// Whether `link`, a citation's, points at an element the spec's Piton
628:/// source defines, as the PlanningScope's spec grounding says: its file
629:/// under the spec location of the project at `project_dir`, and the anchor
630:/// or property it names declared in it. Gives the line it is declared on,
631:/// or why not.
632:pub fn check_link(link: &str, project_dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<usize>, String> {
633:    let (path, element) = link.split_once('#').unwrap_or((link, ""));
634:    let path = path.trim_start_matches("./");
635:    let under = Path::new(path).starts_with(spec_location(project_dir))
636:        && path.ends_with(".pi")
637:        && !Path::new(path)
638:            .components()
639:            .any(|part| matches!(part, std::path::Component::ParentDir));
640:    let text = under
641:        .then(|| std::fs::read_to_string(project_dir.join(path)).ok())
642:        .flatten()
643:        .ok_or_else(|| "no such file".to_string())?;
644:    if element.is_empty() {
645:        return Ok(None);
646:    }
647:    declared_elements(&text)
648:        .into_iter()
649:        .find(|(name, _)| name == element)
650:        .map(|(_, at)| Some(at))
651:        .ok_or_else(|| format!("the file doesn't define {element}"))
652:}
653:
654:/// Every element the spec's Piton source declares, as the link a citation
655:/// takes and the element's name, found from the files under the spec
656:/// location, for "Add citation" to search by name.
657:pub fn spec_elements(project_dir: &Path) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
658:    fn walk(dir: &Path, files: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) {
659:        let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir) else {
660:            return;
661:        };
662:        let mut entries: Vec<PathBuf> = entries.flatten().map(|entry| entry.path()).collect();
663:        entries.sort();
664:        for path in entries {
665:            if path.is_dir() {
666:                walk(&path, files);
667:            } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == "pi") {
668:                files.push(path);
669:            }
670:        }
671:    }
672:    let mut files = Vec::new();
673:    walk(&project_dir.join(spec_location(project_dir)), &mut files);
674:    let mut elements = Vec::new();
675:    for file in files {
676:        let (Ok(text), Ok(relative)) = (
677:            std::fs::read_to_string(&file),
678:            file.strip_prefix(project_dir),
679:        ) else {
680:            continue;
681:        };
682:        let relative = relative.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/");
683:        for (name, _) in declared_elements(&text) {
684:            elements.push((format!("{relative}#{name}"), name));
685:        }
686:    }
687:    elements
688:}
689:
690:/// Opens the Piton source where `link` points, at its element's
691:/// declaration where the file still defines it.
692:fn open_citation(
693:    link: &str,
694:    project_dir: &Path,
695:    open: &OpenSource,
696:    window: &mut Window,
697:    cx: &mut App,
698:) {
699:    let path = link.split('#').next().unwrap_or(link);
700:    let line = check_link(link, project_dir).ok().flatten();
701:    open(project_dir.join(path), line, window, cx)
702:}

704:/// Checks each link `proposal` cites against the Piton source of the
705:/// project at `project_dir`, as it arrives from drafting, research, or an Ask
706:/// answer alike: any that fails is dropped, with a note of which and why.
707:pub fn check_citations(proposal: &mut Proposal, project_dir: &Path) {
708:    let mut dropped = Vec::new();
709:    proposal.spec.retain(|citation| {
710:        let Some(link) = &citation.link else {
711:            return true;
712:        };
713:        match check_link(link, project_dir) {
714:            Ok(_) => true,
715:            Err(why) => {
716:                dropped.push(format!("Dropped {link}: {why}."));
717:                false
718:            }
719:        }
720:    });
721:    proposal.dropped.extend(dropped);
722:}

724:/// A task the agent proposes, as the PlanningScope's proposals say: its
725:/// title, prompt, and mode, and where it goes, a new story or a step of an
726:/// existing one, waiting to be accepted, edited, or rejected.
727:#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
728:pub struct Proposal {
729:    /// What it is known by while it waits.
730:    pub id: String,
731:    pub title: String,
732:    pub prompt: String,
733:    /// Its mode, as a `suspense-story` block names it.
734:    pub mode: String,
735:    /// The story it is to be added to, by name; none for a new story.
736:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
737:    pub target: Option<String>,
738:    /// Why it is a new story though another was named, shown muted.
739:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
740:    pub note: Option<String>,
741:    /// The story the drafting run was asked about, beneath which it shows.
742:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
743:    pub about: Option<String>,
744:    /// The spec elements it touches, as found.
745:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
746:    pub spec: Vec<Citation>,
747:    /// The citations dropped as it arrived, and why, shown muted.
748:    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
749:    pub dropped: Vec<String>,
750:}
751:
752:impl Proposal {
753:    pub fn mode(&self) -> SendMode {
754:        mode_of_slug(&self.mode).unwrap_or(SendMode::Both)
755:    }
756:
757:    /// What a new story made from it is titled: its title, or its prompt's
758:    /// first line.
759:    fn story_title(&self) -> String {
760:        if self.title.trim().is_empty() {
761:            self.prompt
762:                .lines()
763:                .next()
764:                .unwrap_or_default()
765:                .trim()
766:                .to_string()
767:        } else {
768:            self.title.trim().to_string()
769:        }
770:    }
771:}

773:/// A fresh id for a proposal.
774:fn proposal_id() -> String {
775:    HiddenAnchor::random_name().replacen("Prompt_", "Proposal_", 1)
776:}
777:
778:/// The proposal a `suspense-story` block holds: a line each for its title,
779:/// mode, and target, `new` or a story's id, then, after a blank line, its
780:/// prompt. None for a block with neither title nor prompt.
781:pub fn parse_story_block(body: &str) -> Option<Proposal> {
782:    let mut proposal = Proposal {
783:        id: proposal_id(),
784:        title: String::new(),
785:        prompt: String::new(),
786:        mode: "chain".into(),
787:        target: None,
788:        note: None,
789:        about: None,
790:        spec: Vec::new(),
791:        dropped: Vec::new(),
792:    };
793:    let mut lines = body.lines();
794:    let mut prompt: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
795:    let mut in_spec = false;
796:    for line in lines.by_ref() {
797:        if line.trim().is_empty() {
798:            break;
799:        }
800:        // The spec list: its entries, one per line, each begun with a dash.
801:        if in_spec && line.trim_start().starts_with(['-', '*']) {
802:            proposal.spec.extend(Citation::parse(line));
803:            continue;
804:        }
805:        in_spec = false;
806:        let Some((key, value)) = line.split_once(':') else {
807:            prompt.push(line);
808:            break;
809:        };
810:        let value = value.trim();
811:        match key.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
812:            "title" => proposal.title = value.to_string(),
813:            "mode" => {
814:                if let Some(mode) = mode_of_slug(value) {
815:                    proposal.mode = mode_slug(mode).into();
816:                }
817:            }
818:            "target" => {
819:                proposal.target = (!value.is_empty() && !value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("new"))
820:                    .then(|| value.to_string());
821:            }
822:            "spec" => {
823:                in_spec = true;
824:                proposal.spec.extend(Citation::parse(value));
825:            }
826:            "prompt" => {
827:                if !value.is_empty() {
828:                    prompt.push(value);
829:                }
830:                break;
831:            }
832:            _ => {
833:                prompt.push(line);
834:                break;
835:            }
836:        }
837:    }
838:    prompt.extend(lines);
839:    proposal.prompt = prompt.join("\n").trim().to_string();
840:    (!proposal.title.trim().is_empty() || !proposal.prompt.is_empty()).then_some(proposal)
841:}

848:        .filter_map(|part| match part {
849:            crate::answer_blocks::AnswerPart::Story { proposal, .. } => proposal,
850:            _ => None,
851:        })
852:        .collect()
853:}

924:
925:    /// Why Send sprint can't send now, if it can't.
926:    pub fn held(self) -> Option<&'static str> {
927:        if self.paused {
928:            Some("Planning is paused for review: resume or approve it first")
929:        } else if self.under_way {
930:            Some("A sprint is already under way")
931:        } else if self.waiting == 0 {
932:            Some("Nothing in Next sprint is waiting to be sent")
933:        } else {
934:            None
935:        }
936:    }

1065:/// The plan as saved, as [`load`] gives it, or, where its `order.json` is
1066:/// there but can't be read, which file and why.
1067:fn load_checked(project_dir: &Path) -> Result<Plan, String> {
1068:    let file = planning_dir(project_dir).join(ORDER_FILE);
1069:    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(&file)
1070:        && let Err(err) = serde_json::from_str::<Order>(&text)
1071:    {
1072:        return Err(format!(
1073:            "{} couldn't be read: {err}",
1074:            file.strip_prefix(project_dir).unwrap_or(&file).display()
1075:        ));
1076:    }
1077:    Ok(load_order(project_dir))
1078:}
1079:
1080:/// The plan as saved, an unreadable order read as empty.
1081:fn load_order(project_dir: &Path) -> ([Vec<Story>; 4], Vec<Proposal>, Notes) {
1082:    let dir = planning_dir(project_dir);
1083:    let order: Order = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(ORDER_FILE))
1084:        .ok()
1085:        .and_then(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).ok())
1086:        .unwrap_or_default();
1087:    let read = |entry: &Entry| -> Option<Story> {
1088:        let source = std::fs::read_to_string(story_file(project_dir, &entry.story)).ok()?;
1089:        let (anchor, prompt) = HiddenAnchor::parse(&source)?;
1090:        Some(Story {
1091:            name: entry.story.clone(),
1092:            title: entry.title.clone(),
1093:            prompt,
1094:            anchor,
1095:            from_agent: entry.from_agent,
1096:            updated: entry.updated,
1097:            highlight: entry.highlight,
1098:            spec: entry.spec.clone(),
1099:            task: entry.task.clone(),
1100:        })
1101:    };
1102:    let mut listed = std::collections::HashSet::new();
1103:    let mut sections = [order.next, order.backlog, order.done, order.current].map(|entries| {
1104:        entries
1105:            .iter()
1106:            .filter(|entry| listed.insert(entry.story.clone()))
1107:            .filter_map(read)
1108:            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
1109:    });
1110:    let mut unlisted: Vec<PathBuf> = std::fs::read_dir(&dir)
1111:        .map(|entries| {
1112:            entries
1113:                .flatten()
1114:                .map(|entry| entry.path())
1115:                .filter(|path| path.extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == "pi"))
1116:                .collect()
1117:        })
1118:        .unwrap_or_default();
1119:    unlisted.sort();
1120:    for path in unlisted {
1121:        let Some(name) = path.file_stem().and_then(|stem| stem.to_str()) else {
1122:            continue;
1123:        };
1124:        if listed.contains(name) {
1125:            continue;
1126:        }
1127:        let entry = Entry {
1128:            story: name.to_string(),
1129:            title: String::new(),
1130:            from_agent: false,
1131:            updated: false,
1132:            highlight: None,
1133:            spec: Vec::new(),
1134:            task: None,
1135:        };
1136:        if let Some(story) = read(&entry) {
1137:            sections[1].push(story);
1138:        }
1139:    }
1140:    let notes = Notes {
1141:        findings: order.findings,
1142:        auto_start: order.auto_start,
1143:        paused: order.paused,
1144:    };
1145:    (sections, order.proposals, notes)
1146:}

1148:/// Saves a story's file, named `name`, holding `source`, its hidden
1149:/// anchor's.
1150:fn save_story(project_dir: &Path, name: &str, source: &str) -> Result<()> {
1151:    let dir = planning_dir(project_dir);
1152:    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).with_context(|| format!("could not create {}", dir.display()))?;
1153:    let file = story_file(project_dir, name);
1154:    std::fs::write(&file, source).with_context(|| format!("could not save {}", file.display()))
1155:}

1157:/// The plan as `order.json` holds it.
1158:fn order_of(sections: &[Vec<Story>; 4], proposals: &[Proposal], notes: &Notes) -> String {
1159:    let entries = |stories: &Vec<Story>| {
1160:        stories
1161:            .iter()
1162:            .map(|story| Entry {
1163:                story: story.name.clone(),
1164:                title: story.title.clone(),
1165:                from_agent: story.from_agent,
1166:                updated: story.updated,
1167:                highlight: story.highlight,
1168:                spec: story.spec.clone(),
1169:                task: story.task.clone(),
1170:            })
1171:            .collect()
1172:    };
1173:    let order = Order {
1174:        next: entries(&sections[0]),
1175:        backlog: entries(&sections[1]),
1176:        done: entries(&sections[2]),
1177:        proposals: proposals.to_vec(),
1178:        current: entries(&sections[3]),
1179:        findings: notes.findings.clone(),
1180:        paused: notes.paused.clone(),
1181:        auto_start: notes.auto_start,
1182:    };
1183:    serde_json::to_string_pretty(&order).unwrap_or_default()
1184:}

1186:fn save_order_text(project_dir: &Path, text: &str) -> Result<()> {

1191:}

1195:/// The example of a `suspense-story` block the request shows, its links
1196:/// under `spec`, the spec location.
1197:fn story_example(spec: &str) -> String {
1198:    format!(
1199:        "```suspense-story\ntitle: Short title\nmode: chain\ntarget: new\nspec:\n- Behaviour {spec}/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint — how the sprint is sent, which it changes — \"sends every story in it, in order\"\n- No match — a sprint calendar, which the spec doesn't describe yet\n\nWhat should be true once it is done, naming and linking [Planning.sprint]({spec}/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint). The spec has nothing on a sprint calendar yet, so that part is new.\n```"
1200:    )
1201:}
1202:
1203:/// How a proposal is grounded in the spec, as planning.pi says: read from
1204:/// the compiled reference at `reference`, cited into the Piton source under
1205:/// `spec`.
1206:fn grounding(reference: &str, spec: &str) -> String {
1207:    use crate::baked_prompts::{fill, planning};
1208:    fill(
1209:        planning::GROUNDING,
1210:        &[("reference", reference), ("spec", spec)],
1211:    )
1212:}

1214:/// The request asking the harness to propose stories for `wanted`, shown
1215:/// the `stories` it may add to, by id and title, and the story it is asked
1216:/// `about`, its title, prompt, and steps, where there is one.
1217:pub fn draft_request(
1218:    wanted: &str,
1219:    stories: &[(String, String)],
1220:    about: Option<&Story>,
1221:    reference: &str,
1222:    spec: &str,
1223:) -> String {
1224:    // Written in system-prompts/planning.pi.
1225:    use crate::baked_prompts::planning;
1226:    let mut request = format!(
1227:        "{}\n\n{}\n\n",
1228:        planning::REQUEST,
1229:        grounding(reference, spec)
1230:    );
1231:    if !stories.is_empty() {
1232:        request.push_str(&format!("{}:\n", planning::STORIES));
1233:        for (id, title) in stories {
1234:            request.push_str(&format!("- {id}: {title}\n"));
1235:        }
1236:        request.push('\n');
1237:    }
1238:    if let Some(story) = about {
1239:        request.push_str(&format!(
1240:            "{}: {}\nTitle: {}\nPrompt:\n{}\n",
1241:            planning::ABOUT,
1242:            story.name,
1243:            story.title,
1244:            story.text()
1245:        ));
1246:        for (ix, step) in story.steps().iter().enumerate() {
1247:            request.push_str(&format!("Step {}: {}\n", ix + 2, step.prompt));
1248:        }
1249:        request.push('\n');
1250:    }
1251:    request.push_str(&format!(
1252:        "{}\n\n{}\n\n{}:\n{wanted}",
1253:        planning::REPLY,
1254:        story_example(spec),
1255:        planning::WANTED
1256:    ));
1257:    request
1258:}

1260:/// The request asking the harness to research `topic`, as `research.pi`
1261:/// says, shown the `stories` a proposal may add to, and the story it was
1262:/// asked `about`, where there is one.
1263:pub fn research_request(
1264:    topic: &str,
1265:    stories: &[(String, String)],
1266:    about: Option<&Story>,
1267:    reference: &str,
1268:    spec: &str,
1269:) -> String {
1270:    // Written in system-prompts/research.pi.
1271:    use crate::baked_prompts::research;
1272:    let mut request = format!("{}\n\n", research::REQUEST);
1273:    if !stories.is_empty() {
1274:        request.push_str(&format!("{}:\n", research::STORIES));
1275:        for (id, title) in stories {
1276:            request.push_str(&format!("- {id}: {title}\n"));
1277:        }
1278:        request.push('\n');
1279:    }
1280:    if let Some(story) = about {
1281:        request.push_str(&format!(
1282:            "{}: {}\nTitle: {}\nPrompt:\n{}\n\n",
1283:            research::ABOUT,
1284:            story.name,
1285:            story.title,
1286:            story.text()
1287:        ));
1288:    }
1289:    // Its proposals grounded as drafting's are.
1290:    request.push_str(&format!(
1291:        "{}\n\n{}\n\n{}\n\n{}:\n{topic}",
1292:        research::REPLY,
1293:        story_example(spec),
1294:        grounding(reference, spec),
1295:        research::TOPIC
1296:    ));
1297:    request
1298:}

1300:/// What a line the harness streams says it is doing, as the drafting
1301:/// panel's live line reads it, given the tool it last started.
1302:fn activity(line: &str, tool: &mut String) -> Option<String> {
1303:    let event: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).ok()?;
1304:    let mut said = None;
1305:    for event in crate::harness::parse(&event) {
1306:        match event {
1307:            crate::harness::HarnessEvent::ToolStarted { name, .. } => *tool = name,
1308:            crate::harness::HarnessEvent::ToolInput { summary, .. } => {
1309:                let doing = match tool.as_str() {
1310:                    "Read" => "Reading",
1311:                    "Grep" => "Searching for",
1312:                    "Glob" => "Looking for",
1313:                    other => other,
1314:                };
1315:                said = Some(format!("{doing} {summary}…"));
1316:            }
1317:            crate::harness::HarnessEvent::TextStarted => {
1318:                said = Some("Writing its proposals…".into());
1319:            }
1320:            _ => {}
1321:        }
1322:    }
1323:    said
1324:}

1328:/// The story open beneath its row, and its editors.
1329:struct OpenStory {
1330:    name: String,
1331:    title: Entity<InputState>,
1332:    prompt: Entity<EditorState>,
1333:    completion: Entity<CompletionMenu>,
1334:    fit: GrowToFit,
1335:    /// The step the agent added, highlighted this once.
1336:    highlight: Option<usize>,
1337:    /// Add citation: the spec's elements searched by name, to add one.
1338:    spec_input: Entity<InputState>,
1339:    /// The spec's elements, from its Piton source, as links and names.
1340:    spec_elements: Rc<Vec<(String, String)>>,
1341:    /// Why the citation given couldn't be added.
1342:    spec_error: Option<String>,
1343:    _subscriptions: Vec<Subscription>,
1344:}
1345:
1346:/// How many of the spec's elements Add citation lists at once.
1347:const CITATION_PICKS: usize = 8;
1348:
1349:/// The spec's elements whose names hold every word of `query`, ignoring
1350:/// case, those it begins first, as Add citation lists them.
1351:fn citation_picks<'a>(elements: &'a [(String, String)], query: &str) -> Vec<&'a (String, String)> {
1352:    let query = query.trim().to_lowercase();
1353:    if query.is_empty() {
1354:        return Vec::new();
1355:    }
1356:    let mut picks: Vec<&(String, String)> = elements
1357:        .iter()
1358:        .filter(|(_, name)| {
1359:            let name = name.to_lowercase();
1360:            query.split_whitespace().all(|word| name.contains(word))
1361:        })
1362:        .collect();
1363:    picks.sort_by_key(|(_, name)| !name.to_lowercase().starts_with(&query));
1364:    picks.truncate(CITATION_PICKS);
1365:    picks
1366:}

1464:pub struct Planning {
1465:    project_dir: PathBuf,
1466:    /// Next sprint, Backlog, and Done, in order.
1467:    sections: [Vec<Story>; 4],
1468:    /// The proposals waiting to be decided, in the order given.
1469:    proposals: Vec<Proposal>,
1470:    /// The research done, and whether planning is paused for review.
1471:    notes: Notes,
1472:    open: Option<OpenStory>,
1473:    done_expanded: bool,
1474:    drafting: Option<DraftPanel>,
1475:    editing: Option<ProposalEditor>,
1476:    /// The step of the open story being edited.
1477:    open_step: Option<StepEditor>,
1478:    /// A step that couldn't be added: to which story, why, and what it
1479:    /// held, kept so it can be added elsewhere.
1480:    step_error: Option<(String, String, String)>,
1481:    /// Each step row's focus, by its story and place.
1482:    step_focus: std::collections::HashMap<(String, usize), FocusHandle>,
1483:    /// While a step is dragged: the gap it would land in.
1484:    step_gap: Rc<Cell<Option<usize>>>,
1485:    /// Proposals whose prompts are shown whole.
1486:    read_more: std::collections::HashSet<String>,
1487:    /// The lists of steps, in Done and in findings, shown whole, by where
1488:    /// they are listed.
1489:    steps_expanded: std::collections::HashSet<String>,
1490:    rejected: Vec<Rejected>,
1491:    decided: Vec<Decided>,
1492:    /// Why a proposal couldn't be accepted, by its id.
1493:    refused: std::collections::HashMap<String, String>,
1494:    /// The mode picker open: the story's, and the mode highlighted.
1495:    mode_menu: Option<(String, usize)>,
1496:    /// The story asking to be deleted.
1497:    confirm_delete: Option<String>,
1498:    hooks: PlanningHooks,
1499:    focus: FocusHandle,
1500:    /// Each row's and proposal's focus, by its story's name or its id.
1501:    row_focus: std::collections::HashMap<String, FocusHandle>,
1502:    /// The Done section's toggle's focus.
1503:    done_focus: FocusHandle,
1504:    /// While a row is dragged: the gap it would land in.
1505:    gap: Rc<Cell<Option<(Section, usize)>>>,
1506:    /// The plan's files waiting to be written, each with what is latest
1507:    /// for it, none to remove it.
1508:    writes: Arc<Mutex<std::collections::HashMap<PathBuf, Option<String>>>>,
1509:    /// Ticks once a second while something runs, for its time.
1510:    _tick: Option<Task<()>>,
1511:    /// The sprint is being started by itself now.
1512:    advancing: bool,
1513:    /// Next sprint, found waiting as the plan was read with Start
1514:    /// automatically on, held until Start now: a sprint never begins unseen
1515:    /// as the project opens.
1516:    waiting_start: bool,
1517:    /// How many empty stories the last Send sprint left in Next sprint, as
1518:    /// it says beside its heading while they are still there.
1519:    left_empty: usize,
1520:    /// The plan has been read from the project.
1521:    loaded: bool,
1522:    /// Why the plan couldn't be read, naming the file.
1523:    load_error: Option<String>,
1524:    /// A change made before the plan was read, to save once it is.
1525:    save_waiting: Cell<bool>,
1526:    /// Why a file of the plan couldn't be saved, naming it.
1527:    save_error: Arc<Mutex<Option<String>>>,
1528:    /// The pause bar's focus, a stop of its own for F6.
1529:    pause_focus: FocusHandle,
1530:    /// An open panel's focus, a stop of its own for F6.
1531:    panel_focus: FocusHandle,
1532:    /// Each section's focus, by its key, a stop for F6.
1533:    section_focus: std::collections::HashMap<&'static str, FocusHandle>,
1534:    /// The Start automatically switch's focus, reached by Tab.
1535:    switch_focus: FocusHandle,
1536:    /// The region F6 last moved to.
1537:    region: usize,
1538:}

1540:impl EventEmitter<()> for Planning {}

1542:impl Focusable for Planning {
1543:    fn focus_handle(&self, _: &App) -> FocusHandle {
1544:        self.focus.clone()
1545:    }
1546:}
1547:
1548:impl Planning {
1549:    pub fn new(project_dir: PathBuf, hooks: PlanningHooks, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> Self {
1550:        // Read in the background; skeleton rows meanwhile.
1551:        let dir = project_dir.clone();
1552:        cx.spawn(async move |this, cx| {
1553:            let read = cx.background_spawn(async move { load_checked(&dir) }).await;
1554:            this.update(cx, |this, cx| this.read(read, cx)).ok();
1555:        })
1556:        .detach();
1557:        let section_focus = ["current", "next", "backlog", "findings", "done"]
1558:            .into_iter()
1559:            .map(|key| (key, cx.focus_handle()))
1560:            .collect();
1561:        let this = Self {
1562:            project_dir,
1563:            sections: Default::default(),
1564:            proposals: Vec::new(),
1565:            notes: Notes::default(),
1566:            open: None,
1567:            done_expanded: false,
1568:            drafting: None,
1569:            editing: None,
1570:            open_step: None,
1571:            step_error: None,
1572:            step_focus: Default::default(),
1573:            step_gap: Rc::default(),
1574:            read_more: Default::default(),
1575:            steps_expanded: Default::default(),
1576:            rejected: Vec::new(),
1577:            decided: Vec::new(),
1578:            refused: Default::default(),
1579:            mode_menu: None,
1580:            confirm_delete: None,
1581:            hooks,
1582:            focus: cx.focus_handle(),
1583:            row_focus: Default::default(),
1584:            done_focus: cx.focus_handle().tab_stop(true),
1585:            gap: Rc::default(),
1586:            writes: Arc::default(),
1587:            _tick: None,
1588:            advancing: false,
1589:            waiting_start: false,
1590:            left_empty: 0,
1591:            loaded: false,
1592:            load_error: None,
1593:            save_waiting: Cell::new(false),
1594:            save_error: Arc::default(),
1595:            pause_focus: cx.focus_handle().tab_stop(true),
1596:            panel_focus: cx.focus_handle(),
1597:            section_focus,
1598:            switch_focus: cx.focus_handle().tab_stop(true),
1599:            region: 0,
1600:        };
1601:        this.publish(cx);
1602:        this
1603:    }
1604:
1605:    /// The plan as read from the project: what was added meanwhile is kept
1606:    /// after what was saved, and saved once it is in. Where it couldn't be
1607:    /// read, the list says so, naming the file, and nothing is saved over
1608:    /// it.
1609:    fn read(&mut self, read: Result<Plan, String>, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1610:        match read {
1611:            Ok((mut sections, mut proposals, notes)) => {
1612:                for (saved, added) in sections.iter_mut().zip(self.sections.iter_mut()) {
1613:                    saved.append(added);
1614:                }
1615:                proposals.append(&mut self.proposals);
1616:                let mut notes = notes;
1617:                notes.findings.append(&mut self.notes.findings);
1618:                notes.paused = notes.paused.or(self.notes.paused.take());
1619:                notes.auto_start |= self.notes.auto_start;
1620:                self.sections = sections;
1621:                self.proposals = proposals;
1622:                self.notes = notes;
1623:                self.loaded = true;
1624:                self.load_error = None;
1625:                self.waiting_start = self.notes.auto_start
1626:                    && self.sections[section_ix(Section::Current)].is_empty()
1627:                    && self.has_sendable();
1628:                if self.save_waiting.take() {
1629:                    self.save_order(cx);
1630:                }
1631:                self.publish(cx);
1632:            }
1633:            Err(why) => self.load_error = Some(why),
1634:        }
1635:        cx.notify();
1636:    }
1637:
1638:    /// F6: focus moves to the next of the column's regions, the pause bar,
1639:    /// each section, and an open panel, each a single stop: a section's
1640:    /// first row, or the section itself while it holds none.
1641:    fn next_region(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1642:        let mut regions: Vec<FocusHandle> = Vec::new();
1643:        if self.notes.paused.is_some() {
1644:            regions.push(self.pause_focus.clone());
1645:        }
1646:        let shown = [
1647:            (Section::Current, !self.sections[3].is_empty()),
1648:            (Section::Next, true),
1649:            (Section::Backlog, true),
1650:        ];
1651:        for (section, visible) in shown {
1652:            if !visible {
1653:                continue;
1654:            }
1655:            let first = self.sections[section_ix(section)]
1656:                .first()
1657:                .map(|story| story.name.clone());
1658:            match first {
1659:                Some(name) => regions.push(self.row_focus(&name, cx)),
1660:                None => regions.push(self.section_focus[section.key()].clone()),
1661:            }
1662:        }
1663:        let first_finding = self
1664:            .notes
1665:            .findings
1666:            .last()
1667:            .map(|finding| finding.file.clone());
1668:        regions.push(match first_finding {
1669:            Some(file) => self.row_focus(&format!("finding:{file}"), cx),
1670:            None => self.section_focus["findings"].clone(),
1671:        });
1672:        if !self.sections[2].is_empty() {
1673:            regions.push(self.done_focus.clone());
1674:        }
1675:        if let Some(open) = &self.open {
1676:            regions.push(open.title.focus_handle(cx));
1677:        } else if let Some(drafting) = &self.drafting {
1678:            regions.push(drafting.input.focus_handle(cx));
1679:        }
1680:        // On from the region holding the focus, or the one last moved to.
1681:        let at = regions
1682:            .iter()
1683:            .position(|region| region.contains_focused(window, cx))
1684:            .unwrap_or(self.region);
1685:        self.region = (at + 1) % regions.len();
1686:        regions[self.region].focus(window, cx);
1687:        cx.notify();
1688:    }
1689:
1690:    /// Reads the plan again, as its error's Try again does.
1691:    fn read_again(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1692:        self.load_error = None;
1693:        let dir = self.project_dir.clone();
1694:        cx.spawn(async move |this, cx| {
1695:            let read = cx.background_spawn(async move { load_checked(&dir) }).await;
1696:            this.update(cx, |this, cx| this.read(read, cx)).ok();
1697:        })
1698:        .detach();
1699:        cx.notify();
1700:    }
1701:
1702:    /// The stories of `section`.
1703:    #[cfg(test)]
1704:    pub fn stories(&self, section: Section) -> &[Story] {
1705:        &self.sections[section_ix(section)]
1706:    }
1707:
1708:    /// Where the sprint stands, for the ribbon.
1709:    pub fn pending(&self) -> SprintPending {
1710:        SprintPending {
1711:            waiting: self.unsent(),
1712:            under_way: !self.sections[3].is_empty(),
1713:            paused: self.notes.paused.is_some(),
1714:            auto_start: self.notes.auto_start,
1715:        }
1716:    }
1717:
1718:    /// Whether planning is paused for review.
1719:    pub fn is_paused(&self) -> bool {
1720:        self.notes.paused.is_some()
1721:    }
1722:
1723:    /// While planning is paused for review, the stories of Current sprint,
1724:    /// by the names they were sent as: what is held until it ends.
1725:    pub fn held_for_review(&self) -> Option<std::collections::HashSet<String>> {
1726:        self.notes.paused.as_ref()?;
1727:        Some(
1728:            self.sections[section_ix(Section::Current)]
1729:                .iter()
1730:                .filter_map(|story| story.task.clone())
1731:                .collect(),
1732:        )
1733:    }
1734:
1735:    /// The names every story sent was sent as.
1736:    pub fn story_tasks(&self) -> std::collections::HashSet<String> {
1737:        self.sections
1738:            .iter()
1739:            .flatten()
1740:            .filter_map(|story| story.task.clone())
1741:            .collect()
1742:    }
1743:
1744:    /// How many of Current sprint's stories are running, or running a step.
1745:    fn running(&self, cx: &App) -> usize {
1746:        self.sections[section_ix(Section::Current)]
1747:            .iter()
1748:            .filter(|story| {
1749:                let Some(task) = story.task.as_deref() else {
1750:                    return false;
1751:                };
1752:                self.state(story, cx) == Some(StoryState::Running)
1753:                    || (0..story.steps().len()).any(|at| {
1754:                        (self.hooks.step_state)(task, at, cx)
1755:                            .is_some_and(|(state, _)| state == StoryState::Running)
1756:                    })
1757:            })
1758:            .count()
1759:    }
1760:
1761:    /// A story's status as its row shows it: "Held for review", muted, for
1762:    /// one of Current sprint still waiting while planning is paused.
1763:    fn status_shown(
1764:        &self,
1765:        status: Option<(StoryState, Option<Duration>)>,
1766:        current: bool,
1767:        cx: &App,
1768:    ) -> Option<AnyElement> {
1769:        let (state, elapsed) = status?;
1770:        if current && state == StoryState::Queued && self.is_paused() {
1771:            return Some(
1772:                div()
1773:                    .text_xs()
1774:                    .text_color(cx.theme().muted_foreground)
1775:                    .child("Held for review")
1776:                    .into_any_element(),
1777:            );
1778:        }
1779:        Some(status_label(state, elapsed, cx))
1780:    }
1781:
1782:    /// Says how many stories wait in Next sprint to be sent, for the ribbon.
1783:    fn publish(&self, cx: &mut App) {
1784:        cx.set_global(self.pending());
1785:    }
1786:
1787:    fn unsent(&self) -> usize {
1788:        self.sections[0]
1789:            .iter()
1790:            .filter(|story| story.task.is_none())
1791:            .count()
1792:    }
1793:
1794:    fn find(&self, name: &str) -> Option<(Section, usize)> {
1795:        SECTIONS.into_iter().find_map(|section| {
1796:            self.sections[section_ix(section)]
1797:                .iter()
1798:                .position(|story| story.name == name)
1799:                .map(|ix| (section, ix))
1800:        })
1801:    }
1802:
1803:    fn story(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Story> {
1804:        let (section, ix) = self.find(name)?;
1805:        self.sections[section_ix(section)].get(ix)
1806:    }
1807:
1808:    fn story_mut(&mut self, name: &str) -> Option<&mut Story> {
1809:        let (section, ix) = self.find(name)?;
1810:        self.sections[section_ix(section)].get_mut(ix)
1811:    }
1812:
1813:    /// The title of the story named so, while it exists.
1814:    pub fn title_of(&self, name: &str) -> Option<String> {
1815:        self.story(name).map(|story| story.title.clone())
1816:    }
1817:
1818:    fn proposal(&self, id: &str) -> Option<&Proposal> {
1819:        self.proposals.iter().find(|proposal| proposal.id == id)
1820:    }
1821:
1822:    fn proposal_mut(&mut self, id: &str) -> Option<&mut Proposal> {
1823:        self.proposals.iter_mut().find(|proposal| proposal.id == id)
1824:    }
1825:
1826:    /// Saves the order and the proposals in the background.
1827:    fn save_order(&self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1828:        if !self.loaded {
1829:            // Never over a plan not read yet.
1830:            self.save_waiting.set(true);
1831:            self.publish(cx);
1832:            return;
1833:        }
1834:        let text = order_of(&self.sections, &self.proposals, &self.notes);
1835:        let file = planning_dir(&self.project_dir).join(ORDER_FILE);
1836:        self.write_later(file, Some(text), cx);
1837:        self.publish(cx);
1838:        if self.notes.auto_start && !self.advancing {
1839:            let this = cx.entity().downgrade();
1840:            cx.defer(move |cx| {
1841:                this.update(cx, |this, cx| this.auto_advance(cx)).ok();
1842:            });
1843:        }
1844:    }
1845:
1846:    /// Saves the story named so in the background.
1847:    fn save_story_named(&self, name: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1848:        let Some(story) = self.story(name) else {
1849:            return;
1850:        };
1851:        let source = story.anchor.source(&story.prompt);
1852:        self.write_later(story_file(&self.project_dir, name), Some(source), cx);
1853:    }
1854:
1855:    /// Writes `text` to `file` in the background, or with none removes it.
1856:    /// Whichever write runs takes what is latest for its file, so writes
1857:    /// made one after another never land out of order, however the
1858:    /// background runs them.
1859:    fn write_later(&self, file: PathBuf, text: Option<String>, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1860:        let writes = self.writes.clone();
1861:        writes.lock().unwrap().insert(file.clone(), text);
1862:        let failed = self.save_error.clone();
1863:        let this = cx.entity().downgrade();
1864:        let write = cx.background_spawn(async move {
1865:            let mut pending = writes.lock().unwrap();
1866:            let Some(text) = pending.remove(&file) else {
1867:                // A later write took it, with what is newer.
1868:                return false;
1869:            };
1870:            let written = match text {
1871:                Some(text) => file
1872:                    .parent()
1873:                    .map_or(Ok(()), std::fs::create_dir_all)
1874:                    .and_then(|_| std::fs::write(&file, text)),
1875:                None => match std::fs::remove_file(&file) {
1876:                    Err(err) if err.kind() != std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Err(err),
1877:                    _ => Ok(()),
1878:                },
1879:            };
1880:            drop(pending);
1881:            match written {
1882:                Err(err) => {
1883:                    let name = file.file_name().map_or_else(
1884:                        || file.display().to_string(),
1885:                        |name| name.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
1886:                    );
1887:                    *failed.lock().unwrap() = Some(format!("{name} couldn't be saved: {err}"));
1888:                    true
1889:                }
1890:                Ok(()) => false,
1891:            }
1892:        });
1893:        cx.spawn(async move |_, cx| {
1894:            if write.await {
1895:                this.update(cx, |_, cx| cx.notify()).ok();
1896:            }
1897:        })
1898:        .detach();
1899:    }
1900:
1901:    /// Adds a story at the end of Next sprint, open, its title focused, in
1902:    /// the mode last picked: however a story is added, it goes there, as
1903:    /// the PlanningScope's creating says.
1904:    pub fn new_story(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1905:        self.new_story_at(None, window, cx);
1906:    }
1907:
1908:    /// Adds a story to Next sprint at place `at`, or its end.
1909:    fn new_story_at(&mut self, at: Option<usize>, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1910:        let section = Section::Next;
1911:        self.close_story(window, cx);
1912:        let story = Story::new(String::new(), String::new(), last_mode(), &self.project_dir);
1913:        let name = story.name.clone();
1914:        let stories = &mut self.sections[section_ix(section)];
1915:        let at = at.unwrap_or(stories.len()).min(stories.len());
1916:        stories.insert(at, story);
1917:        self.save_story_named(&name, cx);
1918:        self.save_order(cx);
1919:        self.open_story(&name, window, cx);
1920:    }
1921:
1922:    /// Adds a story of `mode` holding `prompt` at the end of `section`.
1923:    pub fn add_story(
1924:        &mut self,
1925:        section: Section,
1926:        title: String,
1927:        prompt: String,
1928:        mode: SendMode,
1929:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
1930:    ) {
1931:        let story = Story::new(title, prompt, mode, &self.project_dir);
1932:        let name = story.name.clone();
1933:        self.sections[section_ix(section)].push(story);
1934:        self.save_story_named(&name, cx);
1935:        self.save_order(cx);
1936:        cx.notify();
1937:    }
1938:
1939:    /// Opens the story named so beneath its row, closing any other. Opened,
1940:    /// it is no longer marked as the agent's.
1941:    fn open_story(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
1942:        if self.open.as_ref().is_some_and(|open| open.name == name) {
1943:            return;
1944:        }
1945:        // A story of the current sprint can't be edited there.
1946:        if self
1947:            .find(name)
1948:            .is_some_and(|(section, _)| section == Section::Current)
1949:        {
1950:            return;
1951:        }
1952:        self.close_story(window, cx);
1953:        let Some(story) = self.story_mut(name) else {
1954:            return;
1955:        };
1956:        let marked = story.from_agent || story.updated || story.highlight.is_some();
1957:        story.from_agent = false;
1958:        story.updated = false;
1959:        let highlight = story.highlight.take();
1960:        let (title_text, prompt_text) = (story.title.clone(), story.prompt.clone());
1961:        let title = cx.new(|cx| {
1962:            InputState::new(window, cx)
1963:                .placeholder("Story title")
1964:                .default_value(title_text)
1965:        });
1966:        let prompt = cx.new(|cx| {
1967:            EditorState::new(window, cx)
1968:                .language(piton_syntax::LANGUAGE_NAME)
1969:                .line_number(false)
1970:                .folding(false)
1971:                .soft_wrap(true)
1972:                .scroll_beyond_last_line(Some(0))
1973:                .cursor_surrounding_lines(Some(0))
1974:                .placeholder("Prompt… (the title is sent where there's none)")
1975:                .default_value(prompt_text)
1976:        });
1977:        let completion = cx.new(|cx| CompletionMenu::new(prompt.clone(), window, cx));
1978:        let provider = (self.hooks.completions)(cx);
1979:        completion.update(cx, |menu, cx| menu.set_provider(provider, cx));
1980:        let story_name = name.to_string();
1981:        let subscriptions = vec![
1982:            cx.subscribe_in(&title, window, {
1983:                let name = story_name.clone();
1984:                move |this, input, event: &InputEvent, window, cx| match event {
1985:                    InputEvent::Change => {
1986:                        let value = input.read(cx).value().to_string();
1987:                        if let Some(story) = this.story_mut(&name) {
1988:                            story.title = value;
1989:                        }
1990:                        this.save_order(cx);
1991:                        cx.notify();
1992:                    }
1993:                    InputEvent::PressEnter { .. } => this.title_entered(&name, window, cx),
1994:                    _ => {}
1995:                }
1996:            }),
1997:            cx.subscribe_in(&prompt, window, {
1998:                let name = story_name.clone();
1999:                move |this, editor, event: &InputEvent, window, cx| {
2000:                    if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
2001:                        let value = editor.read(cx).value().to_string();
2002:                        if let Some(story) = this.story_mut(&name) {
2003:                            story.prompt = value;
2004:                        }
2005:                        this.save_story_named(&name, cx);
2006:                        if let Some(open) = &this.open {
2007:                            open.completion
2008:                                .update(cx, |menu, cx| menu.on_edit(window, cx));
2009:                        }
2010:                    }
2011:                    cx.notify();
2012:                }
2013:            }),
2014:        ];
2015:        let spec_input = cx.new(|cx| {
2016:            InputState::new(window, cx)
2017:                .placeholder("Add citation: search the spec's elements by name, or No match…")
2018:        });
2019:        let spec_elements = Rc::new(spec_elements(&self.project_dir));
2020:        let mut subscriptions = subscriptions;
2021:        subscriptions.push(cx.subscribe_in(&spec_input, window, {
2022:            let name = story_name.clone();
2023:            let elements = spec_elements.clone();
2024:            move |this, input, event: &InputEvent, window, cx| match event {
2025:                // The first element found, or what was typed, as No match.
2026:                InputEvent::PressEnter { .. } => {
2027:                    let text = input.read(cx).value().to_string();
2028:                    let added = match citation_picks(&elements, &text).first() {
2029:                        Some((link, _)) => this.add_citation(&name, link, cx),
2030:                        None => this.add_citation(&name, &text, cx),
2031:                    };
2032:                    if added {
2033:                        input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("", window, cx));
2034:                    }
2035:                }
2036:                InputEvent::Change => cx.notify(),
2037:                _ => {}
2038:            }
2039:        }));
2040:        title.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
2041:        self.open = Some(OpenStory {
2042:            name: story_name,
2043:            title,
2044:            prompt,
2045:            completion,
2046:            fit: GrowToFit::new(PROMPT_MAX_ROWS),
2047:            highlight,
2048:            spec_input,
2049:            spec_elements,
2050:            spec_error: None,
2051:            _subscriptions: subscriptions,
2052:        });
2053:        if marked {
2054:            self.save_order(cx);
2055:        }
2056:        cx.notify();
2057:    }
2058:
2059:    /// Closes the open story, its row focused again; one left with neither
2060:    /// title nor prompt is removed.
2061:    fn close_story(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2062:        self.close_step(window, cx);
2063:        self.step_error = None;
2064:        let Some(open) = self.open.take() else {
2065:            return;
2066:        };
2067:        let empty = self.story(&open.name).is_some_and(|story| {
2068:            story.title.trim().is_empty()
2069:                && story.prompt.trim().is_empty()
2070:                && story.steps().is_empty()
2071:        });
2072:        if empty {
2073:            self.delete(&open.name, cx);
2074:            self.focus.focus(window, cx);
2075:        } else {
2076:            self.focus_row(&open.name, window, cx);
2077:        }
2078:        cx.notify();
2079:    }
2080:
2081:    fn toggle_story(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2082:        if self.open.as_ref().is_some_and(|open| open.name == name) {
2083:            self.close_story(window, cx);
2084:        } else {
2085:            self.open_story(name, window, cx);
2086:        }
2087:    }
2088:
2089:    /// Enter in an open story's title: it is kept, and another starts
2090:    /// beneath it, so a list can be written down without the mouse.
2091:    fn title_entered(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2092:        let Some((section, ix)) = self.find(name) else {
2093:            return;
2094:        };
2095:        if self.sections[section_ix(section)][ix]
2096:            .title
2097:            .trim()
2098:            .is_empty()
2099:        {
2100:            return;
2101:        }
2102:        // Beneath it, in Next sprint; from elsewhere, at Next sprint's end.
2103:        let at = (section == Section::Next).then_some(ix + 1);
2104:        self.new_story_at(at, window, cx);
2105:    }
2106:
2107:    /// Escape: closes what is open, one layer at a time: the mode picker or
2108:    /// a confirmation, the completions, a proposal being edited, the open
2109:    /// story, then the drafting panel. Whether there was anything to close.
2110:    fn escape(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> bool {
2111:        if self.mode_menu.take().is_some() || self.confirm_delete.take().is_some() {
2112:            cx.notify();
2113:            return true;
2114:        }
2115:        if let Some(open) = &self.open
2116:            && open.completion.read(cx).is_open()
2117:        {
2118:            open.completion.update(cx, |menu, cx| menu.hide(cx));
2119:            return true;
2120:        }
2121:        if self.open_step.is_some() {
2122:            self.close_step(window, cx);
2123:            return true;
2124:        }
2125:        if let Some(editor) = self.editing.take() {
2126:            self.focus_row(&editor.id, window, cx);
2127:            return true;
2128:        }
2129:        if self.open.is_some() {
2130:            self.close_story(window, cx);
2131:            return true;
2132:        }
2133:        if self
2134:            .drafting
2135:            .as_ref()
2136:            .is_some_and(|draft| draft.running.is_none())
2137:            && self.proposals.is_empty()
2138:        {
2139:            self.drafting = None;
2140:            self.focus.focus(window, cx);
2141:            cx.notify();
2142:            return true;
2143:        }
2144:        false
2145:    }
2146:
2147:    /// Ctrl/Cmd+N: a story at the end of Next sprint.
2148:    fn new_story_here(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2149:        self.new_story(window, cx);
2150:    }
2151:
2152:    /// Ctrl/Cmd+Enter: the story named so moves to Next sprint, or from it
2153:    /// to the Backlog; never sent, as no story is on its own.
2154:    fn toggle_sprint(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2155:        match self.find(name).map(|(section, _)| section) {
2156:            Some(Section::Next) => self.move_story(name, Section::Backlog, 0, cx),
2157:            Some(Section::Backlog | Section::Done) => self.move_to(name, Section::Next, cx),
2158:            _ => return,
2159:        }
2160:        self.focus_row(name, window, cx);
2161:    }
2162:
2163:    fn delete(&mut self, name: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2164:        let Some((section, ix)) = self.find(name) else {
2165:            return;
2166:        };
2167:        self.sections[section_ix(section)].remove(ix);
2168:        self.row_focus.remove(name);
2169:        if self.open.as_ref().is_some_and(|open| open.name == name) {
2170:            self.open = None;
2171:        }
2172:        if self.confirm_delete.as_deref() == Some(name) {
2173:            self.confirm_delete = None;
2174:        }
2175:        self.write_later(story_file(&self.project_dir, name), None, cx);
2176:        self.save_order(cx);
2177:        cx.notify();
2178:    }
2179:
2180:    /// Asks, in its row, to delete the story named so.
2181:    fn ask_delete(&mut self, name: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2182:        self.mode_menu = None;
2183:        self.confirm_delete = Some(name.to_string());
2184:        cx.notify();
2185:    }
2186:
2187:    /// Deletes the story asking to be, the focus going to the row beside.
2188:    fn confirm_delete(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2189:        let Some(name) = self.confirm_delete.take() else {
2190:            return;
2191:        };
2192:        let next = self
2193:            .row_after(&name, 1)
2194:            .or_else(|| self.row_after(&name, -1));
2195:        self.delete(&name, cx);
2196:        match next {
2197:            Some(next) => self.focus_row(&next, window, cx),
2198:            None => self.focus.focus(window, cx),
2199:        }
2200:    }
2201:
2202:    fn duplicate(&mut self, name: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2203:        let Some((section, ix)) = self.find(name) else {
2204:            return;
2205:        };
2206:        // A duplicate is added as any story is: at the end of Next sprint.
2207:        let copy = self.sections[section_ix(section)][ix].duplicate();
2208:        let copy_name = copy.name.clone();
2209:        self.sections[0].push(copy);
2210:        self.save_story_named(&copy_name, cx);
2211:        self.save_order(cx);
2212:        cx.notify();
2213:    }
2214:
2215:    /// Moves the story named so to place `to` of `section`, counted as the
2216:    /// section is before it is taken out.
2217:    fn move_story(&mut self, name: &str, section: Section, to: usize, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2218:        let Some((from_section, from)) = self.find(name) else {
2219:            return;
2220:        };
2221:        // Only Send sprint moves a story into the current sprint, and only
2222:        // Close sprint out of it.
2223:        if from_section == Section::Current || section == Section::Current {
2224:            return;
2225:        }
2226:        let to = if from_section == section && to > from {
2227:            to - 1
2228:        } else {
2229:            to
2230:        };
2231:        let story = self.sections[section_ix(from_section)].remove(from);
2232:        let stories = &mut self.sections[section_ix(section)];
2233:        stories.insert(to.min(stories.len()), story);
2234:        self.save_order(cx);
2235:        cx.notify();
2236:    }
2237:
2238:    pub(crate) fn move_to(&mut self, name: &str, section: Section, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2239:        let end = self.sections[section_ix(section)].len();
2240:        self.move_story(name, section, end, cx);
2241:    }
2242:
2243:    /// Alt+Up or Alt+Down: the story moves one place, and past the end of
2244:    /// its section into the next, between Next sprint and the Backlog, as
2245:    /// dragging does; a story done moves only among its own.
2246:    pub(crate) fn shift_story(&mut self, name: &str, up: bool, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2247:        let Some((section, ix)) = self.find(name) else {
2248:            return;
2249:        };
2250:        let len = self.sections[section_ix(section)].len();
2251:        match (section, up) {
2252:            (_, true) if ix > 0 => self.move_story(name, section, ix - 1, cx),
2253:            (_, false) if ix + 1 < len => self.move_story(name, section, ix + 2, cx),
2254:            (Section::Backlog, true) => self.move_to(name, Section::Next, cx),
2255:            (Section::Next, false) => self.move_story(name, Section::Backlog, 0, cx),
2256:            _ => {}
2257:        }
2258:    }
2259:
2260:    fn set_mode(&mut self, name: &str, mode: SendMode, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2261:        if let Ok(mut last) = LAST_MODE.lock() {
2262:            *last = Some(mode);
2263:        }
2264:        if let Some(story) = self.story_mut(name) {
2265:            story.anchor.mode = Some(mode);
2266:        }
2267:        self.mode_menu = None;
2268:        self.save_story_named(name, cx);
2269:        cx.notify();
2270:    }
2271:
2272:    /// Opens the mode picker of the story named so, its mode highlighted.
2273:    fn open_mode_menu(&mut self, name: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2274:        let Some(story) = self.story(name).filter(|_| {
2275:            self.find(name)
2276:                .is_some_and(|(section, _)| section != Section::Current)
2277:        }) else {
2278:            return;
2279:        };
2280:        let at = SendMode::ALL
2281:            .iter()
2282:            .position(|mode| *mode == story.mode())
2283:            .unwrap_or(0);
2284:        self.confirm_delete = None;
2285:        self.mode_menu = Some((name.to_string(), at));
2286:        cx.notify();
2287:    }
