2289:    fn change(
2290:        &mut self,
2291:        name: &str,
2292:        change: impl FnOnce(&mut HiddenAnchor),
2293:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
2294:    ) {
2295:        if let Some(story) = self.story_mut(name) {
2296:            change(&mut story.anchor);
2297:        }
2298:        self.save_story_named(name, cx);
2299:        cx.notify();
2300:    }
2301:
2302:    /// Attaches files picked to the story named so.
2303:    fn attach(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2304:        let picked = cx.prompt_for_paths(PathPromptOptions {
2305:            files: true,
2306:            directories: false,
2307:            multiple: true,
2308:            prompt: Some("Attach".into()),
2309:        });
2310:        let (name, project_dir) = (name.to_string(), self.project_dir.clone());
2311:        cx.spawn_in(window, async move |this, cx| {
2312:            let Ok(Ok(Some(paths))) = picked.await else {
2313:                return;
2314:            };
2315:            let saved = cx
2316:                .background_spawn(async move {
2317:                    let (mut images, mut files) = (Vec::new(), Vec::new());
2318:                    for path in paths {
2319:                        match attached_file::load(&path) {
2320:                            Ok(attached_file::Loaded::Image(image)) => images.push(image),
2321:                            Ok(attached_file::Loaded::File(file)) => files.push(file),
2322:                            Err(_) => {}
2323:                        }
2324:                    }
2325:                    let images = attached_image::save_all(&images, &project_dir)?;
2326:                    let files = attached_file::save_all(&files, &project_dir)?;
2327:                    anyhow::Ok((images, files))
2328:                })
2329:                .await;
2330:            this.update(cx, |this, cx| match saved {
2331:                Ok((images, files)) => this.change(
2332:                    &name,
2333:                    |anchor| {
2334:                        anchor.attached_images.extend(images);
2335:                        anchor.attached_files.extend(files);
2336:                    },
2337:                    cx,
2338:                ),
2339:                Err(err) => eprintln!("could not attach the files: {err:#}"),
2340:            })
2341:            .ok();
2342:        })
2343:        .detach();
2344:    }
2345:
2346:    /// How the story stands, once sent, and how long its task has run while
2347:    /// it runs.
2348:    fn status(&self, story: &Story, cx: &App) -> Option<(StoryState, Option<Duration>)> {
2349:        let task = story.task.as_deref()?;
2350:        // A task that can't be found never arrives: missing, not queued.
2351:        Some((self.hooks.state)(task, cx).unwrap_or((StoryState::Missing, None)))
2352:    }
2353:
2354:    fn state(&self, story: &Story, cx: &App) -> Option<StoryState> {
2355:        self.status(story, cx).map(|(state, _)| state)
2356:    }
2357:
2358:    /// Sends the stories named so, in order, as one block, each with its
2359:    /// steps after it.
2360:    fn send_named(&mut self, names: &[String], cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2361:        let mut sends = Vec::new();
2362:        for name in names {
2363:            let project_dir = self.project_dir.clone();
2364:            let Some(story) = self.story_mut(name) else {
2365:                continue;
2366:            };
2367:            if story.text().trim().is_empty() {
2368:                continue;
2369:            }
2370:            let task = crate::prompt_title::name_for(&project_dir, &story.title);
2371:            story.task = Some(task.clone());
2372:            story.from_agent = false;
2373:            sends.push(StorySend {
2374:                name: task,
2375:                text: story.text(),
2376:                mode: story.mode(),
2377:                attached: story.anchor.attached(),
2378:                sliced: story.anchor.sliced,
2379:                model: story.anchor.model.clone(),
2380:                effort: story.anchor.effort.clone(),
2381:                steps: story.steps().to_vec(),
2382:            });
2383:        }
2384:        if sends.is_empty() {
2385:            return;
2386:        }
2387:        self.save_order(cx);
2388:        (self.hooks.send)(sends, cx);
2389:        cx.notify();
2390:    }
2391:
2392:    /// Sends Next sprint, as the PlanningScope's sprint says: every story in
2393:    /// it, in order, as one block, moved into Current sprint. Nothing is
2394:    /// sent while a sprint is under way, or while planning is paused for
2395:    /// review; and no story is ever sent on its own.
2396:    pub fn send_sprint(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2397:        if self.pending().held().is_some() {
2398:            return;
2399:        }
2400:        self.close_story(window, cx);
2401:        self.start_sprint(cx);
2402:    }
2403:
2404:    /// Sends Next sprint's stories, moving them into Current sprint: those
2405:    /// with something to send, as a story with neither title nor prompt has
2406:    /// not. Gives how many were sent.
2407:    fn start_sprint(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> usize {
2408:        self.waiting_start = false;
2409:        let (sendable, rest): (Vec<Story>, Vec<Story>) = std::mem::take(&mut self.sections[0])
2410:            .into_iter()
2411:            .partition(|story| !story.text().trim().is_empty());
2412:        // Only what is sent goes into Current sprint: an empty story stays.
2413:        self.left_empty = rest.len();
2414:        self.sections[0] = rest;
2415:        if sendable.is_empty() {
2416:            cx.notify();
2417:            return 0;
2418:        }
2419:        // A story sent is no longer written in place.
2420:        if self
2421:            .open
2422:            .as_ref()
2423:            .is_some_and(|open| sendable.iter().any(|story| story.name == open.name))
2424:        {
2425:            self.open = None;
2426:            self.open_step = None;
2427:        }
2428:        let names: Vec<String> = sendable.iter().map(|story| story.name.clone()).collect();
2429:        self.sections[3].extend(sendable);
2430:        self.send_named(&names, cx);
2431:        self.save_order(cx);
2432:        cx.notify();
2433:        names.len()
2434:    }
2435:
2436:    /// Turns Start automatically on or off, saved with the plan. Off, a
2437:    /// sprint already sent carries on; on, the next may start at once.
2438:    fn set_auto_start(&mut self, on: bool, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2439:        self.notes.auto_start = on;
2440:        self.waiting_start = false;
2441:        self.save_order(cx);
2442:        cx.notify();
2443:    }
2444:
2445:    /// Why Next sprint isn't starting by itself, while Start automatically
2446:    /// is on and it would otherwise: paused for review, or the current
2447:    /// sprint ended with a story not done.
2448:    fn auto_held(&self, cx: &App) -> Option<&'static str> {
2449:        if !self.notes.auto_start {
2450:            return None;
2451:        }
2452:        if self.notes.paused.is_some() {
2453:            return Some(
2454:                "Planning is paused for review, so the next sprint waits for Resume or Approve.",
2455:            );
2456:        }
2457:        if !self.sprint_ended(cx) {
2458:            return None;
2459:        }
2460:        let states: Vec<Option<StoryState>> = self.sections[3]
2461:            .iter()
2462:            .map(|story| self.state(story, cx))
2463:            .collect();
2464:        if states.contains(&Some(StoryState::Missing)) {
2465:            return Some(
2466:                "A story's task can't be found, so the next sprint waits: close the sprint to go on.",
2467:            );
2468:        }
2469:        states
2470:            .iter()
2471:            .any(|state| *state != Some(StoryState::Done))
2472:            .then_some(
2473:                "A story of the current sprint didn't finish, so the next sprint waits: close the sprint to go on.",
2474:            )
2475:    }
2476:
2477:    /// Starts Next sprint by itself, as the PlanningScope's auto start
2478:    /// says, when Start automatically is on: once every story of Current
2479:    /// sprint ended Done, it is closed and Next sprint sent; with no sprint
2480:    /// current, Next sprint is sent once it holds a story. Never while
2481:    /// paused, after a sprint that ended with a story not done, while a
2482:    /// story of Next sprint is being written, or for an empty Next sprint.
2483:    pub fn auto_advance(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2484:        if !self.notes.auto_start || self.notes.paused.is_some() || self.advancing {
2485:            return;
2486:        }
2487:        // Found waiting as the project opened, it waits for Start now; once
2488:        // Next sprint is empty, a story added starts it as ever.
2489:        if self.waiting_start {
2490:            if self.sections[section_ix(Section::Current)].is_empty() && self.has_sendable() {
2491:                return;
2492:            }
2493:            self.waiting_start = false;
2494:        }
2495:        if !self.sections[3].is_empty() {
2496:            let all_done = self.sprint_ended(cx)
2497:                && self.sections[3]
2498:                    .iter()
2499:                    .all(|story| self.state(story, cx) == Some(StoryState::Done));
2500:            if !all_done {
2501:                return;
2502:            }
2503:        }
2504:        let writing = self
2505:            .open
2506:            .as_ref()
2507:            .and_then(|open| self.find(&open.name))
2508:            .is_some_and(|(section, _)| section == Section::Next);
2509:        let sendable = self.has_sendable();
2510:        if writing || !sendable {
2511:            // A sprint all done still closes by itself.
2512:            if !self.sections[3].is_empty() && !writing {
2513:                self.advancing = true;
2514:                self.close_sprint(cx);
2515:                self.advancing = false;
2516:            }
2517:            return;
2518:        }
2519:        self.advancing = true;
2520:        if !self.sections[3].is_empty() {
2521:            self.close_sprint(cx);
2522:        }
2523:        let count = self.start_sprint(cx);
2524:        self.advancing = false;
2525:        if count == 0 {
2526:            return;
2527:        }
2528:        let said = format!(
2529:            "Next sprint started: {count} {}",
2530:            if count == 1 { "story" } else { "stories" }
2531:        );
2532:        crate::debug_log::log(Some(&self.project_dir), format!("{said}, by itself"));
2533:        notify_started(said, cx);
2534:    }
2535:
2536:    /// Whether Next sprint holds a story with something to send.
2537:    fn has_sendable(&self) -> bool {
2538:        self.sections[section_ix(Section::Next)]
2539:            .iter()
2540:            .any(|story| !story.text().trim().is_empty())
2541:    }
2542:
2543:    /// Start now: sends Next sprint, found waiting as the project opened.
2544:    fn start_now(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2545:        self.waiting_start = false;
2546:        if self.pending().held().is_none() {
2547:            self.start_sprint(cx);
2548:        }
2549:        cx.notify();
2550:    }
2551:
2552:    /// Whether every story of the current sprint has ended.
2553:    fn sprint_ended(&self, cx: &App) -> bool {
2554:        !self.sections[3].is_empty()
2555:            && self.sections[3]
2556:                .iter()
2557:                .all(|story| self.state(story, cx).is_some_and(StoryState::ended))
2558:    }
2559:
2560:    /// Closes the current sprint: the stories done go to Done; those that
2561:    /// failed, were cancelled, or stopped go to the start of Next sprint, in
2562:    /// their order, to be sent again.
2563:    fn close_sprint(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2564:        let stories = std::mem::take(&mut self.sections[3]);
2565:        let mut again = Vec::new();
2566:        for mut story in stories {
2567:            if self.state(&story, cx) == Some(StoryState::Done) {
2568:                self.sections[2].push(story);
2569:            } else {
2570:                story.task = None;
2571:                again.push(story);
2572:            }
2573:        }
2574:        again.append(&mut self.sections[0]);
2575:        self.sections[0] = again;
2576:        self.save_order(cx);
2577:        cx.notify();
2578:    }
2579:
2580:    // --- Research and the pause ----------------------------------------------
2581:
2582:    /// Opens the panel to research a topic, beneath the Backlog's heading,
2583:    /// or, from a story, beneath it.
2584:    fn open_research(
2585:        &mut self,
2586:        about: Option<String>,
2587:        window: &mut Window,
2588:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
2589:    ) {
2590:        let running = self
2591:            .drafting
2592:            .as_ref()
2593:            .is_some_and(|drafting| drafting.running.is_some());
2594:        if !running {
2595:            self.drafting = None;
2596:        }
2597:        self.open_draft_panel(about, true, window, cx);
2598:        if let Some(drafting) = &mut self.drafting
2599:            && drafting.running.is_none()
2600:        {
2601:            drafting.research = true;
2602:        }
2603:        cx.notify();
2604:    }
2605:
2606:    /// Research ended, however it ended: its findings are saved as a file
2607:    /// of their own and listed, any proposals it ended with wait to be
2608:    /// decided, and planning pauses for review, naming it.
2609:    fn researched(
2610:        &mut self,
2611:        topic: String,
2612:        about: Option<String>,
2613:        reply: Result<String>,
2614:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
2615:    ) {
2616:        if let Some(drafting) = &mut self.drafting {
2617:            drafting.running = None;
2618:            drafting._run = None;
2619:        }
2620:        let ran_at = std::time::SystemTime::now()
2621:            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
2622:            .map_or(0, |since| since.as_secs());
2623:        let (state, body) = match reply {
2624:            Ok(reply) => {
2625:                for mut proposal in parse_proposals(&reply) {
2626:                    proposal.about = about.clone();
2627:                    self.settle_target(&mut proposal);
2628:                    self.proposals.push(proposal);
2629:                }
2630:                (FindingState::Done, reply)
2631:            }
2632:            Err(err) if format!("{err:#}") == "stopped" => (
2633:                FindingState::Stopped,
2634:                "Stopped before it finished, so there are no findings.".to_string(),
2635:            ),
2636:            Err(err) => {
2637:                // Shown in the panel too, with Try again, the question kept.
2638:                if let Some(drafting) = &mut self.drafting {
2639:                    drafting.error = Some(format!("{err:#}"));
2640:                }
2641:                (
2642:                    FindingState::Failed,
2643:                    format!("The research couldn't be done.\n\n```\n{err:#}\n```"),
2644:                )
2645:            }
2646:        };
2647:        let slug: String =
2648:            crate::prompt_title::anchor_name(&topic).unwrap_or_else(|| "Research".into());
2649:        // Never two of the same name, however quickly they come.
2650:        let mut file = format!("{ran_at}-{slug}.md");
2651:        let mut n = 2;
2652:        while self
2653:            .notes
2654:            .findings
2655:            .iter()
2656:            .any(|finding| finding.file == file)
2657:        {
2658:            file = format!("{ran_at}-{slug}{n}.md");
2659:            n += 1;
2660:        }
2661:        let text = format!("# Research: {topic}\n\n{body}\n");
2662:        self.write_later(research_dir(&self.project_dir).join(&file), Some(text), cx);
2663:        self.notes.findings.push(Finding {
2664:            file: file.clone(),
2665:            topic: topic.clone(),
2666:            ran_at,
2667:            state,
2668:            story: about,
2669:        });
2670:        // Paused, or kept paused, naming the latest.
2671:        self.notes.paused = Some(Pause {
2672:            research: file,
2673:            topic,
2674:        });
2675:        self.save_order(cx);
2676:        cx.notify();
2677:    }
2678:
2679:    /// Ends the pause, as its Resume does.
2680:    pub fn resume_planning(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2681:        self.notes.paused = None;
2682:        self.save_order(cx);
2683:        cx.notify();
2684:    }
2685:
2686:    /// Ends the pause and sends Next sprint at once, as its Approve and send
2687:    /// sprint does.
2688:    pub fn approve_and_send(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2689:        self.resume_planning(cx);
2690:        self.send_sprint(window, cx);
2691:    }
2692:
2693:    /// Opens the findings in `file` in a body tab of their own.
2694:    fn open_findings(&self, file: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2695:        let Some(finding) = self
2696:            .notes
2697:            .findings
2698:            .iter()
2699:            .find(|finding| finding.file == file)
2700:        else {
2701:            return;
2702:        };
2703:        let path = research_dir(&self.project_dir).join(&finding.file);
2704:        (self.hooks.open_findings)(path, format!("Research: {}", finding.topic), window, cx);
2705:    }
2706:
2707:    /// Deletes the findings in `file`, their file with them.
2708:    fn delete_findings(&mut self, file: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2709:        self.notes.findings.retain(|finding| finding.file != file);
2710:        self.write_later(research_dir(&self.project_dir).join(file), None, cx);
2711:        self.save_order(cx);
2712:        cx.notify();
2713:    }
2714:
2715:    fn resume(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2716:        if let Some(task) = self.story(name).and_then(|story| story.task.clone()) {
2717:            (self.hooks.resume)(&task, window, cx);
2718:        }
2719:    }

2721:    // --- Spec citations ------------------------------------------------------
2722:
2723:    /// Adds `text`, a link into the spec's Piton source, as one picked from
2724:    /// Add citation, or "No match" and what was looked for, to the spec of
2725:    /// the story named so, by hand. A link the Piton source doesn't define
2726:    /// isn't added, and the open story says why. Whether it was added.
2727:    fn add_citation(&mut self, name: &str, text: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> bool {
2728:        let Some(citation) = Citation::parse(text) else {
2729:            return false;
2730:        };
2731:        let checked = citation
2732:            .link
2733:            .as_deref()
2734:            .map_or(Ok(None), |link| check_link(link, &self.project_dir));
2735:        if let Err(why) = checked {
2736:            if let Some(open) = &mut self.open {
2737:                open.spec_error = Some(format!("That link wasn't added: {why}."));
2738:            }
2739:            cx.notify();
2740:            return false;
2741:        }
2742:        let Some(story) = self.story_mut(name).filter(|story| story.task.is_none()) else {
2743:            return false;
2744:        };
2745:        if !story.spec.contains(&citation) {
2746:            story.spec.push(citation);
2747:        }
2748:        if let Some(open) = &mut self.open {
2749:            open.spec_error = None;
2750:        }
2751:        self.save_order(cx);
2752:        cx.notify();
2753:        true
2754:    }
2755:
2756:    /// Removes the citation at `at` from the story named so.
2757:    fn remove_citation(&mut self, name: &str, at: usize, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2758:        let Some(story) = self.story_mut(name).filter(|story| story.task.is_none()) else {
2759:            return;
2760:        };
2761:        if at < story.spec.len() {
2762:            story.spec.remove(at);
2763:        }
2764:        self.save_order(cx);
2765:        cx.notify();
2766:    }
2767:
2768:    /// A list of citations, as a card or an open story shows them: each its
2769:    /// kind, muted, then the element's name and its file, muted, as a link
2770:    /// opening the Piton source at its declaration, with what it touches and
2771:    /// the words it rests on; a No match muted with a small marker; with a
2772:    /// remove button for the story named `editable`.
2773:    fn render_citations(
2774:        &self,
2775:        key: &str,
2776:        citations: &[Citation],
2777:        editable: Option<&str>,
2778:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
2779:    ) -> AnyElement {
2780:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
2781:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
2782:        let sent = editable
2783:            .and_then(|name| self.story(name))
2784:            .is_some_and(|story| story.task.is_some());
2785:        let rows = citations.iter().enumerate().map(|(at, citation)| {
2786:            let label = citation.label();
2787:            let link = citation.link.clone();
2788:            let row = h_flex()
2789:                .gap_1p5()
2790:                .items_center()
2791:                .text_xs()
2792:                .min_w_0()
2793:                .when_some(citation.kind, |row, kind| {
2794:                    row.child(div().flex_none().text_color(muted).child(kind.label()))
2795:                });
2796:            let row = match link {
2797:                Some(link) => {
2798:                    let project_dir = self.project_dir.clone();
2799:                    let open = self.hooks.open_source.clone();
2800:                    let file = citation.file().unwrap_or_default().to_string();
2801:                    row.child(Icon::new(IconName::BookOpen).xsmall().text_color(muted))
2802:                        .child(
2803:                            div()
2804:                                .id(SharedString::from(format!("planning-cite-{key}-{at}")))
2805:                                .key_context("PlanningCitation")
2806:                                .when_some(
2807:                                    self.row_focus.get(&format!("cite:{key}:{at}")),
2808:                                    |link, focus| link.track_focus(focus),
2809:                                )
2810:                                .flex_none()
2811:                                .cursor_pointer()
2812:                                .underline()
2813:                                .rounded(px(3.))
2814:                                .border_1()
2815:                                .border_color(gpui_kit::transparent_black())
2816:                                .focus(|link| link.border_color(theme.ring))
2817:                                .text_color(theme.link)
2818:                                .child(label)
2819:                                .on_action({
2820:                                    let (project_dir, open, link) =
2821:                                        (project_dir.clone(), open.clone(), link.clone());
2822:                                    move |_: &OpenCitation, window, cx| {
2823:                                        open_citation(&link, &project_dir, &open, window, cx)
2824:                                    }
2825:                                })
2826:                                .on_click(move |_, window, cx| {
2827:                                    open_citation(&link, &project_dir, &open, window, cx)
2828:                                }),
2829:                        )
2830:                        .child(div().min_w_0().truncate().text_color(muted).child(file))
2831:                }
2832:                None => row
2833:                    .child(div().text_color(muted).child("∅"))
2834:                    .child(div().flex_none().text_color(muted).child(label)),
2835:            };
2836:            let row = row
2837:                .when(!citation.note.is_empty(), |row| {
2838:                    row.child(
2839:                        div()
2840:                            .min_w_0()
2841:                            .truncate()
2842:                            .text_color(muted)
2843:                            .child(format!("— {}", citation.note)),
2844:                    )
2845:                })
2846:                .when(!citation.quote.is_empty(), |row| {
2847:                    row.child(
2848:                        div()
2849:                            .min_w_0()
2850:                            .truncate()
2851:                            .italic()
2852:                            .text_color(muted)
2853:                            .child(format!("“{}”", citation.quote)),
2854:                    )
2855:                })
2856:                .when_some(editable.filter(|_| !sent), |row, name| {
2857:                    let name = name.to_string();
2858:                    row.child(
2859:                        Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-uncite-{key}-{at}")))
2860:                            .ghost()
2861:                            .xsmall()
2862:                            .icon(IconName::X)
2863:                            .label("Remove")
2864:                            .tooltip("Remove this citation")
2865:                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, _, cx| {
2866:                                this.remove_citation(&name, at, cx)
2867:                            })),
2868:                    )
2869:                });
2870:            gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
2871:                div()
2872:                    .id(SharedString::from(format!("planning-citation-{key}-{at}")))
2873:                    .child(row),
2874:            )
2875:        });
2876:        v_flex()
2877:            .gap_0p5()
2878:            .children(rows)
2879:            .when(citations.is_empty() && editable.is_some(), |list| {
2880:                list.child(
2881:                    div()
2882:                        .text_xs()
2883:                        .text_color(muted)
2884:                        .child("Nothing cited yet."),
2885:                )
2886:            })
2887:            .into_any_element()

2890:    // --- Steps ---------------------------------------------------------------
2891:
2892:    /// Adds a step of `mode` holding `prompt` at the end of the story named
2893:    /// so, its model and effort the story's, saved straight away: only to a
2894:    /// story that exists, in the plan and in `.suspense/planning/`, and
2895:    /// isn't sent. Otherwise nothing changes and nothing is saved, and the
2896:    /// error says why. Gives the step's place among the story's steps.
2897:    pub fn add_step(
2898:        &mut self,
2899:        name: &str,
2900:        mode: SendMode,
2901:        prompt: String,
2902:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
2903:    ) -> Result<usize, String> {
2904:        let on_disk = story_file(&self.project_dir, name).exists();
2905:        let Some(story) = self.story_mut(name).filter(|_| on_disk) else {
2906:            return Err(NO_SUCH_STORY.into());
2907:        };
2908:        if story.task.is_some() {
2909:            return Err(STORY_SENT.into());
2910:        }
2911:        let mode = if mode == SendMode::Spec {
2912:            SendMode::Spec
2913:        } else {
2914:            SendMode::Code
2915:        };
2916:        story.anchor.added_steps.push(AddedStep {
2917:            mode,
2918:            prompt,
2919:            model: story.anchor.model.clone(),
2920:            effort: story.anchor.effort.clone(),
2921:        });
2922:        let at = story.anchor.added_steps.len() - 1;
2923:        self.save_story_named(name, cx);
2924:        cx.notify();
2925:        Ok(at)
2926:    }
2927:
2928:    /// Add step: a step at the end of the story named so, in the story's
2929:    /// mode where that is Code or Spec, open and focused. Where it can't be
2930:    /// added, says why beneath the steps, keeping what was to be added.
2931:    fn add_step_here(
2932:        &mut self,
2933:        name: &str,
2934:        kept: String,
2935:        window: &mut Window,
2936:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
2937:    ) {
2938:        let mode = self.story(name).map_or(SendMode::Code, Story::mode);
2939:        self.close_step(window, cx);
2940:        match self.add_step(name, mode, kept.clone(), cx) {
2941:            Ok(at) => {
2942:                self.step_error = None;
2943:                self.open_step(name, at, window, cx);
2944:            }
2945:            Err(why) => {
2946:                self.step_error = Some((name.to_string(), why, kept));
2947:                cx.notify();
2948:            }
2949:        }
2950:    }
2951:
2952:    /// Opens the step at `at` of the story named so to edit in place, its
2953:    /// prompt focused.
2954:    fn open_step(&mut self, name: &str, at: usize, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
2955:        if self
2956:            .open_step
2957:            .as_ref()
2958:            .is_some_and(|step| step.story == name && step.at == at)
2959:        {
2960:            return;
2961:        }
2962:        self.close_step(window, cx);
2963:        let Some(step) = self.story(name).and_then(|story| story.steps().get(at)) else {
2964:            return;
2965:        };
2966:        let text = step.prompt.clone();
2967:        let prompt = cx.new(|cx| {
2968:            TextareaState::new(window, cx)
2969:                .auto_grow(1, PROMPT_MAX_ROWS)
2970:                .placeholder("What this step does… (Enter adds another)")
2971:                .default_value(text)
2972:        });
2973:        let subscription = cx.subscribe_in(&prompt, window, {
2974:            let name = name.to_string();
2975:            move |this, input, event: &InputEvent, _, cx| {
2976:                if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
2977:                    let value = input.read(cx).value().to_string();
2978:                    let at = this.open_step.as_ref().map_or(at, |step| step.at);
2979:                    this.change(
2980:                        &name,
2981:                        |anchor| {
2982:                            if let Some(step) = anchor.added_steps.get_mut(at) {
2983:                                step.prompt = value;
2984:                            }
2985:                        },
2986:                        cx,
2987:                    );
2988:                }
2989:            }
2990:        });
2991:        prompt.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
2992:        self.open_step = Some(StepEditor {
2993:            story: name.to_string(),
2994:            at,
2995:            prompt,
2996:            _subscription: subscription,
2997:        });
2998:        cx.notify();
2999:    }
3000:
3001:    /// Closes the step being edited, its row focused again; one left with
3002:    /// nothing in it is removed, as an empty step adds none.
3003:    fn close_step(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3004:        self.keep_step_text(cx);
3005:        let Some(step) = self.open_step.take() else {
3006:            return;
3007:        };
3008:        let empty = self
3009:            .story(&step.story)
3010:            .and_then(|story| story.steps().get(step.at))
3011:            .is_some_and(|added| added.prompt.trim().is_empty());
3012:        if empty {
3013:            self.remove_step(&step.story, step.at, cx);
3014:        } else {
3015:            let handle = self.step_focus(&step.story, step.at, cx);
3016:            handle.focus(window, cx);
3017:        }
3018:        cx.notify();
3019:    }
3020:
3021:    /// Keeps what the open step's prompt holds in its step, as typed.
3022:    fn keep_step_text(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3023:        let Some(step) = self.open_step.as_ref() else {
3024:            return;
3025:        };
3026:        let (story, at) = (step.story.clone(), step.at);
3027:        let text = step.prompt.read(cx).value().to_string();
3028:        let changed = self
3029:            .story(&story)
3030:            .and_then(|found| found.steps().get(at))
3031:            .is_some_and(|added| added.prompt != text);
3032:        if changed {
3033:            self.change(
3034:                &story,
3035:                |anchor| {
3036:                    if let Some(step) = anchor.added_steps.get_mut(at) {
3037:                        step.prompt = text;
3038:                    }
3039:                },
3040:                cx,
3041:            );
3042:        }
3043:    }
3044:
3045:    /// Enter in a step's prompt: one with something in it is kept and
3046:    /// another starts after it; an empty one adds none.
3047:    fn step_entered(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3048:        let Some(step) = self.open_step.as_ref() else {
3049:            return;
3050:        };
3051:        let (story, at) = (step.story.clone(), step.at);
3052:        self.keep_step_text(cx);
3053:        let filled = self
3054:            .story(&story)
3055:            .and_then(|found| found.steps().get(at))
3056:            .is_some_and(|added| !added.prompt.trim().is_empty());
3057:        if filled {
3058:            self.add_step_here(&story, String::new(), window, cx);
3059:        } else {
3060:            self.close_step(window, cx);
3061:        }
3062:    }
3063:
3064:    /// Removes the step at `at` of the story named so; a sent story's steps
3065:    /// read as they were sent.
3066:    fn remove_step(&mut self, name: &str, at: usize, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3067:        if self.story(name).is_none_or(|story| story.task.is_some()) {
3068:            return;
3069:        }
3070:        if self
3071:            .open_step
3072:            .as_ref()
3073:            .is_some_and(|step| step.story == name && step.at == at)
3074:        {
3075:            self.open_step = None;
3076:        }
3077:        self.change(
3078:            name,
3079:            |anchor| {
3080:                if at < anchor.added_steps.len() {
3081:                    anchor.added_steps.remove(at);
3082:                }
3083:            },
3084:            cx,
3085:        );
3086:    }
3087:
3088:    /// Moves the step at `from` of the story named so to `to`, among its
3089:    /// steps only: the story's own prompt stays first.
3090:    fn move_step(&mut self, name: &str, from: usize, to: usize, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3091:        let Some(story) = self.story(name).filter(|story| story.task.is_none()) else {
3092:            return;
3093:        };
3094:        let count = story.steps().len();
3095:        if from >= count || to >= count || from == to {
3096:            return;
3097:        }
3098:        if let Some(step) = &mut self.open_step
3099:            && step.story == name
3100:            && step.at == from
3101:        {
3102:            step.at = to;
3103:        }
3104:        self.change(
3105:            name,
3106:            |anchor| {
3107:                let step = anchor.added_steps.remove(from);
3108:                anchor.added_steps.insert(to, step);
3109:            },
3110:            cx,
3111:        );
3112:    }
3113:
3114:    fn step_focus(&mut self, name: &str, at: usize, cx: &mut App) -> FocusHandle {
3115:        self.step_focus
3116:            .entry((name.to_string(), at))
3117:            .or_insert_with(|| cx.focus_handle().tab_stop(true))
3118:            .clone()
3119:    }
3120:
3121:    /// The step of the open story whose row has the focus.
3122:    fn focused_step(&self, window: &Window) -> Option<(String, usize)> {
3123:        self.step_focus
3124:            .iter()
3125:            .find(|(_, handle)| handle.is_focused(window))
3126:            .map(|(key, _)| key.clone())
3127:    }
3128:
3129:    /// Alt+Up or Alt+Down on a focused step: it moves one place among the
3130:    /// steps, the focus going with it.
3131:    fn shift_step(&mut self, up: bool, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3132:        let Some((name, at)) = self.focused_step(window) else {
3133:            return;
3134:        };
3135:        let to = if up { at.checked_sub(1) } else { Some(at + 1) };
3136:        let Some(to) = to.filter(|to| {
3137:            self.story(&name)
3138:                .is_some_and(|story| *to < story.steps().len())
3139:        }) else {
3140:            return;
3141:        };
3142:        self.move_step(&name, at, to, cx);
3143:        let handle = self.step_focus(&name, to, cx);
3144:        handle.focus(window, cx);
3145:    }

3149:    /// Opens the drafting panel beneath the Backlog's heading, or, `about`
3150:    /// a story, beneath it; focused, it takes the request.
3151:    fn open_draft_panel(
3152:        &mut self,
3153:        about: Option<String>,
3154:        focus: bool,
3155:        window: &mut Window,
3156:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
3157:    ) {
3158:        let same = self
3159:            .drafting
3160:            .as_ref()
3161:            .is_some_and(|drafting| drafting.about == about);
3162:        if !same
3163:            && self
3164:                .drafting
3165:                .as_ref()
3166:                .is_none_or(|drafting| drafting.running.is_none())
3167:        {
3168:            let placeholder = if about.is_some() {
3169:                "What this story still needs…"
3170:            } else {
3171:                "In a sentence or a paragraph…"
3172:            };
3173:            let input = cx.new(|cx| {
3174:                TextareaState::new(window, cx)
3175:                    .auto_grow(2, DRAFT_MAX_ROWS)
3176:                    .placeholder(placeholder)
3177:            });
3178:            self.drafting = Some(DraftPanel {
3179:                input,
3180:                about,
3181:                running: None,
3182:                activity: Arc::default(),
3183:                cancel: Arc::default(),
3184:                error: None,
3185:                details_open: false,
3186:                proposed_nothing: false,
3187:                research: false,
3188:                _run: None,
3189:            });
3190:        }
3191:        if focus && let Some(drafting) = &self.drafting {
3192:            drafting
3193:                .input
3194:                .update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
3195:        }
3196:        cx.notify();
3197:    }

3199:    /// Ask agent: drafting about the story named so.
3200:    fn ask_agent(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3201:        self.open_draft_panel(Some(name.to_string()), true, window, cx);
3202:    }

3204:    /// The stories proposals may add to, by id and title: every one not yet
3205:    /// sent, the one asked about first.
3206:    fn targets(&self, about: Option<&str>) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
3207:        let mut targets: Vec<(String, String)> = self.sections[..2]
3208:            .iter()
3209:            .flatten()
3210:            .filter(|story| story.task.is_none())
3211:            .map(|story| (story.name.clone(), story.title.clone()))
3212:            .collect();
3213:        if let Some(about) = about
3214:            && let Some(at) = targets.iter().position(|(name, _)| name == about)
3215:        {
3216:            let first = targets.remove(at);
3217:            targets.insert(0, first);
3218:        }
3219:        targets
3220:    }
3221:
3222:    /// Asks the harness, reading the project and changing nothing, to
3223:    /// propose stories for what the panel's request says.
3224:    fn draft(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3225:        let Some(drafting) = &self.drafting else {
3226:            return;
3227:        };
3228:        let wanted = drafting.input.read(cx).value().to_string();
3229:        self.draft_wanted(wanted, cx);
3230:    }
3231:
3232:    /// Draft again, for a proposal that cited no spec: it goes, and the
3233:    /// draft for that story runs once more.
3234:    fn draft_again(&mut self, id: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3235:        let Some(proposal) = self.proposal(id).cloned() else {
3236:            return;
3237:        };
3238:        self.proposals.retain(|kept| kept.id != id);
3239:        self.save_order(cx);
3240:        if self.drafting.is_none() {
3241:            self.open_draft_panel(proposal.about.clone(), false, window, cx);
3242:        }
3243:        let wanted = format!(
3244:            "This one story, grounded in the spec this time: search the spec for what it touches and cite it, or say No match.\n\nTitle: {}\n\n{}",
3245:            proposal.story_title(),
3246:            proposal.prompt
3247:        );
3248:        self.draft_wanted(wanted, cx);
3249:    }
3250:
3251:    /// Asks the harness to propose stories for `wanted`, as the panel's run.
3252:    fn draft_wanted(&mut self, wanted: String, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3253:        let Some(drafting) = &self.drafting else {
3254:            return;
3255:        };
3256:        if wanted.trim().is_empty() || drafting.running.is_some() {
3257:            return;
3258:        }
3259:        let about = drafting.about.clone();
3260:        let research = drafting.research;
3261:        let targets = self.targets(about.as_deref());
3262:        let story = about.as_deref().and_then(|name| self.story(name));
3263:        let reference = crate::hidden_anchor::reference_root(&self.project_dir);
3264:        let spec = spec_location(&self.project_dir);
3265:        let request = if research {
3266:            research_request(&wanted, &targets, story, reference, &spec)
3267:        } else {
3268:            draft_request(&wanted, &targets, story, reference, &spec)
3269:        };
3270:        let Some(drafting) = &mut self.drafting else {
3271:            return;
3272:        };
3273:        drafting.running = Some(Instant::now());
3274:        drafting.error = None;
3275:        drafting.details_open = false;
3276:        drafting.proposed_nothing = false;
3277:        drafting.cancel = Arc::default();
3278:        *drafting.activity.lock().unwrap() = "Starting the harness…".into();
3279:        let run = if research {
3280:            self.hooks.research
3281:        } else {
3282:            self.hooks.draft
3283:        };
3284:        let project_dir = self.project_dir.clone();
3285:        let (live, cancel) = (drafting.activity.clone(), drafting.cancel.clone());
3286:        let task = cx.spawn(async move |this, cx| {
3287:            let reply = cx
3288:                .background_spawn(async move {
3289:                    let tool = Mutex::new(String::new());
3290:                    run(&project_dir, &request, &cancel, &|line| {
3291:                        if let Some(said) = activity(&line, &mut tool.lock().unwrap()) {
3292:                            *activity_of(&live) = said;
3293:                        }
3294:                    })
3295:                })
3296:                .await;
3297:            this.update(cx, |this, cx| {
3298:                if research {
3299:                    this.researched(wanted, about, reply, cx)
3300:                } else {
3301:                    this.drafted(about, reply.map(|reply| parse_proposals(&reply)), cx)
3302:                }
3303:            })
3304:            .ok();
3305:        });
3306:        if let Some(drafting) = &mut self.drafting {
3307:            drafting._run = Some(task);
3308:        }
3309:        self.keep_ticking(cx);
3310:        cx.notify();
3311:    }
3312:
3313:    /// Stops the run, whatever it would have proposed never shown; research
3314:    /// stopped still ends, its findings saying so, and pauses planning.
3315:    fn stop_draft(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3316:        let Some(drafting) = &mut self.drafting else {
3317:            return;
3318:        };
3319:        drafting.cancel.cancel();
3320:        drafting._run = None;
3321:        let was_running = drafting.running.take().is_some();
3322:        if drafting.research && was_running {
3323:            let topic = drafting.input.read(cx).value().to_string();
3324:            let about = drafting.about.clone();
3325:            self.researched(topic, about, Err(anyhow::anyhow!("stopped")), cx);
3326:        }
3327:        cx.notify();
3328:    }
3329:
3330:    fn drafted(
3331:        &mut self,
3332:        about: Option<String>,
3333:        reply: Result<Vec<Proposal>>,
3334:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
3335:    ) {
3336:        let Some(drafting) = &mut self.drafting else {
3337:            return;
3338:        };
3339:        drafting.running = None;
3340:        drafting._run = None;
3341:        match reply {
3342:            Ok(proposals) if proposals.is_empty() => drafting.proposed_nothing = true,
3343:            Ok(proposals) => {
3344:                for mut proposal in proposals {
3345:                    proposal.about = about.clone();
3346:                    self.settle_target(&mut proposal);
3347:                    self.proposals.push(proposal);
3348:                }
3349:                self.save_order(cx);
3350:            }
3351:            Err(err) => drafting.error = Some(format!("{err:#}")),
3352:        }
3353:        cx.notify();
3354:    }
3355:
3356:    /// A proposal naming a story it can't be added to, one that no longer
3357:    /// exists or was sent, becomes a new story, saying why.
3358:    fn settle_target(&self, proposal: &mut Proposal) {
3359:        self.check_citations(proposal);
3360:        self.settle_story_target(proposal);
3361:    }
3362:
3363:    /// Checks each link a proposal cites, dropping any the Piton source
3364:    /// doesn't define, with a note of which and why.
3365:    fn check_citations(&self, proposal: &mut Proposal) {
3366:        check_citations(proposal, &self.project_dir);
3367:    }
3368:
3369:    fn settle_story_target(&self, proposal: &mut Proposal) {
3370:        let Some(target) = proposal.target.clone() else {
3371:            return;
3372:        };
3373:        let why = match self.story(&target) {
3374:            None => "The story it named no longer exists, so it would be a new story.",
3375:            Some(story) if story.task.is_some() => {
3376:                "The story it named has been sent, so it would be a new story."
3377:            }
3378:            Some(_) => return,
3379:        };
3380:        proposal.target = None;
3381:        proposal.note = Some(why.into());
3382:    }

3384:    /// Takes in a proposal from elsewhere, as an Ask answer's card, to be
3385:    /// edited in the panel before it is accepted.
3386:    pub fn take_proposal(
3387:        &mut self,
3388:        mut proposal: Proposal,
3389:        window: &mut Window,
3390:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
3391:    ) {
3392:        self.settle_target(&mut proposal);
3393:        let id = proposal.id.clone();
3394:        if self.proposal(&id).is_none() {
3395:            self.proposals.push(proposal);
3396:            self.save_order(cx);
3397:        }
3398:        self.open_draft_panel(None, false, window, cx);
3399:        self.edit_proposal(&id, window, cx);
3400:    }

3418:    /// Accepts a proposal from elsewhere, as an Ask answer's card does:
3419:    /// recorded in the plan, or, where it can't be, kept in the panel to be
3420:    /// accepted again, and why.
3421:    pub fn accept_outside(
3422:        &mut self,
3423:        mut proposal: Proposal,
3424:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
3425:    ) -> Result<(), String> {
3426:        self.settle_target(&mut proposal);
3427:        let id = proposal.id.clone();
3428:        if self.proposal(&id).is_none() {
3429:            self.proposals.push(proposal);
3430:        }
3431:        self.accept(&id, cx).map(|_| ())
3432:    }
3433:
3434:    /// Accepts the proposal `id` into the plan, saved straight away: a new
3435:    /// story at the Backlog's end, marked "From agent", or a step at the end
3436:    /// of the story it names, which is marked "Updated by agent". Where that
3437:    /// story no longer exists, or was sent, nothing is added: the proposal
3438:    /// stays, made a new story, saying why. Gives the story it went to.
3439:    pub(crate) fn accept(&mut self, id: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> Result<String, String> {
3440:        let Some(proposal) = self.proposal(id).cloned() else {
3441:            return Err("That proposal was already decided.".into());
3442:        };
3443:        if proposal.spec.is_empty() {
3444:            return Err(
3445:                "The drafter cited no spec, so this isn't a story yet: draft it again.".into(),
3446:            );
3447:        }
3448:        let (story, note) = match &proposal.target {
3449:            None => {
3450:                let mut story = Story::new(
3451:                    proposal.story_title(),
3452:                    proposal.prompt.clone(),
3453:                    proposal.mode(),
3454:                    &self.project_dir,
3455:                );
3456:                story.from_agent = true;
3457:                story.spec = proposal.spec.clone();
3458:                let name = story.name.clone();
3459:                self.sections[section_ix(Section::Backlog)].push(story);
3460:                self.save_story_named(&name, cx);
3461:                (name, "Added to Backlog".to_string())
3462:            }
3463:            Some(target) => {
3464:                let mode = match (proposal.mode(), self.story(target).map(Story::mode)) {
3465:                    (SendMode::Spec, _) => SendMode::Spec,
3466:                    (SendMode::Code, _) => SendMode::Code,
3467:                    (_, Some(SendMode::Spec)) => SendMode::Spec,
3468:                    _ => SendMode::Code,
3469:                };
3470:                let target = target.clone();
3471:                match self.add_step(&target, mode, proposal.prompt.clone(), cx) {
3472:                    Ok(at) => {
3473:                        let story = self.story_mut(&target).expect("added to just now");
3474:                        story.updated = true;
3475:                        story.highlight = Some(at);
3476:                        for citation in &proposal.spec {
3477:                            if !story.spec.contains(citation) {
3478:                                story.spec.push(citation.clone());
3479:                            }
3480:                        }
3481:                        (target.clone(), format!("Added to {}", story.title))
3482:                    }
3483:                    Err(why) => {
3484:                        if let Some(kept) = self.proposal_mut(id) {
3485:                            kept.target = None;
3486:                            kept.note = Some("It would now be a new story.".into());
3487:                        }
3488:                        self.refused.insert(id.to_string(), why.clone());
3489:                        self.save_order(cx);
3490:                        cx.notify();
3491:                        return Err(why);
3492:                    }
3493:                }
3494:            }
3495:        };
3496:        self.proposals.retain(|proposal| proposal.id != id);
3497:        self.refused.remove(id);
3498:        if self.editing.as_ref().is_some_and(|editor| editor.id == id) {
3499:            self.editing = None;
3500:        }
3501:        self.decided.push(Decided {
3502:            text: note,
3503:            story: story.clone(),
3504:        });
3505:        self.save_order(cx);
3506:        self.close_panel_when_decided(cx);
3507:        cx.notify();
3508:        Ok(story)
3509:    }
3510:
3511:    /// Rejects the proposal `id`: its card goes, an Undo in its place for a
3512:    /// few seconds.
3513:    pub(crate) fn reject(&mut self, id: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3514:        let Some(at) = self.proposals.iter().position(|proposal| proposal.id == id) else {
3515:            return;
3516:        };
3517:        let proposal = self.proposals.remove(at);
3518:        if self.editing.as_ref().is_some_and(|editor| editor.id == id) {
3519:            self.editing = None;
3520:        }
3521:        self.rejected.push(Rejected {
3522:            proposal,
3523:            at,
3524:            when: Instant::now(),
3525:        });
3526:        self.save_order(cx);
3527:        cx.spawn(async move |this, cx| {
3528:            cx.background_executor().timer(UNDO_FOR).await;
3529:            this.update(cx, |this, cx| {
3530:                this.rejected
3531:                    .retain(|rejected| rejected.when.elapsed() < UNDO_FOR);
3532:                this.close_panel_when_decided(cx);
3533:                cx.notify();
3534:            })
3535:            .ok();
3536:        })
3537:        .detach();
3538:        cx.notify();
3539:    }
3540:
3541:    /// Brings back the proposal `id`, rejected moments ago, where it was.
3542:    fn undo_reject(&mut self, id: &str, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3543:        let Some(at) = self
3544:            .rejected
3545:            .iter()
3546:            .position(|rejected| rejected.proposal.id == id)
3547:        else {
3548:            return;
3549:        };
3550:        let rejected = self.rejected.remove(at);
3551:        let place = rejected.at.min(self.proposals.len());
3552:        self.proposals.insert(place, rejected.proposal);
3553:        self.save_order(cx);
3554:        cx.notify();
3555:    }
3556:
3557:    fn accept_all(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3558:        let ids: Vec<String> = self
3559:            .proposals
3560:            .iter()
3561:            .map(|proposal| proposal.id.clone())
3562:            .collect();
3563:        for id in ids {
3564:            self.accept(&id, cx).ok();
3565:        }
3566:    }
3567:
3568:    fn reject_all(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3569:        let ids: Vec<String> = self
3570:            .proposals
3571:            .iter()
3572:            .map(|proposal| proposal.id.clone())
3573:            .collect();
3574:        for id in ids {
3575:            self.reject(&id, cx);
3576:        }
3577:    }
3578:
3579:    /// The panel closes itself once no proposal is left to decide, nor any
3580:    /// rejected one waiting on its Undo.
3581:    fn close_panel_when_decided(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3582:        let running = self
3583:            .drafting
3584:            .as_ref()
3585:            .is_some_and(|drafting| drafting.running.is_some());
3586:        if self.proposals.is_empty() && self.rejected.is_empty() && !running {
3587:            self.drafting = None;
3588:            self.editing = None;
3589:            self.decided.clear();
3590:            cx.notify();
3591:        }
3592:    }
3593:
3594:    /// Opens the proposal `id` for editing in its card: its title, prompt,
3595:    /// mode, and target, changed before it is accepted.
3596:    pub(crate) fn edit_proposal(&mut self, id: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3597:        let Some(proposal) = self.proposal(id).cloned() else {
3598:            return;
3599:        };
3600:        let title = cx.new(|cx| {
3601:            InputState::new(window, cx)
3602:                .placeholder("Story title")
3603:                .default_value(proposal.title.clone())
3604:        });
3605:        let prompt = cx.new(|cx| {
3606:            TextareaState::new(window, cx)
3607:                .auto_grow(2, PROMPT_MAX_ROWS)
3608:                .default_value(proposal.prompt.clone())
3609:        });
3610:        let subscriptions = vec![
3611:            cx.subscribe_in(&title, window, {
3612:                let id = id.to_string();
3613:                move |this, input, event: &InputEvent, _, cx| {
3614:                    if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
3615:                        let value = input.read(cx).value().to_string();
3616:                        if let Some(proposal) = this.proposal_mut(&id) {
3617:                            proposal.title = value;
3618:                        }
3619:                        this.save_order(cx);
3620:                        cx.notify();
3621:                    }
3622:                }
3623:            }),
3624:            cx.subscribe_in(&prompt, window, {
3625:                let id = id.to_string();
3626:                move |this, input, event: &InputEvent, _, cx| {
3627:                    if matches!(event, InputEvent::Change) {
3628:                        let value = input.read(cx).value().to_string();
3629:                        if let Some(proposal) = this.proposal_mut(&id) {
3630:                            proposal.prompt = value;
3631:                        }
3632:                        this.save_order(cx);
3633:                        cx.notify();
3634:                    }
3635:                }
3636:            }),
3637:        ];
3638:        title.update(cx, |input, cx| input.focus(window, cx));
3639:        self.editing = Some(ProposalEditor {
3640:            id: id.to_string(),
3641:            title,
3642:            prompt,
3643:            _subscriptions: subscriptions,
3644:        });
3645:        cx.notify();
3646:    }
3647:
3648:    fn set_proposal(
3649:        &mut self,
3650:        id: &str,
3651:        change: impl FnOnce(&mut Proposal),
3652:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
3653:    ) {
3654:        if let Some(proposal) = self.proposal_mut(id) {
3655:            change(proposal);
3656:            proposal.note = None;
3657:        }
3658:        self.refused.remove(id);
3659:        self.save_order(cx);
3660:        cx.notify();
3661:    }
3662:
3663:    // --- Rows and the keyboard ----------------------------------------------
3664:
3665:    /// The rows in the order they are shown, by their stories' names and
3666:    /// proposals' ids.
3667:    fn shown_rows(&self) -> Vec<String> {
3668:        let ids: Vec<String> = self
3669:            .proposals
3670:            .iter()
3671:            .map(|proposal| proposal.id.clone())
3672:            .collect();
3673:        let about = self.panel_about();
3674:        let mut rows = Vec::new();
3675:        for section in SECTIONS {
3676:            if section == Section::Done && !self.done_expanded {
3677:                continue;
3678:            }
3679:            if section == Section::Backlog && about.is_none() {
3680:                rows.extend(ids.iter().cloned());
3681:            }
3682:            for story in &self.sections[section_ix(section)] {
3683:                rows.push(story.name.clone());
3684:                if about.as_deref() == Some(story.name.as_str()) {
3685:                    rows.extend(ids.iter().cloned());
3686:                }
3687:            }
3688:        }
3689:        rows
3690:    }
3691:
3692:    /// The story the panel shows beneath, while it shows beneath one.
3693:    fn panel_about(&self) -> Option<String> {
3694:        let about = match &self.drafting {
3695:            Some(drafting) => drafting.about.clone(),
3696:            None => self
3697:                .proposals
3698:                .first()
3699:                .and_then(|proposal| proposal.about.clone()),
3700:        }?;
3701:        self.find(&about).is_some().then_some(about)
3702:    }
3703:
3704:    /// The row `step` rows on from the one named so.
3705:    fn row_after(&self, name: &str, step: isize) -> Option<String> {
3706:        let rows = self.shown_rows();
3707:        let at = rows.iter().position(|row| row == name)? as isize + step;
3708:        rows.get(usize::try_from(at).ok()?).cloned()
3709:    }
3710:
3711:    fn row_focus(&mut self, name: &str, cx: &mut App) -> FocusHandle {
3712:        self.row_focus
3713:            .entry(name.to_string())
3714:            .or_insert_with(|| cx.focus_handle().tab_stop(true))
3715:            .clone()
3716:    }
3717:
3718:    fn focus_row(&mut self, name: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3719:        let handle = self.row_focus(name, cx);
3720:        handle.focus(window, cx);
3721:        cx.notify();
3722:    }
3723:
3724:    /// The story or proposal whose row has the focus.
3725:    fn focused_row(&self, window: &Window) -> Option<String> {
3726:        self.row_focus
3727:            .iter()
3728:            .find(|(_, handle)| handle.is_focused(window))
3729:            .map(|(name, _)| name.clone())
3730:    }
3731:
3732:    /// Up or Down on a focused row: the mode picker's highlight moves while
3733:    /// it is open, else the focus moves to the row beside.
3734:    fn step_rows(&mut self, step: isize, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3735:        if let Some((_, at)) = &mut self.mode_menu {
3736:            let count = SendMode::ALL.len() as isize;
3737:            *at = (*at as isize + step).rem_euclid(count) as usize;
3738:            cx.notify();
3739:            return;
3740:        }
3741:        let Some(name) = self.focused_row(window) else {
3742:            return;
3743:        };
3744:        if let Some(next) = self.row_after(&name, step) {
3745:            self.focus_row(&next, window, cx);
3746:        }
3747:    }
3748:
3749:    /// Enter on a focused row: picks the highlighted mode while the picker
3750:    /// is open, deletes a story whose delete was asked for, else opens or
3751:    /// closes the story.
3752:    fn enter_row(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3753:        if let Some((name, at)) = self.mode_menu.clone() {
3754:            self.set_mode(&name, SendMode::ALL[at], cx);
3755:            return;
3756:        }
3757:        if self.confirm_delete.is_some() {
3758:            return self.confirm_delete(window, cx);
3759:        }
3760:        if let Some(name) = self.focused_row(window) {
3761:            self.toggle_story(&name, window, cx);
3762:        }
3763:    }
3764:
3765:    /// The focus after a proposal is decided: the next row, else the one
3766:    /// before, else Planning.
3767:    fn focus_after_decision(&mut self, id: &str, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3768:        let next = self.row_after(id, 1).or_else(|| self.row_after(id, -1));
3769:        match next {
3770:            Some(next) if next != id => self.focus_row(&next, window, cx),
3771:            _ => self.focus.focus(window, cx),
3772:        }
3773:    }
3774:
3775:    /// Ticks once a second while something runs, so its time counts.
3776:    fn keep_ticking(&mut self, cx: &mut Context<Self>) {
3777:        if self._tick.is_some() {
3778:            return;
3779:        }
3780:        self._tick = Some(cx.spawn(async move |this, cx| {
3781:            loop {
3782:                cx.background_executor().timer(Duration::from_secs(1)).await;
3783:                let running = this
3784:                    .update(cx, |this, cx| {
3785:                        let running = this.any_running(cx)
3786:                            || this
3787:                                .drafting
3788:                                .as_ref()
3789:                                .is_some_and(|drafting| drafting.running.is_some());
3790:                        if running {
3791:                            cx.notify();
3792:                        } else {
3793:                            this._tick = None;
3794:                        }
3795:                        running
3796:                    })
3797:                    .unwrap_or(false);
3798:                if !running {
3799:                    break;
3800:                }
3801:            }
3802:        }));
3803:    }
3804:
3805:    fn any_running(&self, cx: &App) -> bool {
3806:        self.sections
3807:            .iter()
3808:            .flatten()
3809:            .any(|story| self.state(story, cx) == Some(StoryState::Running))
3810:    }
3811:}

3815:    activity
3816:        .lock()
3817:        .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner())
3818:}

3820:/// Every section, in the order they are shown.
3821:const SECTIONS: [Section; 4] = [
3822:    Section::Current,

3824:    Section::Backlog,

3827:
3828:fn section_ix(section: Section) -> usize {
3829:    match section {
3830:        Section::Next => 0,
3831:        Section::Backlog => 1,
3832:        Section::Done => 2,
3833:        Section::Current => 3,
3834:    }
3835:}

3869:/// A sent story's status, as a task's is shown in the message list: its
3870:/// coloured label, with a spinner and its time while it runs, and a pause
3871:/// icon while paused.
3872:fn status_label(state: StoryState, elapsed: Option<Duration>, cx: &App) -> AnyElement {
3873:    use crate::theme::Hue;
3874:    // Its task can't be found: muted, in the warning colour, saying so.
3875:    if state == StoryState::Missing {
3876:        return div()
3877:            .id("planning-task-missing")
3878:            .text_xs()
3879:            .text_color(cx.theme().warning)
3880:            .tooltip(|window, cx| {
3881:                gpui_kit::component::tooltip::Tooltip::new("Its task can't be found")
3882:                    .build(window, cx)
3883:            })
3884:            .child(state.label())
3885:            .into_any_element();
3886:    }
3887:    let hue = match state {
3888:        StoryState::Queued => Hue::Grey,
3889:        StoryState::Running => Hue::Amber,
3890:        StoryState::Done => Hue::Green,
3891:        StoryState::Failed => Hue::Red,
3892:        StoryState::Cancelled => Hue::Orange,
3893:        // The theme's warning colour, as a paused task's.
3894:        StoryState::Paused | StoryState::Missing => Hue::Amber,
3895:    };
3896:    let muted = cx.theme().muted_foreground;
3897:    let tag = crate::theme::tag(hue, cx).text_xs().child(
3898:        h_flex()
3899:            .gap_1()
3900:            .items_center()
3901:            .when(state == StoryState::Paused, |tag| {
3902:                tag.child(Icon::new(IconName::Pause).xsmall())
3903:            })
3904:            .child(state.label()),
3905:    );
3906:    h_flex()
3907:        .gap_1()
3908:        .justify_end()
3909:        .items_center()
3910:        .when(state == StoryState::Running, |row| {
3911:            row.child(Spinner::new().xsmall())
3912:        })
3913:        .child(tag)
3914:        .children(
3915:            elapsed
3916:                .filter(|_| state == StoryState::Running)
3917:                .map(|elapsed| {
3918:                    div()
3919:                        .text_xs()
3920:                        .text_color(muted)
3921:                        .font_features(crate::subagents::tabular_figures())
3922:                        .child(crate::subagents::format_elapsed(elapsed))
3923:                }),
3924:        )
3925:        .into_any_element()
3926:}

3994:/// `text` cut to its first `lines` lines, and whether anything was cut.
3995:fn clipped(text: &str, lines: usize) -> (String, bool) {
3996:    let all: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
3997:    if all.len() <= lines {
3998:        return (text.to_string(), false);
3999:    }
4000:    (format!("{}…", all[..lines].join("\n")), true)
4001:}
