4003:impl Planning {
4004:    fn render_heading(&self, section: Section, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> AnyElement {
4005:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
4006:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
4007:        let count = self.sections[section_ix(section)].len();
4008:        let paused = self.sections[section_ix(section)]
4009:            .iter()
4010:            .filter(|story| self.state(story, cx) == Some(StoryState::Paused))
4011:            .count();
4012:        let warning = crate::theme::Hue::Amber.of(crate::theme::palette(cx));
4013:        let mut actions: Vec<AnyElement> = Vec::new();
4014:        match section {
4015:            Section::Current => {
4016:                if self.sprint_ended(cx) {
4017:                    actions.push(
4018:                        Button::new("planning-close-sprint")
4019:                            .ghost()
4020:                            .small()
4021:                            .label("Close sprint")
4022:                            .tooltip(
4023:                                "Move the stories done to Done, and any that didn't finish to Next sprint",
4024:                            )
4025:                            .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| this.close_sprint(cx)))
4026:                            .into_any_element(),
4027:                    );
4028:                }
4029:            }
4030:            Section::Next => {
4031:                let pending = self.pending();
4032:                actions.push(
4033:                    div()
4034:                        .id("planning-auto-start")
4035:                        .key_context("PlanningSwitch")
4036:                        .track_focus(&self.switch_focus)
4037:                        .rounded(px(4.))
4038:                        .border_1()
4039:                        .border_color(gpui_kit::transparent_black())
4040:                        .focus(|switch| switch.border_color(theme.ring))
4041:                        .aria_label("Start automatically")
4042:                        .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &ToggleSwitch, _, cx| {
4043:                            this.set_auto_start(!this.notes.auto_start, cx)
4044:                        }))
4045:                        .tooltip(|window, cx| {
4046:                            gpui_kit::component::tooltip::Tooltip::new(
4047:                                "Start the next sprint as soon as the current one is done",
4048:                            )
4049:                            .build(window, cx)
4050:                        })
4051:                        .child(
4052:                            gpui_kit::component::switch::Switch::new("planning-auto-start-switch")
4053:                                .xsmall()
4054:                                .label("Start automatically")
4055:                                .checked(self.notes.auto_start)
4056:                                .on_click(cx.listener(|this, on: &bool, _, cx| {
4057:                                    this.set_auto_start(*on, cx)
4058:                                })),
4059:                        )
4060:                        .into_any_element(),
4061:                );
4062:                actions.push(
4063:                    Button::new("planning-send-sprint")
4064:                        .primary()
4065:                        .small()
4066:                        .icon(IconName::Send)
4067:                        .label("Send sprint")
4068:                        .disabled(pending.held().is_some())
4069:                        .tooltip(match (pending.held(), pending.waiting) {
4070:                            (Some(why), _) => why.to_string(),
4071:                            (None, 1) => format!("Send the one story ({MOD}Shift+Enter)"),
4072:                            (None, n) => {
4073:                                format!("Send the {n} stories, in order ({MOD}Shift+Enter)")
4074:                            }
4075:                        })
4076:                        .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, window, cx| this.send_sprint(window, cx)))
4077:                        .into_any_element(),
4078:                );
4079:            }
4080:            Section::Backlog => {
4081:                actions.push(
4082:                    Button::new("planning-draft-stories")
4083:                        .ghost()
4084:                        .small()
4085:                        .icon(IconName::Sparkles)
4086:                        .label("Draft stories")
4087:                        .tooltip("Describe what you want, and have the agent propose stories")
4088:                        .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, window, cx| {
4089:                            this.open_draft_panel(None, true, window, cx)
4090:                        }))
4091:                        .into_any_element(),
4092:                );
4093:                actions.push(
4094:                    Button::new("planning-research")
4095:                        .ghost()
4096:                        .small()
4097:                        .icon(IconName::Search)
4098:                        .label("Research")
4099:                        .tooltip("Have the agent look into something, reading only, before anything is built")
4100:                        .on_click(
4101:                            cx.listener(|this, _, window, cx| this.open_research(None, window, cx)),
4102:                        )
4103:                        .into_any_element(),
4104:                );
4105:            }
4106:            Section::Done => {}
4107:        }
4108:        // What the last Send sprint left behind, while it is still there.
4109:        let empty = self.sections[section_ix(Section::Next)]
4110:            .iter()
4111:            .filter(|story| story.text().trim().is_empty())
4112:            .count()
4113:            .min(self.left_empty);
4114:        if section == Section::Next && empty > 0 {
4115:            actions.insert(
4116:                0,
4117:                gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4118:                    div()
4119:                        .id("planning-left-empty")
4120:                        .min_w_0()
4121:                        .truncate()
4122:                        .text_xs()
4123:                        .text_color(muted)
4124:                        .child(if empty == 1 {
4125:                            "1 empty story left in Next sprint".to_string()
4126:                        } else {
4127:                            format!("{empty} empty stories left in Next sprint")
4128:                        }),
4129:                )
4130:                .into_any_element(),
4131:            );
4132:        }
4133:        // Found waiting as the project opened: started only by hand.
4134:        if section == Section::Next
4135:            && self.waiting_start
4136:            && self.notes.auto_start
4137:            && self.auto_held(cx).is_none()
4138:        {
4139:            actions.insert(
4140:                0,
4141:                gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4142:                    h_flex()
4143:                        .id("planning-auto-start-waiting")
4144:                        .gap_2()
4145:                        .items_center()
4146:                        .child(div().text_xs().text_color(muted).child("Waiting to start"))
4147:                        .child(
4148:                            Button::new("planning-start-now")
4149:                                .ghost()
4150:                                .xsmall()
4151:                                .icon(IconName::Play)
4152:                                .label("Start now")
4153:                                .tooltip("Send Next sprint now")
4154:                                .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| this.start_now(cx))),
4155:                        ),
4156:                )
4157:                .into_any_element(),
4158:            );
4159:        }
4160:        // Why the next sprint isn't starting by itself, in its heading.
4161:        if section == Section::Next
4162:            && let Some(why) = self.auto_held(cx)
4163:        {
4164:            actions.insert(
4165:                0,
4166:                gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4167:                    div()
4168:                        .id("planning-auto-start-held")
4169:                        .min_w_0()
4170:                        .truncate()
4171:                        .text_xs()
4172:                        .text_color(warning)
4173:                        .child(why),
4174:                )
4175:                .into_any_element(),
4176:            );
4177:        }
4178:        let name = div().text_sm().font_semibold().child(section.label());
4179:        let heading = if section == Section::Done {
4180:            // Collapsed until opened, from its heading.
4181:            gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4182:                h_flex()
4183:                    .id("planning-done-toggle")
4184:                    .track_focus(&self.done_focus)
4185:                    .gap_2()
4186:                    .px_1()
4187:                    .items_center()
4188:                    .rounded(theme.radius)
4189:                    .border_1()
4190:                    .border_color(gpui_kit::transparent_black())
4191:                    .focus(|toggle| toggle.border_color(theme.ring))
4192:                    .cursor_pointer()
4193:                    .text_color(muted)
4194:                    .aria_label(if self.done_expanded {
4195:                        "Hide the stories done"
4196:                    } else {
4197:                        "Show the stories done"
4198:                    })
4199:                    .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| {
4200:                        this.done_expanded = !this.done_expanded;
4201:                        cx.notify();
4202:                    }))
4203:                    .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &ToggleStory, _, cx| {
4204:                        this.done_expanded = !this.done_expanded;
4205:                        cx.notify();
4206:                    }))
4207:                    .key_context(ROW_CONTEXT)
4208:                    .child(
4209:                        Icon::new(if self.done_expanded {
4210:                            IconName::ChevronDown
4211:                        } else {
4212:                            IconName::ChevronRight
4213:                        })
4214:                        .xsmall(),
4215:                    )
4216:                    .child(name),
4217:            )
4218:            .into_any_element()
4219:        } else {
4220:            name.into_any_element()
4221:        };
4222:        h_flex()
4223:            .w_full()
4224:            .h(HEADING_HEIGHT)
4225:            .flex_none()
4226:            .gap_2()
4227:            .items_center()
4228:            .child(heading)
4229:            .child(div().text_xs().text_color(muted).child(count.to_string()))
4230:            // Start automatically on, beside Next sprint's count.
4231:            .when(section == Section::Next && self.notes.auto_start, |row| {
4232:                row.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4233:                    div()
4234:                        .id("planning-auto-badge")
4235:                        .text_xs()
4236:                        .font_medium()
4237:                        .text_color(theme.accent)
4238:                        .child("Auto"),
4239:                ))
4240:            })
4241:            .when(paused > 0, |row| {
4242:                row.child(
4243:                    div()
4244:                        .text_xs()
4245:                        .text_color(warning)
4246:                        .child(format!("{paused} paused")),
4247:                )
4248:            })
4249:            .child(div().flex_1())
4250:            .children(actions)
4251:            .into_any_element()
4252:    }
4253:
4254:    fn render_section(
4255:        &mut self,
4256:        section: Section,
4257:        window: &mut Window,
4258:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
4259:    ) -> AnyElement {
4260:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
4261:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
4262:        let heading = self.render_heading(section, cx);
4263:        let mut column = v_flex()
4264:            .w_full()
4265:            .gap(ROW_GAP)
4266:            .track_focus(&self.section_focus[section.key()])
4267:            .child(div().pb(HEADING_GAP - ROW_GAP).child(heading));
4268:        // While the plan is read: skeleton rows, never an empty section.
4269:        if !self.loaded && section != Section::Done {
4270:            if section == Section::Next
4271:                && let Some(why) = self.load_error.clone()
4272:            {
4273:                column = column.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4274:                    h_flex()
4275:                        .id("planning-load-error")
4276:                        .gap_2()
4277:                        .items_center()
4278:                        .child(div().flex_1().text_sm().text_color(theme.danger).child(why))
4279:                        .child(
4280:                            Button::new("planning-load-retry")
4281:                                .ghost()
4282:                                .xsmall()
4283:                                .label("Try again")
4284:                                .tooltip("Read the plan again")
4285:                                .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| this.read_again(cx))),
4286:                        ),
4287:                ));
4288:            } else {
4289:                column = column.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4290:                    v_flex()
4291:                        .id(SharedString::from(format!(
4292:                            "planning-loading-{}",
4293:                            section.key()
4294:                        )))
4295:                        .gap(ROW_GAP)
4296:                        .child(skeleton_row(cx))
4297:                        .child(skeleton_row(cx)),
4298:                ));
4299:            }
4300:            return column.into_any_element();
4301:        }
4302:        let about = self.panel_about();
4303:        let panel_wanted = self.drafting.is_some() || !self.proposals.is_empty();
4304:        if section == Section::Backlog && panel_wanted && about.is_none() {
4305:            // Proposals kept from before come back in their panel.
4306:            if self.drafting.is_none() {
4307:                self.open_draft_panel(None, false, window, cx);
4308:            }
4309:            column = column.child(self.render_draft_panel(window, cx));
4310:        }
4311:        if section == Section::Done && !self.done_expanded {
4312:            return column.into_any_element();
4313:        }
4314:        let gap = self.gap.get();
4315:        let count = self.sections[section_ix(section)].len();
4316:        if count == 0 {
4317:            // Lets UI tests find the empty state; inert in normal builds.
4318:            column = column.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
4319:                self.drop_zone(
4320:                    section,
4321:                    0,
4322:                    div().id(SharedString::from(format!(
4323:                        "planning-empty-{}",
4324:                        section.key()
4325:                    ))),
4326:                    cx,
4327:                )
4328:                .child(empty_line(section.empty(), muted)),
4329:            ));
4330:        }
4331:        for ix in 0..count {
4332:            let name = self.sections[section_ix(section)][ix].name.clone();
4333:            let open = self.open.as_ref().is_some_and(|open| open.name == name);
4334:            let row = self.render_row(section, ix, open, gap, window, cx);
4335:            column = column.child(row);
4336:            // A sent story's steps beneath it: in Current sprint always,
4337:            // in Done collapsed to their count until expanded.
4338:            if !open {
4339:                let listed = match section {
4340:                    Section::Current => self.render_story_steps(&name, None, cx),
4341:                    Section::Done => {
4342:                        self.render_story_steps(&name, Some(format!("done:{name}")), cx)
4343:                    }
4344:                    _ => None,
4345:                };
4346:                column = column.children(listed);
4347:            }
4348:            if open {
4349:                let steps = self.story(&name).map_or(0, |story| story.steps().len());
4350:                for at in 0..steps {
4351:                    self.step_focus(&name, at, cx);
4352:                }
4353:                let cited = self.story(&name).map_or(0, |story| story.spec.len());
4354:                for at in 0..cited {
4355:                    self.row_focus(&format!("cite:{name}:{at}"), cx);
4356:                }
4357:                column = column.child(self.render_open(&name, window, cx));
4358:                if let Some(findings) = self.render_findings(Some(&name), window, cx) {
4359:                    column = column.child(div().pl(px(28.)).child(findings));
4360:                }
4361:            }
4362:            if panel_wanted && about.as_deref() == Some(name.as_str()) {
4363:                if self.drafting.is_none() {
4364:                    self.open_draft_panel(Some(name.clone()), false, window, cx);
4365:                }
4366:                column = column.child(self.render_draft_panel(window, cx));
4367:            }
4368:        }
4369:        if section == Section::Next {
4370:            let new_story = self
4371:                .drop_zone(
4372:                    section,
4373:                    count,
4374:                    h_flex()
4375:                        .id(SharedString::from(format!(
4376:                            "planning-new-story-{}",
4377:                            section.key()
4378:                        )))
4379:                        .h(px(28.))
4380:                        .px_3()
4381:                        .gap_2()
4382:                        .items_center()
4383:                        .rounded(theme.radius)
4384:                        .cursor_pointer()
4385:                        .text_sm()
4386:                        .text_color(muted)
4387:                        .aria_label("New story")
4388:                        .tooltip(move |window, cx| {
4389:                            gpui_kit::component::tooltip::Tooltip::new(format!(
4390:                                "Add a story here ({MOD}N)"
4391:                            ))
4392:                            .build(window, cx)
4393:                        })
4394:                        .hover(|row| row.bg(theme.list_hover)),
4395:                    cx,
4396:                )
4397:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| this.new_story(window, cx)))
4398:                .child(Icon::new(IconName::Plus).xsmall())
4399:                .child("New story");
4400:            column = column.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(new_story));
4401:        }
4402:        column.into_any_element()
4403:    }
4404:
4405:    /// `element` as somewhere a dragged story can be dropped, at place `at`
4406:    /// of `section`.
4407:    fn drop_zone(
4408:        &self,
4409:        section: Section,
4410:        at: usize,
4411:        element: Stateful<Div>,
4412:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
4413:    ) -> Stateful<Div> {
4414:        let gap = self.gap.clone();
4415:        let shown = gap.get() == Some((section, at));
4416:        element
4417:            .relative()
4418:            .on_drag_move(move |event: &DragMoveEvent<StoryDrag>, window, _| {
4419:                if event.bounds.contains(&event.event.position) && gap.get() != Some((section, at))
4420:                {
4421:                    gap.set(Some((section, at)));
4422:                    window.refresh();
4423:                }
4424:            })
4425:            .on_drop(cx.listener(move |this, drag: &StoryDrag, _, cx| {
4426:                this.gap.set(None);
4427:                this.move_story(&drag.name, section, at, cx);
4428:            }))
4429:            .when(shown, |this| {
4430:                this.child(crate::prompt_mode::drop_indicator(false, false, cx))
4431:            })
4432:    }
4433:
4434:    fn render_mode_menu(
4435:        &self,
4436:        name: &str,
4437:        mode: SendMode,
4438:        highlighted: usize,
4439:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
4440:    ) -> impl IntoElement + use<> {
4441:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
4442:        let items = SendMode::ALL
4443:            .into_iter()
4444:            .enumerate()
4445:            .map(|(at, choice)| {
4446:                let name = name.to_string();
4447:                h_flex()
4448:                    .id(SharedString::from(format!(
4449:                        "planning-mode-{name}-{}",
4450:                        mode_label(choice)
4451:                    )))
4452:                    .px_2()
4453:                    .h(px(26.))
4454:                    .gap_2()
4455:                    .items_center()
4456:                    .rounded(theme.radius)
4457:                    .cursor_pointer()
4458:                    .when(at == highlighted, |item| item.bg(theme.list_active))
4459:                    .hover(|item| item.bg(theme.list_hover))
4460:                    .aria_label(mode_label(choice))
4461:                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, _, cx| this.set_mode(&name, choice, cx)))
4462:                    .child(
4463:                        div().w(px(14.)).children(
4464:                            (choice == mode).then(|| Icon::new(IconName::Check).xsmall()),
4465:                        ),
4466:                    )
4467:                    .child(mode_pill_label(choice, cx))
4468:            })
4469:            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
4470:        let menu = v_flex()
4471:            .id(SharedString::from(format!("planning-mode-menu-{name}")))
4472:            .mt(px(26.))
4473:            .w(px(140.))
4474:            .p_1()
4475:            .rounded(theme.radius)
4476:            .border_1()
4477:            .border_color(theme.border)
4478:            .bg(theme.popover)
4479:            .shadow_md()
4480:            .occlude()
4481:            .on_mouse_down_out(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| {
4482:                this.mode_menu = None;
4483:                cx.notify();
4484:            }))
4485:            .children(items);
4486:        deferred(
4487:            anchored()
4488:                .snap_to_window()
4489:                .child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(menu)),
4490:        )
4491:        .with_priority(2)
4492:    }
4493:
4494:    fn render_row(
4495:        &mut self,
4496:        section: Section,
4497:        ix: usize,
4498:        open: bool,
4499:        gap: Option<(Section, usize)>,
4500:        window: &mut Window,
4501:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
4502:    ) -> AnyElement {
4503:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
4504:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
4505:        let story = &self.sections[section_ix(section)][ix];
4506:        let name = story.name.clone();
4507:        let mode = story.mode();
4508:        let status = self.status(story, cx);
4509:        let (from_agent, updated, attached) =
4510:            (story.from_agent, story.updated, story.has_attachments());
4511:        let steps: Vec<String> = story
4512:            .steps()
4513:            .iter()
4514:            .enumerate()
4515:            .map(|(at, step)| {
4516:                format!(
4517:                    "{}. {}",
4518:                    at + 2,
4519:                    step.prompt.lines().next().unwrap_or_default()
4520:                )
4521:            })
4522:            .collect();
4523:        let spec_lines: Vec<String> = story
4524:            .spec
4525:            .iter()
4526:            .map(|citation| match &citation.note {
4527:                note if note.is_empty() => format!("- {}", citation.label()),
4528:                note => format!("- {}: {note}", citation.label()),
4529:            })
4530:            .collect();
4531:        let task = story.task.clone();
4532:        let untitled = story.title.trim().is_empty();
4533:        let title: SharedString = if untitled {
4534:            "Untitled".into()
4535:        } else {
4536:            story.title.clone().into()
4537:        };
4538:        let len = self.sections[section_ix(section)].len();
4539:        let focus = self.row_focus(&name, cx);
4540:        let focused = focus.is_focused(window);
4541:        let group = SharedString::from(format!("planning-row-{name}"));
4542:        let paused = status.is_some_and(|(state, _)| state == StoryState::Paused);
4543:        let confirming = self.confirm_delete.as_deref() == Some(name.as_str());
4544:        let current = section == Section::Current;
4545:
4546:        // The mode picker: its pill, and beneath it, while open, the modes.
4547:        let mode_menu = self
4548:            .mode_menu
4549:            .as_ref()
4550:            .filter(|(of, _)| *of == name)
4551:            .map(|(_, at)| *at)
4552:            .map(|highlighted| self.render_mode_menu(&name, mode, highlighted, cx));
4553:        let mode_picker = {
4554:            let name = name.clone();
4555:            Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-mode-{name}")))
4556:                .ghost()
4557:                .xsmall()
4558:                .child(mode_pill_label(mode, cx))
4559:                .disabled(current)
4560:                .tooltip("How it will be sent (M)")
4561:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, _, cx| {
4562:                    if this.mode_menu.as_ref().is_some_and(|(of, _)| *of == name) {
4563:                        this.mode_menu = None;
4564:                        cx.notify();
4565:                    } else {
4566:                        this.open_mode_menu(&name, cx);
4567:                    }
4568:                }))
4569:        };
4570:        let resume = paused.then(|| {
4571:            let name = name.clone();
4572:            Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-resume-{name}")))
4573:                .ghost()
4574:                .xsmall()
4575:                .icon(IconName::Play)
4576:                .label("Resume")
4577:                .tooltip("Resume this story's task where it left off")
4578:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| this.resume(&name, window, cx)))
4579:        });
4580:        let this = cx.entity().downgrade();
4581:        let menu_name = name.clone();
4582:        let sent = task.is_some();
4583:        let menu = Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-menu-{name}")))
4584:            .ghost()
4585:            .xsmall()
4586:            .icon(IconName::Ellipsis)
4587:            .label("More")
4588:            .tooltip("More")
4589:            .dropdown_menu_with_anchor(gpui_kit::Anchor::TopRight, move |menu, window, _| {
4590:                let _ = window;
4591:                let item = |label: &'static str,
4592:                            act: fn(&mut Planning, &str, &mut Window, &mut Context<Planning>)| {
4593:                    let (this, name) = (this.clone(), menu_name.clone());
4594:                    PopupMenuItem::new(label).on_click(move |_, window, cx| {
4595:                        this.update(cx, |this, cx| act(this, &name, window, cx)).ok();
4596:                    })
4597:                };
4598:                let menu = match section {
4599:                    Section::Next => menu
4600:                        .item(item("Move to backlog (Ctrl+Enter)", |this, name, _, cx| {
4601:                            this.move_story(name, Section::Backlog, 0, cx)
4602:                        })),
4603:                    Section::Backlog | Section::Done => menu.item(item(

5144:    /// from 2, its mode pill and first line, opening in place to edit,
5145:    /// dragged or moved with Alt+Up and Alt+Down, and removed from its row or
5146:    /// its menu; then Add step, and why a step couldn't be added. A sent
5147:    /// story's steps read as they were sent.
5148:    fn render_steps(
5149:        &self,
5150:        story: &Story,
5151:        highlight: Option<usize>,
5152:        window: &mut Window,
5153:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
5154:    ) -> AnyElement {
5155:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
5156:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
5157:        let name = story.name.clone();
5158:        let sent = story.task.is_some();
5159:        let count = story.steps().len();
5160:        let dragging = cx.has_active_drag();
5161:        let gap = self.step_gap.get().filter(|_| dragging);
5162:        let mut list = v_flex().w_full().gap(ROW_GAP);
5163:        if count > 0 || !sent {
5164:            list = list.child(div().text_xs().text_color(muted).child("Steps"));
5165:        }
5166:        for (at, step) in story.steps().iter().enumerate() {
5167:            let focus = self
5168:                .step_focus
5169:                .get(&(name.clone(), at))
5170:                .cloned()
5171:                .unwrap_or_else(|| self.focus.clone());
5172:            let focused = focus.is_focused(window);
5173:            let highlighted = highlight == Some(at);
5174:            let editing = self
5175:                .open_step
5176:                .as_ref()
5177:                .filter(|open| open.story == name && open.at == at)
5178:                .map(|open| open.prompt.clone());
5179:            let first = step.prompt.lines().next().unwrap_or_default().to_string();
5180:            let label: SharedString = format!("{}. {first}", at + 2).into();
5181:            let mode_button = {
5182:                let name = name.clone();
5183:                Button::new(SharedString::from(format!(
5184:                    "planning-step-mode-{name}-{at}"
5185:                )))
5186:                .ghost()
5187:                .xsmall()
5188:                .child(mode_pill_label(step.mode, cx))
5189:                .disabled(sent)
5190:                .tooltip("Code or Spec: click to switch")
5191:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, _, cx| {
5192:                    this.change(
5193:                        &name,
5194:                        |anchor| {
5195:                            if let Some(step) = anchor.added_steps.get_mut(at) {
5196:                                step.mode = if step.mode == SendMode::Spec {
5197:                                    SendMode::Code
5198:                                } else {
5199:                                    SendMode::Spec
5200:                                };
5201:                            }
5202:                        },
5203:                        cx,
5204:                    )
5205:                }))
5206:            };
5207:            let remove = (!sent).then(|| {
5208:                let name = name.clone();
5209:                Button::new(SharedString::from(format!(
5210:                    "planning-step-remove-{name}-{at}"
5211:                )))
5212:                .ghost()
5213:                .xsmall()
5214:                .icon(IconName::X)
5215:                .tooltip(format!("Remove step {}", at + 2))
5216:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, _, cx| this.remove_step(&name, at, cx)))
5217:            });
5218:            let menu = (!sent).then(|| {
5219:                let this = cx.entity().downgrade();
5220:                let name = name.clone();
5221:                Button::new(SharedString::from(format!(
5222:                    "planning-step-menu-{name}-{at}"
5223:                )))
5224:                .ghost()
5225:                .xsmall()
5226:                .icon(IconName::Ellipsis)
5227:                .label("More")
5228:                .tooltip("More")
5229:                .dropdown_menu_with_anchor(
5230:                    gpui_kit::Anchor::TopRight,
5231:                    move |menu, _, _| {
5232:                        let item = |label: &'static str,
5233:                                    act: fn(
5234:                            &mut Planning,
5235:                            &str,
5236:                            usize,
5237:                            usize,
5238:                            &mut Context<Planning>,
5239:                        )| {
5240:                            let (this, name) = (this.clone(), name.clone());
5241:                            PopupMenuItem::new(label).on_click(move |_, _, cx| {
5242:                                this.update(cx, |this, cx| act(this, &name, at, count, cx))
5243:                                    .ok();
5244:                            })
5245:                        };
5246:                        menu.item(item("Move up (Alt+Up)", |this, name, at, _, cx| {
5247:                            if at > 0 {
5248:                                this.move_step(name, at, at - 1, cx)
5249:                            }
5250:                        }))
5251:                        .item(item("Move down (Alt+Down)", |this, name, at, count, cx| {
5252:                            if at + 1 < count {
5253:                                this.move_step(name, at, at + 1, cx)
5254:                            }
5255:                        }))
5256:                        .separator()
5257:                        .item(item("Remove step", |this, name, at, _, cx| {
5258:                            this.remove_step(name, at, cx)
5259:                        }))
5260:                    },
5261:                )
5262:            });
5263:            let drag = StepDrag {
5264:                story: name.clone(),
5265:                from: at,
5266:                label: label.clone(),
5267:            };
5268:            let click_name = name.clone();
5269:            let drop_name = name.clone();
5270:            let row = h_flex()
5271:                .id(SharedString::from(format!("planning-step-{name}-{at}")))
5272:                .key_context(STEP_CONTEXT)
5273:                .track_focus(&focus)
5274:                .aria_label(label.clone())
5275:                .relative()
5276:                .gap_2()
5277:                .px_2()
5278:                .min_h(px(28.))
5279:                .items_center()
5280:                .rounded(px(4.))
5281:                .border_1()
5282:                .border_color(if focused {
5283:                    theme.ring
5284:                } else {
5285:                    gpui_kit::transparent_black()
5286:                })
5287:                .cursor_pointer()
5288:                .when(highlighted, |row| row.bg(theme.primary.opacity(0.12)))
5289:                .when(!highlighted, |row| {
5290:                    row.hover(|row| row.bg(theme.list_hover))
5291:                })
5292:                .when(!sent, |row| {
5293:                    row.on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| {
5294:                        this.open_step(&click_name, at, window, cx)
5295:                    }))
5296:                    .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &MoveStoryUp, window, cx| {
5297:                        this.shift_step(true, window, cx)
5298:                    }))
5299:                    .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &MoveStoryDown, window, cx| {
5300:                        this.shift_step(false, window, cx)
5301:                    }))
5302:                    .on_drag(drag, |drag, _, _, cx| cx.new(|_| drag.clone()))
5303:                    .on_drag_move({
5304:                        let gap = self.step_gap.clone();
5305:                        move |event: &DragMoveEvent<StepDrag>, window, _| {
5306:                            let bounds = event.bounds;
5307:                            if !bounds.contains(&event.event.position) {
5308:                                return;
5309:                            }
5310:                            let to = at + (event.event.position.y > bounds.center().y) as usize;
5311:                            if gap.get() != Some(to) {
5312:                                gap.set(Some(to));
5313:                                window.refresh();
5314:                            }
5315:                        }
5316:                    })
5317:                    .on_drop(cx.listener(
5318:                        move |this, drag: &StepDrag, _, cx| {
5319:                            let gap = this.step_gap.take().unwrap_or(at);
5320:                            if drag.story == drop_name {
5321:                                // The gaps either side of it are where it is.
5322:                                let to = if gap > drag.from { gap - 1 } else { gap };
5323:                                this.move_step(&drop_name, drag.from, to, cx);
5324:                            }
5325:                        },
5326:                    ))
5327:                })
5328:                .when(gap == Some(at), |row| {
5329:                    row.child(crate::prompt_mode::drop_indicator(false, false, cx))
5330:                })
5331:                .when(gap == Some(at + 1) && at + 1 == count, |row| {
5332:                    row.child(crate::prompt_mode::drop_indicator(false, true, cx))
5333:                })
5334:                .when(!sent, |row| {
5335:                    row.child(
5336:                        Icon::new(IconName::GripVertical)
5337:                            .xsmall()
5338:                            .text_color(muted)
5339:                            .cursor_grab(),
5340:                    )
5341:                })
5342:                .child(
5343:                    div()
5344:                        .w(px(16.))
5345:                        .text_xs()
5346:                        .text_color(muted)
5347:                        .child(format!("{}", at + 2)),
5348:                )
5349:                .child(own_clicks(div().flex_none()).child(mode_button))
5350:                .child(
5351:                    div()
5352:                        .flex_1()
5353:                        .min_w_0()
5354:                        .truncate()
5355:                        .text_sm()
5356:                        .text_color(if first.trim().is_empty() {
5357:                            muted
5358:                        } else {
5359:                            theme.foreground
5360:                        })
5361:                        .child(if first.trim().is_empty() {
5362:                            "Empty step".to_string()
5363:                        } else {
5364:                            first
5365:                        }),
5366:                )
5367:                .when(highlighted, |row| {
5368:                    row.child(
5369:                        div()
5370:                            .text_xs()
5371:                            .text_color(theme.primary)
5372:                            .child("Added by agent"),
5373:                    )
5374:                })
5375:                .children(remove.map(|remove| own_clicks(div().flex_none()).child(remove)))
5376:                .children(menu.map(|menu| own_clicks(div().flex_none()).child(menu)));
5377:            list = list.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(row));
5378:            if let Some(prompt) = editing {
5379:                list = list.child(div().pl(px(40.)).child(Textarea::new(&prompt)));
5380:            }
5381:        }
5382:        if !sent {
5383:            let name = name.clone();
5384:            list = list.child(
5385:                Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-add-step-{name}")))
5386:                    .ghost()
5387:                    .xsmall()
5388:                    .icon(IconName::Plus)
5389:                    .label("Add step")
5390:                    .tooltip("Add a step after the last, done once the one before it is")
5391:                    .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| {
5392:                        this.add_step_here(&name, String::new(), window, cx)
5393:                    })),
5394:            );
5395:        }
5396:        if let Some((story, why, kept)) = &self.step_error
5397:            && *story == name
5398:        {
5399:            list = list.child(
5400:                v_flex()
5401:                    .id(SharedString::from(format!("planning-step-error-{name}")))
5402:                    .gap_0p5()
5403:                    .child(div().text_xs().text_color(theme.danger).child(why.clone()))
5404:                    .when(!kept.trim().is_empty(), |error| {
5405:                        error.child(
5406:                            div()
5407:                                .text_xs()
5408:                                .text_color(muted)
5409:                                .child(format!("What was to be added: {kept}")),
5410:                        )
5411:                    }),
5412:            );
5413:        }
5414:        list.into_any_element()
5415:    }
5416:
5417:    /// A proposal's card: where it goes, its title, mode, and prompt, and
5418:    /// Accept, Edit, and Reject; edited in place while Edit has it open.
5419:    fn render_proposal(
5420:        &mut self,
5421:        proposal: &Proposal,
5422:        window: &mut Window,
5423:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
5424:    ) -> AnyElement {
5425:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
5426:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
5427:        let id = proposal.id.clone();
5428:        let focus = self.row_focus(&id, cx);
5429:        let focused = focus.is_focused(window);
5430:        let target = proposal.target.as_deref().and_then(|name| self.story(name));
5431:        let (label, label_color) = match target {
5432:            Some(story) => (
5433:                if proposal.about.as_deref() == Some(story.name.as_str()) {
5434:                    "Add to this story".to_string()
5435:                } else {
5436:                    format!("Add to {}", story.title)
5437:                },
5438:                chat_input::mode_color(story.mode(), cx),
5439:            ),
5440:            None => ("New story".to_string(), theme.accent),
5441:        };
5442:        // What it would add to: the story's own prompt and its steps.
5443:        let target_steps = target
5444:            .filter(|story| !story.steps().is_empty())
5445:            .map(|story| {
5446:                let mut lines = vec![format!(
5447:                    "1. {}",
5448:                    story.text().lines().next().unwrap_or_default()
5449:                )];
5450:                lines.extend(story.steps().iter().enumerate().map(|(at, step)| {
5451:                    format!(
5452:                        "{}. {}",
5453:                        at + 2,
5454:                        step.prompt.lines().next().unwrap_or_default()
5455:                    )
5456:                }));
5457:                lines.join("\n")
5458:            });
5459:        let editing = self.editing.as_ref().filter(|editor| editor.id == id);
5460:        let read_more = self.read_more.contains(&id);
5461:        let (shown, cut) = if read_more {
5462:            (proposal.prompt.clone(), false)
5463:        } else {
5464:            clipped(&proposal.prompt, PROPOSAL_LINES)
5465:        };
5466:        let refused = self.refused.get(&id).cloned();
5467:        // A proposal citing nothing, not even No match, isn't a story yet.
5468:        let ungrounded = proposal.spec.is_empty();
5469:        let accept = {
5470:            let id = id.clone();
5471:            Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-accept-{id}")))
5472:                .primary()
5473:                .xsmall()
5474:                .icon(IconName::Check)
5475:                .label("Accept")
5476:                .tooltip("Record it in the plan (A)")
5477:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| {
5478:                    if this.accept(&id, cx).is_ok() {
5479:                        this.focus_after_decision(&id, window, cx);
5480:                    }
5481:                }))
5482:        };
5483:        let edit = {
5484:            let id = id.clone();
5485:            Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-edit-{id}")))
5486:                .ghost()
5487:                .xsmall()
5488:                .icon(IconName::Pencil)
5489:                .label(if editing.is_some() { "Done" } else { "Edit" })
5490:                .tooltip("Change it before it is accepted (E)")
5491:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| {
5492:                    if this.editing.as_ref().is_some_and(|editor| editor.id == id) {
5493:                        this.editing = None;
5494:                        this.focus_row(&id, window, cx);
5495:                    } else {
5496:                        this.edit_proposal(&id, window, cx);
5497:                    }
5498:                }))
5499:        };
5500:        let reject = {
5501:            let id = id.clone();
5502:            Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-reject-{id}")))
5503:                .ghost()
5504:                .xsmall()
5505:                .icon(IconName::X)
5506:                .label("Reject")
5507:                .tooltip("Let it go (R)")
5508:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| {
5509:                    this.focus_after_decision(&id, window, cx);
5510:                    this.reject(&id, cx);
5511:                }))
5512:        };
5513:        let card = card_box(SharedString::from(format!("planning-proposal-{id}")), cx)
5514:            .key_context(PROPOSAL_CONTEXT)
5515:            .track_focus(&focus)
5516:            .aria_label(format!("Proposal: {}", proposal.story_title()))
5517:            .when(focused, |card| card.border_color(theme.ring))
5518:            .on_action(
5519:                cx.listener(|this, _: &PreviousRow, window, cx| this.step_rows(-1, window, cx)),
5520:            )
5521:            .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &NextRow, window, cx| this.step_rows(1, window, cx)))
5522:            .on_action(cx.listener({
5523:                let id = id.clone();
5524:                move |this, _: &AcceptProposal, window, cx| {
5525:                    if this.accept(&id, cx).is_ok() {
5526:                        this.focus_after_decision(&id, window, cx);
5527:                    }
5528:                }
5529:            }))
5530:            .on_action(cx.listener({
5531:                let id = id.clone();
5532:                move |this, _: &EditProposal, window, cx| this.edit_proposal(&id, window, cx)
5533:            }))
5534:            .on_action(cx.listener({
5535:                let id = id.clone();
5536:                move |this, _: &RejectProposal, window, cx| {
5537:                    this.focus_after_decision(&id, window, cx);
5538:                    this.reject(&id, cx);
5539:                }
5540:            }))
5541:            .child(
5542:                div()
5543:                    .text_xs()
5544:                    .font_medium()
5545:                    .text_color(label_color)
5546:                    .child(label),
5547:            )
5548:            .children(
5549:                proposal
5550:                    .note
5551:                    .clone()
5552:                    .map(|note| div().text_xs().text_color(muted).child(note)),
5553:            )
5554:            .children(target_steps.map(|steps| {
5555:                div()
5556:                    .text_xs()
5557:                    .text_color(muted)
5558:                    .whitespace_normal()
5559:                    .child(steps)
5560:            }))
5561:            .child(
5562:                h_flex()
5563:                    .gap_2()
5564:                    .items_center()
5565:                    .child(mode_pill_label(proposal.mode(), cx))
5566:                    .child(
5567:                        div()
5568:                            .flex_1()
5569:                            .min_w_0()
5570:                            .truncate()
5571:                            .text_sm()
5572:                            .font_medium()
5573:                            .child(proposal.story_title()),
5574:                    ),
5575:            )
5576:            .when(editing.is_none(), |card| {
5577:                card.child(
5578:                    div()
5579:                        .text_sm()
5580:                        .text_color(theme.foreground)
5581:                        .whitespace_normal()
5582:                        .child(shown),
5583:                )
5584:                .when(cut || read_more, |card| {
5585:                    let id = id.clone();
5586:                    card.child(
5587:                        Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-read-more-{id}")))
5588:                            .ghost()
5589:                            .xsmall()
5590:                            .label(if read_more { "Show less" } else { "Read more" })
5591:                            .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, _, cx| {
5592:                                if !this.read_more.remove(&id) {
5593:                                    this.read_more.insert(id.clone());
5594:                                }
5595:                                cx.notify();
5596:                            })),
5597:                    )
5598:                })
5599:            })
5600:            .child(self.render_citations(&id, &proposal.spec, None, cx))
5601:            .children(
5602:                proposal
5603:                    .dropped
5604:                    .iter()
5605:                    .map(|why| div().text_xs().text_color(muted).child(why.clone())),
5606:            )
5607:            .children(refused.map(|why| div().text_xs().text_color(theme.danger).child(why)))
5608:            .when(ungrounded, |card| {
5609:                card.child(
5610:                    div()
5611:                        .text_xs()
5612:                        .text_color(theme.warning)
5613:                        .child("The drafter cited no spec, so this isn't offered as a story."),
5614:                )
5615:            })
5616:            .child(
5617:                h_flex()
5618:                    .gap_1()
5619:                    .justify_end()
5620:                    .when(!ungrounded, |foot| foot.child(accept).child(edit))
5621:                    .when(ungrounded, |foot| {
5622:                        let id = id.clone();
5623:                        foot.child(
5624:                            Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-draft-again-{id}")))
5625:                                .primary()
5626:                                .xsmall()
5627:                                .icon(IconName::Sparkles)
5628:                                .label("Draft again")
5629:                                .tooltip(
5630:                                    "Run the draft for this story once more, grounded in the spec",
5631:                                )
5632:                                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| {
5633:                                    this.draft_again(&id, window, cx)
5634:                                })),
5635:                        )
5636:                    })
5637:                    .child(reject),
5638:            );
5639:        // The editor beside the card rather than in it, so typing never
5640:        // reaches the card's own keys.
5641:        let editor = editing.map(|editor| {
5642:            let (title, prompt) = (editor.title.clone(), editor.prompt.clone());
5643:            let this = cx.entity().downgrade();
5644:            let mode = proposal.mode();
5645:            let mode_picker = {
5646:                let (this, id) = (this.clone(), id.clone());
5647:                Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-proposal-mode-{id}")))
5648:                    .ghost()
5649:                    .xsmall()
5650:                    .child(mode_pill_label(mode, cx))
5651:                    .tooltip("How it will be sent")
5652:                    .dropdown_menu_with_anchor(gpui_kit::Anchor::TopLeft, move |mut menu, _, _| {
5653:                        for choice in SendMode::ALL {
5654:                            let (this, id) = (this.clone(), id.clone());
5655:                            menu = menu.item(
5656:                                PopupMenuItem::new(mode_label(choice))
5657:                                    .checked(choice == mode)
5658:                                    .on_click(move |_, _, cx| {
5659:                                        this.update(cx, |this, cx| {
5660:                                            this.set_proposal(
5661:                                                &id,
5662:                                                |proposal| proposal.mode = mode_slug(choice).into(),
5663:                                                cx,
5664:                                            )
5665:                                        })
5666:                                        .ok();
5667:                                    }),
5668:                            );
5669:                        }
5670:                        menu
5671:                    })
5672:            };
5673:            let targets = self.targets(proposal.about.as_deref());
5674:            let current = proposal.target.clone();
5675:            let target_picker = {
5676:                let (this, id) = (this.clone(), id.clone());
5677:                Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-proposal-target-{id}")))
5678:                    .ghost()
5679:                    .xsmall()
5680:                    .label(
5681:                        current
5682:                            .as_deref()
5683:                            .and_then(|name| targets.iter().find(|(target, _)| target == name))
5684:                            .map_or("New story".to_string(), |(name, title)| {
5685:                                if proposal.about.as_ref() == Some(name) {
5686:                                    "Add to this story".to_string()
5687:                                } else {
5688:                                    format!("Add to {title}")
5689:                                }
5690:                            }),
5691:                    )
5692:                    .tooltip("Where it goes")
5693:                    .dropdown_menu_with_anchor(gpui_kit::Anchor::TopLeft, move |mut menu, _, _| {
5694:                        let choices = std::iter::once((None, "New story".to_string())).chain(
5695:                            targets
5696:                                .iter()
5697:                                .map(|(name, title)| (Some(name.clone()), title.clone())),
5698:                        );
5699:                        for (target, label) in choices {
5700:                            let (this, id) = (this.clone(), id.clone());
5701:                            let checked = target == current;
5702:                            menu = menu.item(PopupMenuItem::new(label).checked(checked).on_click(
5703:                                move |_, _, cx| {
5704:                                    let target = target.clone();
5705:                                    this.update(cx, |this, cx| {
5706:                                        this.set_proposal(
5707:                                            &id,
5708:                                            |proposal| proposal.target = target,
5709:                                            cx,
5710:                                        )
5711:                                    })
5712:                                    .ok();
5713:                                },
5714:                            ));
5715:                        }
5716:                        menu
5717:                    })
5718:            };
5719:            let label = |text: &'static str| div().text_xs().text_color(muted).child(text);
5720:            v_flex()
5721:                .gap_2()
5722:                .p_2()
5723:                .rounded(theme.radius)
5724:                .bg(theme.background)
5725:                .child(label("Title"))
5726:                .child(Input::new(&title))
5727:                .child(label("Prompt"))
5728:                .child(Textarea::new(&prompt))
5729:                .child(
5730:                    h_flex()
5731:                        .gap_1()
5732:                        .items_center()
5733:                        .child(mode_picker)
5734:                        .child(target_picker),
5735:                )
5736:        });
5737:        gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
5738:            v_flex()
5739:                .id(SharedString::from(format!("planning-proposal-block-{id}")))
5740:                .w_full()
5741:                .gap_1()
5742:                .child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(card))
5743:                .children(editor),
5744:        )
5745:        .into_any_element()
5746:    }
5747:
5748:    /// The Findings list, newest first: each a row with its topic, when it
5749:    /// ran, and its state, opening its findings in a body tab, deleted from
5750:    /// its menu; only those started from `story` where one is given. None
5751:    /// while there are none.
5752:    /// The story `name`'s steps, a row each, in order, indented beneath it,
5753:    /// each with its own status once the story is sent; none while it has
5754:    /// none. Listed at `collapse`, they show only their count until it is
5755:    /// expanded.
5756:    fn render_story_steps(
5757:        &self,
5758:        name: &str,
5759:        collapse: Option<String>,
5760:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
5761:    ) -> Option<AnyElement> {
5762:        let story = self.story(name)?;
5763:        if story.steps().is_empty() {
5764:            return None;
5765:        }
5766:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
5767:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
5768:        let task = story.task.clone();
5769:        let count = story.steps().len() + 1;
5770:        let current = collapse.is_none();
5771:        let expanded = collapse
5772:            .as_ref()
5773:            .is_none_or(|key| self.steps_expanded.contains(key));
5774:        let toggle = collapse.map(|key| {
5775:            let id = SharedString::from(format!("planning-step-list-{key}"));
5776:            h_flex()
5777:                .id(id)
5778:                .h(px(24.))
5779:                .px_3()
5780:                .gap_1()
5781:                .items_center()
5782:                .rounded(theme.radius)
5783:                .cursor_pointer()
5784:                .text_xs()
5785:                .text_color(muted)
5786:                .hover(|row| row.bg(theme.list_hover))
5787:                .aria_label(if expanded { "Hide steps" } else { "Show steps" })
5788:                .on_click(cx.listener(move |this, _, _, cx| {
5789:                    if !this.steps_expanded.remove(&key) {
5790:                        this.steps_expanded.insert(key.clone());
5791:                    }
5792:                    cx.notify();
5793:                }))
5794:                .child(
5795:                    Icon::new(if expanded {
5796:                        IconName::ChevronDown
5797:                    } else {
5798:                        IconName::ChevronRight
5799:                    })
5800:                    .xsmall(),
5801:                )
5802:                .child(format!("{count} steps"))
5803:        });
5804:        let rows = story
5805:            .steps()
5806:            .iter()
5807:            .enumerate()
5808:            .filter(|_| expanded)
5809:            .map(|(at, step)| {
5810:                // Not started yet, a step is still to come, unless its
5811:                // story's task is missing, so it never will.
5812:                let status = task.as_deref().map(|task| {
5813:                    (self.hooks.step_state)(task, at, cx).unwrap_or((
5814:                        if missing {
5815:                            StoryState::Missing
5816:                        } else {
5817:                            StoryState::Queued
5818:                        },
5819:                        None,
5820:                    ))
5821:                });
5822:                gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
5823:                    h_flex()
5824:                        .id(SharedString::from(format!(
5825:                            "planning-sent-step-{name}-{at}"
5826:                        )))
5827:                        .w_full()
5828:                        .h(ROW_HEIGHT)
5829:                        .flex_none()
5830:                        .px_3()
5831:                        .gap_2()
5832:                        .items_center()
5833:                        .child(div().w(GRIP_WIDTH).flex_none())
5834:                        .child(
5835:                            div()
5836:                                .w(PILL_WIDTH)
5837:                                .flex_none()
5838:                                .child(mode_pill_label(step.mode, cx)),
5839:                        )
5840:                        .child(
5841:                            div()
5842:                                .flex_none()
5843:                                .text_xs()
5844:                                .text_color(muted)
5845:                                .child(format!("{}.", at + 2)),
5846:                        )
5847:                        .child(
5848:                            div()
5849:                                .flex_1()
5850:                                .min_w_0()
5851:                                .truncate()
5852:                                .text_sm()
5853:                                .text_color(muted)
5854:                                .child(step.prompt.lines().next().unwrap_or_default().to_string()),
5855:                        )
5856:                        .child(
5857:                            div()
5858:                                .w(STATUS_WIDTH)
5859:                                .flex_none()
5860:                                .flex()
5861:                                .justify_end()
5862:                                .children(self.status_shown(status, current, cx)),
5863:                        ),
5864:                )
5865:            })
5866:            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
5867:        Some(
5868:            v_flex()
5869:                .w_full()
5870:                .pl(px(16.))
5871:                .gap(ROW_GAP)
5872:                .children(toggle)
5873:                .children(rows)
5874:                .into_any_element(),
5875:        )
5876:    }
5877:
5878:    fn render_findings(
5879:        &self,
5880:        story: Option<&str>,
5881:        window: &mut Window,
5882:        cx: &mut Context<Self>,
5883:    ) -> Option<AnyElement> {
5884:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
5885:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
5886:        let now = crate::divergence::now();
5887:        let findings: Vec<Finding> = self
5888:            .notes
5889:            .findings
5890:            .iter()
5891:            .rev()
5892:            .filter(|finding| story.is_none_or(|story| finding.story.as_deref() == Some(story)))
5893:            .cloned()
5894:            .collect();
5895:        // In a story, nothing is listed while it has none; beneath the
5896:        // Backlog, the section says so.
5897:        if findings.is_empty() && story.is_some() {
5898:            return None;
5899:        }
5900:        let rows = findings.into_iter().map(|finding| {
5901:            let file = finding.file.clone();
5902:            let state: AnyElement = match finding.state {
5903:                FindingState::Done => crate::theme::tag(crate::theme::Hue::Green, cx)
5904:                    .text_xs()
5905:                    .child(finding.state.label())
5906:                    .into_any_element(),
5907:                FindingState::Failed => crate::theme::tag(crate::theme::Hue::Red, cx)
5908:                    .text_xs()
5909:                    .child(finding.state.label())
5910:                    .into_any_element(),
5911:                // Ended early: muted, never a coloured state.
5912:                FindingState::Stopped => div()
5913:                    .text_xs()
5914:                    .text_color(muted)
5915:                    .child(finding.state.label())
5916:                    .into_any_element(),
5917:            };
5918:            let focus = self
5919:                .row_focus
5920:                .get(&format!("finding:{file}"))
5921:                .cloned()
5922:                .unwrap_or_else(|| self.focus.clone());
5923:            let focused = focus.is_focused(window);
5924:            let this = cx.entity().downgrade();
5925:            let menu_file = file.clone();
5926:            let menu = Button::new(SharedString::from(format!("planning-findings-menu-{file}")))
5927:                .ghost()
5928:                .xsmall()
5929:                .icon(IconName::Ellipsis)
5930:                .label("More")
5931:                .tooltip("More")
5932:                .dropdown_menu_with_anchor(gpui_kit::Anchor::TopRight, move |menu, _, _| {
5933:                    let (this, file) = (this.clone(), menu_file.clone());
5934:                    menu.item(
5935:                        PopupMenuItem::new("Delete findings").on_click(move |_, _, cx| {
5936:                            this.update(cx, |this, cx| this.delete_findings(&file, cx))
5937:                                .ok();
5938:                        }),
5939:                    )
5940:                });
5941:            let open_file = file.clone();
5942:            let row = h_flex()
5943:                .id(SharedString::from(format!("planning-findings-{file}")))
5944:                .key_context(ROW_CONTEXT)
5945:                .track_focus(&focus)
5946:                .on_action(cx.listener(move |this, _: &ToggleStory, window, cx| {
5947:                    this.open_findings(&open_file, window, cx)
5948:                }))
5949:                .h(ROW_HEIGHT)
5950:                .px_3()
5951:                .gap_2()
5952:                .items_center()
5953:                .rounded(theme.radius)
5954:                .border_1()
5955:                .border_color(if focused {
5956:                    theme.ring
5957:                } else {
5958:                    gpui_kit::transparent_black()
5959:                })
5960:                .cursor_pointer()
5961:                .hover(|row| row.bg(theme.list_hover))
5962:                .aria_label(format!("Research: {}", finding.topic))
5963:                .on_click(
5964:                    cx.listener(move |this, _, window, cx| this.open_findings(&file, window, cx)),
5965:                )
5966:                .child(Icon::new(IconName::Search).xsmall().text_color(muted))
5967:                .child(
5968:                    div()
5969:                        .flex_1()
5970:                        .min_w_0()
5971:                        .truncate()
5972:                        .text_sm()
5973:                        .child(format!("Research: {}", finding.topic)),
5974:                )
5975:                .child(
5976:                    div()
5977:                        .text_xs()
5978:                        .text_color(muted)
5979:                        .child(crate::divergence::ago(finding.ran_at, now)),
5980:                )
5981:                .child(
5982:                    div()
5983:                        .w(STATUS_WIDTH)
5984:                        .flex_none()
5985:                        .flex()
5986:                        .justify_end()
5987:                        .child(state),
5988:                )
5989:                .child(own_clicks(div().flex_none()).child(menu));
5990:            // The steps of the story it was started from, collapsed.
5991:            let steps = finding.story.as_deref().and_then(|of| {
5992:                self.render_story_steps(of, Some(format!("finding:{}", finding.file)), cx)
5993:            });
5994:            v_flex()
5995:                .w_full()
5996:                .gap(ROW_GAP)
5997:                .child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(row))
5998:                .children(steps)
5999:        });
6000:        Some(
6001:            v_flex()
6002:                .w_full()
6003:                .gap(ROW_GAP)
6004:                .when(story.is_none(), |list| {
6005:                    list.track_focus(&self.section_focus["findings"])
6006:                })
6007:                .child(
6008:                    div()
6009:                        .pb(HEADING_GAP - ROW_GAP)
6010:                        .child(
6011:                            h_flex()
6012:                                .h(HEADING_HEIGHT)
6013:                                .gap_2()
6014:                                .items_center()
6015:                                .child(div().text_sm().font_semibold().child("Findings"))
6016:                                .child(
6017:                                    div()
6018:                                        .text_xs()
6019:                                        .text_color(muted)
6020:                                        .child(self.notes.findings.len().to_string()),
6021:                                ),
6022:                        )
6023:                        .when(story.is_some(), |heading| heading.pb_0()),
6024:                )
6025:                .children(rows)
6026:                .when(self.notes.findings.is_empty(), |list| {
6027:                    list.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
6028:                        div()
6029:                            .id("planning-empty-findings")
6030:                            .child(empty_line("No findings yet.".into(), muted)),
6031:                    ))
6032:                })
6033:                .into_any_element(),
6034:        )
6035:    }

6037:    /// The bar across the top of Planning while it is paused for review:

6133:    /// The drafting panel: the request, the run while it goes, its error,
6134:    /// the proposals, the notes of those decided, and the Undos of those
6135:    /// rejected.
6136:    fn render_draft_panel(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> AnyElement {
6137:        let theme = cx.theme().clone();
6138:        let muted = theme.muted_foreground;
6139:        let Some(drafting) = &self.drafting else {
6140:            return div().into_any_element();
6141:        };
6142:        let running = drafting.running;
6143:        let activity = activity_of(&drafting.activity).clone();
6144:        let error = drafting.error.clone();
6145:        let details_open = drafting.details_open;
6146:        let proposed_nothing = drafting.proposed_nothing;
6147:        let input = drafting.input.clone();
6148:        let about = drafting.about.is_some();
6149:        let research = drafting.research;
6150:        let count = self.proposals.len();
6151:        // It closes once nothing in it is left to decide or run.
6152:        let closable = running.is_none() && count == 0 && self.rejected.is_empty();
6153:        let name = if research {
6154:            "Research"
6155:        } else {
6156:            "Draft stories"
6157:        };
6158:        let heading = panel_heading(
6159:            name.into(),
6160:            closable.then(|| {
6161:                Button::new("planning-draft-close")
6162:                    .ghost()
6163:                    .xsmall()
6164:                    .label("Close")
6165:                    .tooltip("Close this panel (Escape)")
6166:                    .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, window, cx| {
6167:                        this.drafting = None;
6168:                        this.focus.focus(window, cx);
6169:                        cx.notify();
6170:                    }))
6171:                    .into_any_element()
6172:            }),
6173:        );
6174:        let mut content = v_flex()
6175:            .gap_2()
6176:            .child(div().text_xs().text_color(muted).child(if research {
6177:                "What should be looked into?"
6178:            } else if about {
6179:                "What does this story need?"
6180:            } else {
6181:                "Describe what you want"
6182:            }))
6183:            .child(Textarea::new(&input).disabled(running.is_some()));
6184:        // Loading: what the run is doing, where its result will appear.
6185:        if let Some(started) = running {
6186:            let elapsed = crate::subagents::format_elapsed(started.elapsed());
6187:            content = content.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
6188:                h_flex()
6189:                    .id("planning-draft-running")
6190:                    .gap_2()
6191:                    .items_center()
6192:                    .child(loading_line(format!("{activity} {elapsed}"), muted).flex_1())
6193:                    .child(
6194:                        Button::new("planning-draft-stop")
6195:                            .ghost()
6196:                            .small()
6197:                            .icon(IconName::Square)
6198:                            .label("Stop")
6199:                            .tooltip(if research {
6200:                                "Stop the research; its findings say it ended early"
6201:                            } else {
6202:                                "Stop the agent, proposing nothing"
6203:                            })
6204:                            .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| this.stop_draft(cx))),
6205:                    ),
6206:            ));
6207:        }
6208:        // Error: what went wrong, in plain words, its details collapsed, and
6209:        // Try again, the request kept in its box.
6210:        if let Some(error) = error {
6211:            content = content.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
6212:                v_flex()
6213:                    .id("planning-draft-error")
6214:                    .gap_1()
6215:                    .child(
6216:                        h_flex()
6217:                            .gap_2()
6218:                            .items_center()
6219:                            .child(div().flex_1().text_sm().text_color(theme.danger).child(
6220:                                if research {
6221:                                    "The research couldn't be done."
6222:                                } else {
6223:                                    "The agent couldn't propose stories from that."
6224:                                },
6225:                            ))
6226:                            .child(
6227:                                Button::new("planning-draft-details")
6228:                                    .ghost()
6229:                                    .xsmall()
6230:                                    .icon(if details_open {
6231:                                        IconName::ChevronDown
6232:                                    } else {
6233:                                        IconName::ChevronRight
6234:                                    })
6235:                                    .label("Details")
6236:                                    .tooltip(if details_open {
6237:                                        "Hide what was reported"
6238:                                    } else {
6239:                                        "Show what was reported"
6240:                                    })
6241:                                    .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| {
6242:                                        if let Some(drafting) = &mut this.drafting {
6243:                                            drafting.details_open = !drafting.details_open;
6244:                                        }
6245:                                        cx.notify();
6246:                                    })),
6247:                            )
6248:                            .child(
6249:                                Button::new("planning-draft-retry")
6250:                                    .ghost()
6251:                                    .xsmall()
6252:                                    .label("Try again")
6253:                                    .tooltip("Run it again with the same request")
6254:                                    .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| this.draft(cx))),
6255:                            ),
6256:                    )
6257:                    .when(details_open, |details| {
6258:                        details.child(
6259:                            div()
6260:                                .text_xs()
6261:                                .text_color(muted)
6262:                                .font_family(theme.mono_font_family.clone())
6263:                                .child(error.trim().to_string()),
6264:                        )
6265:                    }),
6266:            ));
6267:        }
6268:        // Empty: nothing proposed, with Draft again.
6269:        if proposed_nothing && count == 0 && running.is_none() {
6270:            content = content.child(gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
6271:                h_flex()
6272:                    .id("planning-proposed-nothing")
6273:                    .w_full()
6274:                    .h(ROW_HEIGHT)
6275:                    .gap_2()
6276:                    .items_center()
6277:                    .justify_center()
6278:                    .text_sm()
6279:                    .text_color(muted)
6280:                    .child("No stories proposed.")
6281:                    .child(
6282:                        Button::new("planning-draft-again")
6283:                            .ghost()
6284:                            .xsmall()
6285:                            .label("Draft again")
6286:                            .tooltip("Run the draft again with the same request")
6287:                            .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| this.draft(cx))),
6288:                    ),
6289:            ));
6290:        }
6291:        // The notes of those decided.
6292:        for (at, decided) in self.decided.iter().enumerate() {
6293:            let story = decided.story.clone();
6294:            content = content.child(
6295:                h_flex()
6296:                    .id(("planning-decided", at))
6297:                    .gap_1()
6298:                    .text_xs()
