6537:impl Render for Planning {
6538:    fn render(&mut self, window: &mut Window, cx: &mut Context<Self>) -> impl IntoElement {
6539:        if !cx.has_active_drag() {
6540:            self.gap.set(None);
6541:        }
6542:        if let Some(open) = &mut self.open {
6543:            open.fit.keep_cursor_in_view(&open.prompt, window, cx);
6544:        }
6545:        if self.any_running(cx) {
6546:            self.keep_ticking(cx);
6547:        }
6548:        let files: Vec<String> = self
6549:            .notes
6550:            .findings
6551:            .iter()
6552:            .map(|finding| finding.file.clone())
6553:            .collect();
6554:        for file in files {
6555:            self.row_focus(&format!("finding:{file}"), cx);
6556:        }
6557:        let save_error = self.save_error.lock().unwrap().clone().map(|why| {
6558:            gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(
6559:                h_flex()
6560:                    .id("planning-save-error")
6561:                    .gap_2()
6562:                    .items_center()
6563:                    .child(
6564:                        div()
6565:                            .flex_1()
6566:                            .text_sm()
6567:                            .text_color(cx.theme().danger)
6568:                            .child(why),
6569:                    )
6570:                    .child(
6571:                        Button::new("planning-save-retry")
6572:                            .ghost()
6573:                            .xsmall()
6574:                            .label("Try again")
6575:                            .tooltip("Save the plan again")
6576:                            .on_click(cx.listener(|this, _, _, cx| {
6577:                                *this.save_error.lock().unwrap() = None;
6578:                                this.save_order(cx);
6579:                                let names: Vec<String> = this
6580:                                    .sections
6581:                                    .iter()
6582:                                    .flatten()
6583:                                    .map(|story| story.name.clone())
6584:                                    .collect();
6585:                                for name in names {
6586:                                    this.save_story_named(&name, cx);
6587:                                }
6588:                                cx.notify();
6589:                            })),
6590:                    ),
6591:            )
6592:        });
6593:        let pause = self.render_pause_bar(cx);
6594:        let current = (!self.sections[3].is_empty())
6595:            .then(|| self.render_section(Section::Current, window, cx));
6596:        let next = self.render_section(Section::Next, window, cx);
6597:        let backlog = self.render_section(Section::Backlog, window, cx);
6598:        let findings = self.render_findings(None, window, cx);
6599:        let done =
6600:            (!self.sections[2].is_empty()).then(|| self.render_section(Section::Done, window, cx));
6601:        // 24 pixels between sections, and around the column's top and sides.
6602:        let column = v_flex()
6603:            .w_full()
6604:            .max_w(COLUMN_WIDTH)
6605:            .gap_6()
6606:            .pt_6()
6607:            .px_6()
6608:            .pb_6()
6609:            .children(save_error)
6610:            .children(pause)
6611:            .children(current)
6612:            .child(next)
6613:            .child(backlog)
6614:            .children(findings)
6615:            .children(done);
6616:        let planning = div()
6617:            .id("planning")
6618:            .key_context(CONTEXT)
6619:            .track_focus(&self.focus)
6620:            .size_full()
6621:            .overflow_y_scroll()
6622:            .bg(cx.theme().background)
6623:            .on_action(
6624:                cx.listener(|this, _: &NewStory, window, cx| this.new_story_here(window, cx)),
6625:            )
6626:            .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &SendSprint, window, cx| this.send_sprint(window, cx)))
6627:            .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &OpenDraftStories, window, cx| {
6628:                this.open_draft_panel(None, true, window, cx)
6629:            }))
6630:            .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &OpenResearch, window, cx| {
6631:                this.open_research(None, window, cx)
6632:            }))
6633:            .on_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &NextRegion, window, cx| this.next_region(window, cx)))
6634:            .capture_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &Escape, window, cx| {
6635:                if this.escape(window, cx) {
6636:                    cx.stop_propagation();
6637:                }
6638:            }))
6639:            .on_action(
6640:                cx.listener(|this, _: &crate::main_window::Dismiss, window, cx| {
6641:                    if !this.escape(window, cx) {
6642:                        cx.propagate();
6643:                    }
6644:                }),
6645:            )
6646:            .capture_action(cx.listener(|this, action: &Enter, window, cx| {
6647:                // Enter accepts an open completion rather than adding a line;
6648:                // in the drafting panel, Ctrl/Cmd+Enter drafts.
6649:                if let Some(open) = &this.open
6650:                    && open.completion.read(cx).is_open()
6651:                    && !action.secondary
6652:                {
6653:                    cx.stop_propagation();
6654:                    open.completion
6655:                        .update(cx, |menu, cx| menu.accept_selected(window, cx));
6656:                } else if !action.secondary
6657:                    && this.open_step.as_ref().is_some_and(|step| {
6658:                        step.prompt.read(cx).focus_handle(cx).is_focused(window)
6659:                    })
6660:                {
6661:                    cx.stop_propagation();
6662:                    this.step_entered(window, cx);
6663:                } else if action.secondary
6664:                    && this.drafting.as_ref().is_some_and(|drafting| {
6665:                        drafting.input.read(cx).focus_handle(cx).is_focused(window)
6666:                    })
6667:                {
6668:                    cx.stop_propagation();
6669:                    this.draft(cx);
6670:                }
6671:            }))
6672:            .capture_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &MoveUp, window, cx| {
6673:                if let Some(open) = &this.open
6674:                    && open.completion.read(cx).is_open()
6675:                {
6676:                    cx.stop_propagation();
6677:                    open.completion
6678:                        .update(cx, |menu, cx| menu.select_next(-1, window, cx));
6679:                }
6680:            }))
6681:            .capture_action(cx.listener(|this, _: &MoveDown, window, cx| {
6682:                if let Some(open) = &this.open
6683:                    && open.completion.read(cx).is_open()
6684:                {
6685:                    cx.stop_propagation();
6686:                    open.completion
6687:                        .update(cx, |menu, cx| menu.select_next(1, window, cx));
6688:                }
6689:            }))
6690:            .child(h_flex().w_full().justify_center().child(column));
6691:        gpui_kit::TestSupportExt::test_support(planning)
6692:    }
6693:}

6751:    /// Planning, alone in a window, for the project at `dir`, with `hooks`.
6752:    fn open_planning_with(
6753:        dir: PathBuf,
6754:        hooks: PlanningHooks,
6755:        cx: &mut TestAppContext,
6756:    ) -> (Entity<Planning>, AnyWindowHandle) {
6757:        cx.update(|cx| {
6758:            gpui_kit::init(cx);
6759:            crate::piton_syntax::init();
6760:            super::bind_keys(cx);
6761:        });
6762:        let mut planning = None;
6763:        let window = cx.add_window(|window, cx| {
6764:            let view = cx.new(|cx| Planning::new(dir, hooks, cx));
6765:            planning = Some(view.clone());
6766:            Root::new(view, window, cx)
6767:        });
6768:        // The plan is read in the background.
6769:        cx.run_until_parked();
6770:        (planning.unwrap(), window.into())
6771:    }

6773:    /// A project whose Backlog holds stories titled `titles`, in order.
6774:    fn backlog(name: &str, titles: &[&str]) -> (PathBuf, Vec<String>) {
6775:        let dir = project(name);
6776:        let mut sections: [Vec<Story>; 4] = Default::default();
6777:        for title in titles {
6778:            let story = Story::new(title.to_string(), String::new(), SendMode::Both, &dir);
6779:            save_story(&dir, &story.name, &story.anchor.source(&story.prompt)).unwrap();
6780:            sections[1].push(story);
6781:        }
6782:        save_order_text(&dir, &order_of(&sections, &[], &Default::default())).unwrap();
6783:        let names = sections[1].iter().map(|story| story.name.clone()).collect();
6784:        (dir, names)
6785:    }

6791:    ) -> Vec<String> {

6795:                .iter()
6796:                .map(|story| story.title.clone())
6797:                .collect()
6798:        })

6801:    /// Stories, their order, which section each is in, their marks, and
6802:    /// the proposals waiting come back as they were saved.
6803:    #[test]
6804:    fn the_plan_comes_back_as_it_was_left() {
6805:        let dir = project("planning-save-test");
6806:        let mut sections: [Vec<Story>; 4] = Default::default();
6807:        let mut first = Story::new(
6808:            "First".into(),
6809:            "Do the first thing".into(),
6810:            SendMode::Code,
6811:            &dir,
6812:        );
6813:        first.anchor.sliced = true;
6814:        let mut second = Story::new("Second".into(), String::new(), SendMode::Ask, &dir);
6815:        second.from_agent = true;
6816:        let mut third = Story::new("Third".into(), "Third".into(), SendMode::Spec, &dir);
6817:        third.task = Some("ThirdTask".into());
6818:        for story in [&first, &second, &third] {
6819:            save_story(&dir, &story.name, &story.anchor.source(&story.prompt)).unwrap();
6820:        }
6821:        sections[0].push(first);
6822:        sections[1].push(second);
6823:        sections[2].push(third);
6824:        let proposal =
6825:            parse_story_block("title: Later\nmode: spec\ntarget: new\n\nLater.").unwrap();
6826:        save_order_text(
6827:            &dir,
6828:            &order_of(
6829:                &sections,
6830:                std::slice::from_ref(&proposal),
6831:                &Default::default(),
6832:            ),
6833:        )
6834:        .unwrap();
6835:
6836:        let (loaded, proposals, _) = load(&dir);
6837:        let titles: Vec<Vec<&str>> = loaded
6838:            .iter()
6839:            .map(|stories| stories.iter().map(|story| story.title.as_str()).collect())
6840:            .collect();
6841:        assert_eq!(titles[..3], [["First"], ["Second"], ["Third"]]);
6842:        assert!(titles[3].is_empty());
6843:        assert_eq!(loaded[0][0].prompt, "Do the first thing");
6844:        assert_eq!(loaded[0][0].mode(), SendMode::Code);
6845:        assert!(loaded[0][0].anchor.sliced);
6846:        assert!(loaded[1][0].from_agent);
6847:        // A title with no prompt sends its title.
6848:        assert_eq!(loaded[1][0].text(), "Second");
6849:        assert_eq!(loaded[2][0].task.as_deref(), Some("ThirdTask"));
6850:        assert_eq!(proposals, [proposal]);
6851:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
6852:    }
6853:
6854:    /// Proposals are read from `suspense-story` blocks wherever they stand
6855:    /// in a reply, each with its title, mode, target, and prompt.
6856:    #[test]
6857:    fn proposals_are_read_from_story_blocks() {
6858:        let reply = "I read the queue.\n\n```suspense-story\ntitle: Add sprints\nmode: chain\ntarget: new\n\nStories can be gathered\ninto a sprint.\n```\n\nAnd:\n\n```suspense-story\ntitle: Test it\nmode: code\ntarget: Story_1\n\nCover the sprint.\n```\n";
6859:        let proposals = parse_proposals(reply);
6860:        assert_eq!(proposals.len(), 2);
6861:        assert_eq!(proposals[0].title, "Add sprints");
6862:        assert_eq!(proposals[0].mode(), SendMode::Both);
6863:        assert_eq!(proposals[0].target, None);
6864:        assert_eq!(
6865:            proposals[0].prompt,
6866:            "Stories can be gathered\ninto a sprint."
6867:        );
6868:        assert_eq!(proposals[1].mode(), SendMode::Code);
6869:        assert_eq!(proposals[1].target.as_deref(), Some("Story_1"));
6870:        assert!(parse_proposals("Nothing to propose.").is_empty());
6871:    }

6908:    /// From the keyboard: Down moves to the next row, Alt+Up moves a story
6909:    /// up and on into Next sprint, M opens the mode picker, picked from with
6910:    /// the arrows and Enter, and Delete asks before Enter deletes.
6911:    #[gpui_kit::test]
6912:    async fn stories_are_worked_from_the_keyboard(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
6913:        let (dir, names) = backlog("planning-keys-test", &["One", "Two", "Three"]);
6914:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), unused, cx);
6915:        let press = |key: &str, cx: &mut TestAppContext| {
6916:            cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
6917:                window.render_frame(cx);
6918:                window.press(key, cx);
6919:                window.render_frame(cx);
6920:            })
6921:            .unwrap();
6922:            cx.run_until_parked();
6923:        };
6924:        let focused = |cx: &mut TestAppContext| {
6925:            cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
6926:                planning.read(cx).focused_row(window)
6927:            })
6928:            .unwrap()
6929:        };
6930:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
6931:            window.render_frame(cx);
6932:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.focus_row(&names[0], window, cx));
6933:        })
6934:        .unwrap();
6935:        press("down", cx);
6936:        assert_eq!(focused(cx).as_deref(), Some(names[1].as_str()));
6937:
6938:        press("alt-up", cx);
6939:        assert_eq!(
6940:            titles(&planning, Section::Backlog, cx),
6941:            ["Two", "One", "Three"]
6942:        );
6943:        press("alt-up", cx);
6944:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx), ["Two"]);
6945:        assert_eq!(focused(cx).as_deref(), Some(names[1].as_str()));
6946:
6947:        // Ctrl+Enter moves it to the Backlog, and back: never sends it.
6948:        press("ctrl-enter", cx);
6949:        assert!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx).is_empty());
6950:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Backlog, cx)[0], "Two");
6951:        press("ctrl-enter", cx);
6952:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx), ["Two"]);
6953:        assert!(titles(&planning, Section::Current, cx).is_empty());
6954:
6955:        let mode = |cx: &mut TestAppContext| {
6956:            planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| planning.story(&names[1]).unwrap().mode())
6957:        };
6958:        let before = mode(cx);
6959:        press("m", cx);
6960:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, _| {
6961:            window.find(SharedString::from(format!(
6962:                "planning-mode-menu-{}",
6963:                names[1]
6964:            )));
6965:        })
6966:        .unwrap();
6967:        press("down", cx);
6968:        press("enter", cx);
6969:        assert_ne!(mode(cx), before, "the mode picked wasn't set");
6970:        assert!(planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| planning.mode_menu.is_none()));
6971:
6972:        press("delete", cx);
6973:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, _| {
6974:            window.find(SharedString::from(format!("planning-delete-{}", names[1])));
6975:        })
6976:        .unwrap();
6977:        assert_eq!(
6978:            titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx),
6979:            ["Two"],
6980:            "deleted unasked"
6981:        );
6982:        press("enter", cx);
6983:        assert!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx).is_empty());
6984:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
6985:    }

6987:    /// The story the drafting test's harness is asked to add to.
6988:    static TARGET: std::sync::Mutex<String> = std::sync::Mutex::new(String::new());
6989:
6990:    /// A spec file in the project at `dir`, under the spec location, that
6991:    /// declares Planning and its sprint.
6992:    fn reference(dir: &Path) {
6993:        let page = dir.join("spec/scope/planning");
6994:        std::fs::create_dir_all(&page).unwrap();
6995:        std::fs::write(
6996:            page.join("index.pi"),
6997:            "// The plan.\nexport scope Planning:\n    sprint:\n        Sent in order.\n        note: not a property\n    layout: Rows\n",
6998:        )
6999:        .unwrap();
7000:    }
7001:
7002:    /// A spec list's entries are read as links with what they touch, or as
7003:    /// No match with what was looked for; a link counts only where its
7004:    /// heading exists in the compiled reference.
7005:    #[test]
7006:    fn citations_are_read_and_checked() {
7007:        let dir = project("planning-cite-test");
7008:        reference(&dir);
7009:        let block = "title: Sprint\nmode: chain\ntarget: new\nspec:\n- Behaviour spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint — how it is sent — \"Sent in order.\"\n- [Planning](spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning)\n- No match — a calendar\n\nDo it.";
7010:        let proposal = parse_story_block(block).unwrap();
7011:        assert_eq!(proposal.prompt, "Do it.");
7012:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec.len(), 3);
7013:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec[0].note, "how it is sent");
7014:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec[0].kind, Some(CitationKind::Behaviour));
7015:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec[0].quote, "Sent in order.");
7016:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec[0].label(), "Planning.sprint");
7017:        assert_eq!(
7018:            proposal.spec[0].file(),
7019:            Some("spec/scope/planning/index.pi")
7020:        );
7021:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec[1].kind, None);
7022:        assert_eq!(
7023:            proposal.spec[1].link.as_deref(),
7024:            Some("spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning")
7025:        );
7026:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec[2].link, None);
7027:        assert_eq!(proposal.spec[2].note, "a calendar");
7028:        // Checked against the Piton source, found at its declaration.
7029:        assert_eq!(
7030:            super::check_link("spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint", &dir),
7031:            Ok(Some(2))
7032:        );
7033:        assert_eq!(
7034:            super::check_link("spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning", &dir),
7035:            Ok(Some(1))
7036:        );
7037:        assert_eq!(
7038:            super::check_link("spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.layout", &dir),
7039:            Ok(Some(5))
7040:        );
7041:        // A property's text is no property of it.
7042:        assert_eq!(
7043:            super::check_link("spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.note", &dir),
7044:            Err("the file doesn't define Planning.note".to_string())
7045:        );
7046:        assert_eq!(
7047:            super::check_link("spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.nowhere", &dir),
7048:            Err("the file doesn't define Planning.nowhere".to_string())
7049:        );
7050:        assert_eq!(
7051:            super::check_link("spec/scope/gone/index.pi#Gone.sprint", &dir),
7052:            Err("no such file".to_string())
7053:        );
7054:        // Never the compiled reference, nor outside the spec location.
7055:        assert!(
7056:            super::check_link(".claude/reference/scope/planning/index.md#sprint", &dir).is_err()
7057:        );
7058:        assert!(super::check_link("spec/../spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning", &dir).is_err());
7059:        // Add citation finds the spec's elements by name.
7060:        let elements = super::spec_elements(&dir);
7061:        let picks = super::citation_picks(&elements, "sprint");
7062:        assert_eq!(picks.len(), 1);
7063:        assert_eq!(picks[0].0, "spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint");
7064:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7065:    }

7067:    /// A proposal citing nothing, not even No match, isn't a story: it can't
7068:    /// be accepted, and its card offers Draft again.

7070:    async fn a_proposal_citing_no_spec_is_not_a_story(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {

7079:        });

7088:    fn two_proposals(
7089:        _: &Path,
7090:        request: &str,
7091:        _: &Cancel,
7092:        on_line: &dyn Fn(String),
7093:    ) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
7094:        on_line(r#"{"type":"assistant"}"#.into());
7095:        let target = TARGET.lock().unwrap().clone();
7096:        assert!(
7097:            request.contains(&target),
7098:            "the request doesn't name the stories"
7099:        );
7100:        Ok(format!(
7101:            "Here:\n\n```suspense-story\ntitle: First\nmode: code\ntarget: new\nspec:\n- spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint — how it is sent\n- spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.nowhere — made up\n\nDo the first\n```\n\n```suspense-story\ntitle: More\nmode: spec\ntarget: {target}\nspec:\n- No match — a calendar\n\nDescribe more\n```\n"
7102:        ))
7103:    }
7104:
7105:    /// The agent only proposes: nothing reaches the plan until accepted.
7106:    /// Proposals are kept with the project; accepting a new story puts it at
7107:    /// the Backlog's end from the agent, and an addition becomes a step of
7108:    /// the story it names, marked as updated; once none is left, the panel
7109:    /// closes.
7110:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7111:    async fn proposals_reach_the_plan_only_once_accepted(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7112:        let (dir, names) = backlog("planning-propose-test", &["Queue"]);
7113:        reference(&dir);
7114:        *TARGET.lock().unwrap() = names[0].clone();
7115:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), two_proposals, cx);
7116:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7117:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7118:                planning.open_draft_panel(None, true, window, cx);
7119:                let input = planning.drafting.as_ref().unwrap().input.clone();
7120:                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("Two things", window, cx));
7121:                planning.draft(cx);
7122:            });
7123:        })
7124:        .unwrap();
7125:        cx.run_until_parked();
7126:        let ids: Vec<String> = planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| {
7127:            planning
7128:                .proposals
7129:                .iter()
7130:                .map(|proposal| proposal.id.clone())
7131:                .collect()
7132:        });
7133:        assert_eq!(ids.len(), 2);
7134:        // Nothing written to the plan yet, only proposed, and kept.
7135:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Backlog, cx), ["Queue"]);
7136:        let (saved, proposals, _) = load(&dir);
7137:        assert_eq!(saved[1].len(), 1);
7138:        assert_eq!(proposals.len(), 2);
7139:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7140:            window.render_frame(cx);
7141:            window.find(SharedString::from(format!("planning-proposal-{}", ids[0])));
7142:            window.find("planning-accept-all");
7143:        })
7144:        .unwrap();
7145:
7146:        // A accepts the focused one; the other, an addition, too.
7147:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7148:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.focus_row(&ids[0], window, cx));
7149:            window.render_frame(cx);
7150:            window.press("a", cx);
7151:        })
7152:        .unwrap();
7153:        cx.run_until_parked();
7154:        // A new story goes to the end of the Backlog, never Next sprint.
7155:        assert!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx).is_empty());
7156:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Backlog, cx), ["Queue", "First"]);
7157:        planning
7158:            .update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.accept(&ids[1], cx))
7159:            .unwrap();
7160:        planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| {
7161:            let first = &planning.stories(Section::Backlog)[1];
7162:            let links: Vec<Option<&str>> = first
7163:                .spec
7164:                .iter()
7165:                .map(|citation| citation.link.as_deref())
7166:                .collect();
7167:            assert_eq!(
7168:                links,
7169:                [Some("spec/scope/planning/index.pi#Planning.sprint")]
7170:            );
7171:            assert!(first.from_agent);
7172:            assert_eq!(first.mode(), SendMode::Code);
7173:            let queue = &planning.stories(Section::Backlog)[0];
7174:            assert!(queue.updated);
7175:            assert_eq!(queue.steps().len(), 1);
7176:            assert_eq!(queue.steps()[0].prompt, "Describe more");
7177:            assert_eq!(queue.steps()[0].mode, SendMode::Spec);
7178:            assert!(planning.proposals.is_empty());
7179:            assert!(planning.drafting.is_none(), "the panel didn't close");
7180:        });
7181:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7182:    }
7183:
7184:    /// An addition to a story gone by the time it is accepted adds nothing:
7185:    /// the card stays, made a new story, saying why. A rejected proposal
7186:    /// can be brought back with Undo.
7187:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7188:    async fn additions_to_a_story_gone_are_refused_and_rejects_undone(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7189:        let dir = project("planning-refuse-test");
7190:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), unused, cx);
7191:        let proposal = Proposal {
7192:            target: Some("Story_gone".into()),
7193:            ..parse_story_block("title: More\nmode: code\nspec:\n- No match — more\n\nMore.")
7194:                .unwrap()
7195:        };
7196:        let id = proposal.id.clone();
7197:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7198:            planning.proposals.push(proposal);
7199:            assert!(planning.accept(&id, cx).is_err());
7200:            let kept = planning.proposal(&id).unwrap();
7201:            assert_eq!(kept.target, None);
7202:            assert!(planning.stories(Section::Backlog).is_empty());
7203:            planning.reject(&id, cx);
7204:            assert!(planning.proposals.is_empty());
7205:            planning.undo_reject(&id, cx);
7206:            assert_eq!(planning.proposals.len(), 1);
7207:        });
7208:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| window.render_frame(cx))
7209:            .unwrap();
7210:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7211:    }
7212:
7213:    /// Steps are added at the end of a story, in order, saved in its own
7214:    /// file, kept when the story moves and copied when it is duplicated; one
7215:    /// added to a story that doesn't exist, or whose file is gone, or that
7216:    /// was sent, changes nothing and says why.
7217:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7218:    async fn steps_are_kept_with_their_story(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7219:        let (dir, names) = backlog("planning-steps-test", &["Queue", "Gone"]);
7220:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), unused, cx);
7221:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7222:            assert_eq!(
7223:                planning.add_step(&names[0], SendMode::Code, "Two".into(), cx),
7224:                Ok(0)
7225:            );
7226:            assert_eq!(
7227:                planning.add_step(&names[0], SendMode::Spec, "Three".into(), cx),
7228:                Ok(1)
7229:            );
7230:            planning.move_step(&names[0], 1, 0, cx);
7231:            planning.move_to(&names[0], Section::Next, cx);
7232:            planning.duplicate(&names[0], cx);
7233:        });
7234:        cx.run_until_parked();
7235:        let (saved, _, _) = load(&dir);
7236:        let prompts: Vec<&str> = saved[0][0]
7237:            .steps()
7238:            .iter()
7239:            .map(|step| step.prompt.as_str())
7240:            .collect();
7241:        assert_eq!(prompts, ["Three", "Two"]);
7242:        assert_eq!(saved[0][0].steps()[0].mode, SendMode::Spec);
7243:        assert_eq!(saved[0][1].steps().len(), 2, "the duplicate lost its steps");
7244:
7245:        // A story whose file is gone, or that doesn't exist, takes nothing.
7246:        std::fs::remove_file(
7247:            dir.join(".suspense/planning")
7248:                .join(format!("{}.pi", names[1])),
7249:        )
7250:        .unwrap();
7251:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7252:            let gone = planning.add_step(&names[1], SendMode::Code, "More".into(), cx);
7253:            assert_eq!(gone, Err(super::NO_SUCH_STORY.to_string()));
7254:            assert!(planning.story(&names[1]).unwrap().steps().is_empty());
7255:            let missing = planning.add_step("Story_none", SendMode::Code, "More".into(), cx);
7256:            assert_eq!(missing, Err(super::NO_SUCH_STORY.to_string()));
7257:            planning.story_mut(&names[0]).unwrap().task = Some("Sent".into());
7258:            let sent = planning.add_step(&names[0], SendMode::Code, "More".into(), cx);
7259:            assert_eq!(sent, Err(super::STORY_SENT.to_string()));
7260:            assert_eq!(planning.story(&names[0]).unwrap().steps().len(), 2);
7261:        });
7262:        cx.run_until_parked();
7263:        assert!(
7264:            !dir.join(".suspense/planning")
7265:                .join(format!("{}.pi", names[1]))
7266:                .exists()
7267:        );
7268:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| window.render_frame(cx))
7269:            .unwrap();
7270:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7271:    }
7272:
7273:    /// Add step opens one at the end, its prompt focused; Enter in it once
7274:    /// it holds something starts another, and Enter in an empty one adds
7275:    /// none.
7276:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7277:    async fn steps_are_added_one_after_another(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7278:        let (dir, names) = backlog("planning-add-steps-test", &["Queue"]);
7279:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), unused, cx);
7280:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7281:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7282:                planning.open_story(&names[0], window, cx)
7283:            });
7284:            window.render_frame(cx);
7285:            window.click(
7286:                SharedString::from(format!("planning-add-step-{}", names[0])),
7287:                cx,
7288:            );
7289:            window.render_frame(cx);
7290:            let prompt = planning.read(cx).open_step.as_ref().unwrap().prompt.clone();
7291:            prompt.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("First step", window, cx));
7292:            window.press("enter", cx);
7293:            window.render_frame(cx);
7294:            window.press("enter", cx);
7295:            window.render_frame(cx);
7296:        })
7297:        .unwrap();
7298:        cx.run_until_parked();
7299:        planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| {
7300:            let steps = planning.story(&names[0]).unwrap().steps();
7301:            let prompts: Vec<&str> = steps.iter().map(|step| step.prompt.as_str()).collect();
7302:            assert_eq!(prompts, ["First step"]);
7303:            assert!(planning.open_step.is_none());
7304:        });
7305:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7306:    }

7308:    fn findings(_: &Path, request: &str, _: &Cancel, _: &dyn Fn(String)) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
7309:        assert!(request.contains("change nothing"));
7310:        Ok("Found the queue in `src/prompt_queue.rs:40`.\n\n```suspense-story\ntitle: Tidy it\nmode: code\ntarget: new\n\nTidy the queue.\n```\n".into())
7311:    }

7313:    /// Research ends in findings saved as markdown and listed, its proposal
7314:    /// waiting, not added; and planning pauses for review: Send sprint does
7315:    /// nothing until it is resumed, and the pause is kept across a reload.
7316:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7317:    async fn research_pauses_planning_until_resumed(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7318:        let (dir, names) = backlog("planning-research-test", &["Queue"]);
7319:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), findings, cx);
7320:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7321:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7322:                planning.move_to(&names[0], Section::Next, cx);
7323:                planning.open_research(None, window, cx);
7324:                let input = planning.drafting.as_ref().unwrap().input.clone();
7325:                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("The queue", window, cx));
7326:                planning.draft(cx);
7327:            });
7328:        })
7329:        .unwrap();
7330:        cx.run_until_parked();
7331:        let file = planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| {
7332:            assert!(planning.is_paused());
7333:            assert_eq!(planning.notes.findings.len(), 1);
7334:            assert_eq!(planning.notes.findings[0].state, super::FindingState::Done);
7335:            // Proposed, never added by itself.
7336:            assert_eq!(planning.proposals.len(), 1);
7337:            assert_eq!(planning.stories(Section::Backlog).len(), 0);
7338:            planning.notes.findings[0].file.clone()
7339:        });
7340:        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(super::research_dir(&dir).join(&file)).unwrap();
7341:        assert!(text.starts_with("# Research: The queue"));
7342:        assert!(text.contains("src/prompt_queue.rs:40"));
7343:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7344:            window.render_frame(cx);
7345:            window.find("planning-paused");
7346:            window.find(SharedString::from(format!("planning-findings-{file}")));
7347:            // Paused, nothing is sent.
7348:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.send_sprint(window, cx));
7349:        })
7350:        .unwrap();
7351:        cx.run_until_parked();
7352:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx), ["Queue"]);
7353:        assert!(titles(&planning, Section::Current, cx).is_empty());
7354:        // Kept across a reload.
7355:        let (_, _, notes) = load(&dir);
7356:        assert_eq!(
7357:            notes.paused.as_ref().map(|pause| pause.research.as_str()),
7358:            Some(file.as_str())
7359:        );
7360:        // Resumed, the sprint can be sent, into Current sprint.
7361:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7362:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7363:                planning.resume_planning(cx);
7364:                planning.send_sprint(window, cx);
7365:            });
7366:        })
7367:        .unwrap();
7368:        cx.run_until_parked();
7369:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Current, cx), ["Queue"]);
7370:        assert!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx).is_empty());
7371:        let (_, _, notes) = load(&dir);
7372:        assert!(notes.paused.is_none());
7373:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7374:    }
7375:
7376:    /// With Start automatically on, Next sprint is sent by itself: at once
7377:    /// while no sprint is current, and once every story of the current one
7378:    /// is Done, which closes it; never after a sprint with a story not done,
7379:    /// saying why, nor while paused. The switch is kept with the plan.
7380:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7381:    async fn the_next_sprint_starts_by_itself(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7382:        use std::cell::RefCell;
7383:        use std::collections::HashMap;
7384:        let (dir, names) = backlog("planning-auto-start-test", &["One", "Two", "Three"]);
7385:        let states: Rc<RefCell<HashMap<String, super::StoryState>>> = Rc::default();
7386:        let sent: Rc<RefCell<Vec<String>>> = Rc::default();
7387:        let mut with = hooks(unused);
7388:        with.state = Rc::new({
7389:            let states = states.clone();
7390:            move |task, _| states.borrow().get(task).map(|state| (*state, None))
7391:        });
7392:        with.send = Rc::new({
7393:            let sent = sent.clone();
7394:            move |stories, _| {
7395:                sent.borrow_mut()
7396:                    .extend(stories.into_iter().map(|story| story.text))
7397:            }
7398:        });
7399:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning_with(dir.clone(), with, cx);
7400:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7401:            planning.move_to(&names[0], Section::Next, cx);
7402:            planning.set_auto_start(true, cx);
7403:        });
7404:        cx.run_until_parked();
7405:        assert_eq!(*sent.borrow(), ["One"]);
7406:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Current, cx), ["One"]);
7407:        // A sprint under way: the next waits for it.
7408:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7409:            planning.move_to(&names[1], Section::Next, cx)
7410:        });
7411:        cx.run_until_parked();
7412:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx), ["Two"]);
7413:        // Done, it closes, and the next is sent.
7414:        let task = |planning: &Entity<Planning>, name: &str, cx: &mut TestAppContext| {
7415:            planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| {
7416:                planning.story(name).unwrap().task.clone().unwrap()
7417:            })
7418:        };
7419:        let one = task(&planning, &names[0], cx);
7420:        states.borrow_mut().insert(one, super::StoryState::Done);
7421:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.auto_advance(cx));
7422:        cx.run_until_parked();
7423:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Done, cx), ["One"]);
7424:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Current, cx), ["Two"]);
7425:        assert_eq!(*sent.borrow(), ["One", "Two"]);
7426:        // Failed, the next waits, saying why.
7427:        let two = task(&planning, &names[1], cx);
7428:        states.borrow_mut().insert(two, super::StoryState::Failed);
7429:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7430:            planning.move_to(&names[2], Section::Next, cx)
7431:        });
7432:        cx.run_until_parked();
7433:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Current, cx), ["Two"]);
7434:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx), ["Three"]);
7435:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7436:            window.render_frame(cx);
7437:            window.find("planning-auto-start-held");
7438:        })
7439:        .unwrap();
7440:        // Kept with the plan.
7441:        let (_, _, notes) = load(&dir);
7442:        assert!(notes.auto_start);
7443:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7444:    }
7445:
7446:    /// Opened with Start automatically on and Next sprint waiting, nothing
7447:    /// starts by itself: the switch's row says "Waiting to start", and Start
7448:    /// now sends it.
7449:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7450:    async fn a_waiting_sprint_never_starts_as_the_plan_opens(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7451:        use std::cell::RefCell;
7452:        let (dir, names) = backlog("planning-auto-start-open-test", &["One"]);
7453:        let (planning, _) = open_planning(dir.clone(), unused, cx);
7454:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7455:            planning.move_to(&names[0], Section::Next, cx);
7456:            // Saved on, as left, without starting it here.
7457:            planning.advancing = true;
7458:            planning.notes.auto_start = true;
7459:            planning.save_order(cx);
7460:            planning.advancing = false;
7461:        });
7462:        cx.run_until_parked();
7463:        let sent: Rc<RefCell<Vec<String>>> = Rc::default();
7464:        let mut with = hooks(unused);
7465:        with.send = Rc::new({
7466:            let sent = sent.clone();
7467:            move |stories, _| {
7468:                sent.borrow_mut()
7469:                    .extend(stories.into_iter().map(|story| story.text))
7470:            }
7471:        });
7472:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning_with(dir.clone(), with, cx);
7473:        cx.run_until_parked();
7474:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.auto_advance(cx));
7475:        cx.run_until_parked();
7476:        assert!(sent.borrow().is_empty());
7477:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Next, cx), ["One"]);
7478:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7479:            window.render_frame(cx);
7480:            window.find("planning-auto-start-waiting");
7481:            window.find("planning-auto-badge");
7482:        })
7483:        .unwrap();
7484:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.start_now(cx));
7485:        cx.run_until_parked();
7486:        assert_eq!(*sent.borrow(), ["One"]);
7487:        assert_eq!(titles(&planning, Section::Current, cx), ["One"]);
7488:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7489:    }

7491:    /// The states and keys of the polish: an unreadable plan names its file
7492:    /// with Try again and saves nothing over it; Findings says it has none;
7493:    /// Ctrl+Shift+D opens Draft stories focused; F6 moves between regions;
7494:    /// and Space toggles Start automatically from the keyboard.
7495:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7496:    async fn states_and_keys_of_the_plan(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7497:        let dir = project("planning-polish-test");
7498:        let order = super::planning_dir(&dir).join("order.json");
7499:        std::fs::create_dir_all(order.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
7500:        std::fs::write(&order, "{ not json").unwrap();
7501:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), unused, cx);
7502:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7503:            window.render_frame(cx);
7504:            window.find("planning-load-error");
7505:            window.find("planning-load-retry");
7506:        })
7507:        .unwrap();
7508:        // Nothing is saved over a plan that couldn't be read.
7509:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7510:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.new_story(window, cx));
7511:        })
7512:        .unwrap();
7513:        cx.run_until_parked();
7514:        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&order).unwrap(), "{ not json");
7515:        // Read again once it is fixed, what was added meanwhile kept.
7516:        std::fs::write(&order, "{}").unwrap();
7517:        planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.read_again(cx));
7518:        cx.run_until_parked();
7519:        let press = |key: &str, cx: &mut TestAppContext| {
7520:            cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7521:                window.render_frame(cx);
7522:                window.press(key, cx);
7523:                window.render_frame(cx);
7524:            })
7525:            .unwrap();
7526:            cx.run_until_parked();
7527:        };
7528:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7529:            window.render_frame(cx);
7530:            window.find("planning-empty-findings");
7531:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| {
7532:                planning.close_story(window, cx);
7533:                planning.focus.focus(window, cx);
7534:            });
7535:        })
7536:        .unwrap();
7537:        press("ctrl-shift-d", cx);
7538:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7539:            let drafting = planning
7540:                .read(cx)
7541:                .drafting
7542:                .as_ref()
7543:                .expect("Draft stories didn't open");
7544:            assert!(!drafting.research);
7545:            assert!(drafting.input.read(cx).focus_handle(cx).is_focused(window));
7546:        })
7547:        .unwrap();
7548:        // F6 moves on from the panel to the first region, and on again.
7549:        press("f6", cx);
7550:        let moved = cx
7551:            .update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7552:                planning.read(cx).focused_row(window)
7553:            })
7554:            .unwrap();
7555:        assert!(
7556:            moved.is_some()
7557:                || cx
7558:                    .update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7559:                        planning.read(cx).section_focus["next"].is_focused(window)
7560:                    })
7561:                    .unwrap()
7562:        );
7563:        // Space toggles the switch focused.
7564:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7565:            planning.update(cx, |planning, cx| planning.switch_focus.focus(window, cx));
7566:        })
7567:        .unwrap();
7568:        press("space", cx);
7569:        assert!(planning.read_with(cx, |planning, _| planning.notes.auto_start));
7570:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7571:    }

7574:        anyhow::bail!("the harness isn't signed in")

7578:    #[gpui_kit::test]
7579:    async fn a_failed_draft_says_why_and_keeps_the_request(cx: &mut TestAppContext) {
7580:        let dir = project("planning-draft-error-test");
7581:        let (planning, handle) = open_planning(dir.clone(), failing, cx);

7585:                let input = planning.drafting.as_ref().unwrap().input.clone();
7586:                input.update(cx, |input, cx| input.set_value("Something", window, cx));
7587:                planning.draft(cx);
7588:            });
7589:        })
7590:        .unwrap();
7591:        cx.run_until_parked();
7592:        cx.update_window(handle, |_, window, cx| {
7593:            window.render_frame(cx);

7595:            window.find("planning-draft-retry");
7596:            let drafting = planning.read(cx).drafting.as_ref().unwrap();
7597:            assert_eq!(drafting.input.read(cx).value().as_ref(), "Something");
7598:            assert!(drafting.error.as_deref().unwrap().contains("signed in"));
7599:        })
7600:        .unwrap();
7601:        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
7602:    }
